# EffectSlot inventory

Step 1 of [effect-slot-refactor.md](effect-slot-refactor.md): one row per `EffectSlot` constant. Generated by `scripts/effect-slot-inventory.py` at `7b169f25de`; do not edit it by hand. The kind, step, event, object, relation, zone, multiplicity, self, grouping and notes columns come from `scripts/effect-slot-classification.tsv`, which is kept by hand and was checked against the collectors. Everything else is read from the sources on every run.

Re-run `python3 scripts/effect-slot-inventory.py` after a step moves slots (add `--results` after a full card-test run to refresh the baseline), and update the TSV when a slot changes behaviour. `--gradle-args <step>` prints the Gradle arguments that run the card test classes of a step's cards, one `--tests` filter per class after each module's test task.

## Columns

- **Kind**: spell, static, activated, mana, replacement, special action, marker or triggered (CR 113.3). A slot that mixes kinds is listed under its main kind; its notes name the rest.
- **Step**: the refactor step that moves the constant. 13.x is row x of step 13's table.
- **Event**, **Object**, **Relation**: what happens, the type filter on the object it happens to, and who that object or player is relative to the source and its controller, as the code checks them.
- **Zone**: where the source has to be for the ability to work.
- **Mult.**: how often one event triggers it: per object, per event (one or more), per counter, ….
- **Self**: whether the source's own event triggers it: only, yes, no, heuristic, or n/a.
- **Grouping**: how the readers turn several registrations of the slot on one card into stack objects. *each*: one stack object per registration. *all*: one stack object for all of them. *all-but-independent*: one shared object, plus one for each `TriggerMode.INDEPENDENT` registration. *split*: partitioned some other way (see notes). *none*: never put on the stack. *varies*: the readers disagree (see notes).
- **Cards**: card classes in `magical-vibes-card` that name the constant.

The details under each table give, per constant:

- **Readers**: every method in engine, domain, AI, networking and engine test-fixture main code that names the constant (`EffectSlot.X`, a `@CollectsTrigger` slot, a `case` of a `switch` on a slot, or a `slot.name()` prefix test), by class, with a guess from the method body: *each* (dispatches each registration through the registry or queues `List.of(effect)`), *all* (queues the whole list), *mode* (reads `TriggerMode.INDEPENDENT`), *mixed* (both), *read* (does not queue), *queue?* (queues, shape unclear). The Grouping column is the reviewed answer. A handler gets one registration at a time from `TriggerCollectorRegistry`, so it is listed with the effect class it is registered for. Methods that walk `EffectSlot.values()` are listed once, under [Readers of every slot](#readers-of-every-slot).
- **Wrappers**: trigger-level wrapper effects (`May…`, `…Conditional…`, `Once…`, `SequenceEffect`, `ChooseOne…`) in the card registrations, with counts. **Trigger effects**: `…TriggerEffect` classes. **Aware**: `…AwareEffect` interfaces that the slot's readers bind, with the number of registrations whose effects implement them. Registrations built in helper methods are not resolved.
- **Modes**: registrations with a `TriggerMode` other than `NORMAL`, and `addSimultaneousEffects` calls.
- **Zone-change choke points** (steps 10 to 12): the callers of each reader method.

- **`ON_ANY_CARD_EXILED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyCardExiledTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`; `GameData`: `notifyCardsExiled` (read). Notes: Step 8 moves the Bell Borca static out; what is left is a step 12 trigger. SURPRISE: mixed: The War Doctor's PutCountersOnSelfEffect is a real trigger (TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyCardExiledTriggers → dispatchSlot → MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleGlobalPhaseOutOrCardExileTriggers, direct stack.add), while Bell Borca's NoteManaValueOfExiledCardsEffect is a no-stack static read by GameData#notifyCardsExiled (which does not check ability loss). The plan's step 8 calls the slot a marker; only the Bell Borca half is.
- **`MAY_SKIP_DRAW_STEP_DRAW`**. Readers: `DrawService`: `findMaySkipDrawSource` (read); `StepTriggerService`: `findMaySkipDrawStepDrawSources` (read); `GainActivatedAndTriggeredAbilitiesOfExiledCardsSelfEffectHandler (field)`: `(field)` (read); `GainAbilitiesOfLastChosenExiledCardSelfEffectHandler (field)`: `(field)` (read); `CreateTokenWithTriggeredAbilitiesOfExiledCardsEffectHandler`: `isTriggeredAbilitySlot` (read, switch). Notes: Detected by effect class (IslandSanctuaryEffect, FastingEffect) in DrawService#findMaySkipDrawSource and StepTriggerService#findMaySkipDrawStepDrawSources; the same check reads MaySkipDrawReplacementEffect from STATIC, so the replacement already lives in two slots.
- **`GRAVEYARD_DRAW_REPLACEMENT`**. Readers: `DredgeSupport`: `dredgeEffect` (read); `GainActivatedAndTriggeredAbilitiesOfExiledCardsSelfEffectHandler (field)`: `(field)` (read); `GainAbilitiesOfLastChosenExiledCardSelfEffectHandler (field)`: `(field)` (read). Notes: Dredge (DredgeEffect, 14 cards), read by DredgeSupport#dredgeEffect.
- **`ON_OPENING_HAND_REVEAL`**. Readers: `MulliganService`: `startGame` (read); `StepTriggerService`: `handleOpeningHandTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 29. Trigger effects: `RegisterOpeningHandUpkeepTriggerEffect` 5, `RegisterDelayedCounterTriggerEffect` 1, `RegisterDelayedManaTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: Opening-hand actions (CR 103.6), not a triggered ability. SURPRISE: not a triggered ability - every one of the 29 registrations is MayEffect(PregameChoiceEffect) (18 LeylineStartOnBattlefieldEffect, Gemstone Caverns, 5 RegisterOpeningHandUpkeepTriggerEffect, RegisterDelayedCounter/ManaTriggerEffect, ChooseLegacyWordEffect, BecomeStartingPlayerEffect, ExileSourceCardFromOpeningHand...). MulliganService#startGame adds one PendingMayAbility per registration (bypasses stack; onlyForNonStartingPlayer honoured; scans library for ChooseLegacyWordEffect). Chancellor-style choices register delayed triggers (openingHandUpkeepTriggers etc.) fired later by StepTriggerService#handleOpeningHandUpkeepTriggers. The second reader StepTriggerService#handleOpeningHandTriggers (turn-1 upkeep: MayEffect -> queueMayAbility, else stack.add) skips PregameChoiceEffect, so it is dead for all current cards. mixed: pregame static-like choices (Leylines) + delayed-trigger registration (Chancellors).
- **`MULLIGAN_ACTION`**. Readers: `MulliganService`: `queueMulliganActionChoice` (read), `holdsSerumPowder` (read); `GainActivatedAndTriggeredAbilitiesOfExiledCardsSelfEffectHandler (field)`: `(field)` (read); `GainAbilitiesOfLastChosenExiledCardSelfEffectHandler (field)`: `(field)` (read); `CreateTokenWithTriggeredAbilitiesOfExiledCardsEffectHandler`: `isTriggeredAbilitySlot` (read, switch). Notes: Serum Powder and No Regrets Egret; read by MulliganService#queueMulliganActionChoice and #holdsSerumPowder.
- **`GRANT_STORM_TO_INSTANT_OR_SORCERY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each). Notes: Static ability granting storm. TriggerCollectionService#checkSpellCastTriggers puts the granted storm trigger straight on the stack (stack.add) once per granting permanent.
- **`GRANT_CASCADE_TO_FIRST_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each); `GainActivatedAndTriggeredAbilitiesOfExiledCardsSelfEffectHandler (field)`: `(field)` (read); `GainAbilitiesOfLastChosenExiledCardSelfEffectHandler (field)`: `(field)` (read). Notes: Static ability granting cascade (Maelstrom Nexus). checkSpellCastTriggers puts one cascade trigger per granting permanent straight on the stack, sourced from the spell; all registrations are one stack object.
- **`GRANT_CASCADE_TO_FIRST_SPELL_USING_TREASURE_MANA`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each). Notes: As GRANT_CASCADE_TO_FIRST_SPELL (Rain of Riches).
- **`GRANT_CASCADE_TO_INSTANT_OR_SORCERY_FROM_HAND`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each). Notes: As GRANT_CASCADE_TO_FIRST_SPELL (Quandrix, the Proof).
- **`GRANT_CASCADE_TO_ENCHANTMENT_FROM_HAND`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each). Notes: As GRANT_CASCADE_TO_FIRST_SPELL (Wildsear, Scouring Maw).
- **`GRANT_CASCADE_TO_SLIVER_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each). Notes: As GRANT_CASCADE_TO_FIRST_SPELL (The First Sliver).
- **`GRANT_DEMONSTRATE_TO_FIRST_SPELL_FROM_OUTSIDE_HAND`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Notes: Static ability granting demonstrate (The Twelfth Doctor). Holds a MayEffect; the trigger is sourced from the permanent, not the spell.
- **`GRANT_CASCADE_TO_HAND_SPELLS_WITH_MANA_VALUE_AT_MOST_OPPONENTS_LIFE_LOST`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each). Notes: As GRANT_CASCADE_TO_FIRST_SPELL (Abaddon the Despoiler).

- **`ON_ENCHANTED_PLAYER_NONTOKEN_CREATURE_DIES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkEnchantedPlayerNontokenCreatureDeathTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Aware: `DyingCreatureCardAwareEffect` 2. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkEnchantedPlayerNontokenCreatureDeathTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkEnchantedPlayerNontokenCreatureDeathTriggers -> dispatchSlot (ability-loss checked) -> DeathTriggerCollectorService handleEnchantedPlayerNontokenCreatureDeathDefault / ExileTriggeringCreatureFromGraveyardEffect handler. Source cannot be the dying creature. Choke: PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers (wasCreature branch), batched inside performSimultaneousRemovals.
- **`ON_HAUNTED_CREATURE_DIES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkHauntedCreatureDeathTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkHauntedCreatureDeathTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkHauntedCreatureDeathTriggers (from PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers when the permanent went to a graveyard): all non-modal effects form ONE ability (one DeathTriggerTarget if any effect targets, else one stack entry); each TriggeredModalEffect is its own TriggeredModalTrigger. Controlled by the haunting card's owner. Not registry-dispatched; no doublers.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DIES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `addLookBackConditionalGrantedDeathEffects` (read), `checkAllyCreatureDeathTriggers` (each), `collectTemporaryGlobalAllyCreatureDeathTriggers` (queue?), `collectBatchedAllyCreatureDeathTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `MayEffect`, `MayPayLifeEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`; `SpellEvaluator`: `deathTriggerWithSacOutletBonus` (read), `tokenMakerWithDeathTriggersBonus` (read). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 60, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 43, `SequenceEffect` 16, `MayEffect` 7, `ConditionalEffect` 6, `MayPayLifeEffect` 2, `ConditionalReplacementEffect` 1, `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 6, `OneOrMoreCreatureDeathTriggerEffect` 2. Aware: `DyingCreatureCardAwareEffect` 34, `DyingCreatureCountersAwareEffect` 28, `DyingCreatureCounterAwareEffect` 1. 10 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#addLookBackConditionalGrantedDeathEffects` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreatureDeathTriggers`; `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreatureDeathTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`; `TriggerCollectionService#collectTemporaryGlobalAllyCreatureDeathTriggers` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreatureDeathTriggers`; `TriggerCollectionService#collectBatchedAllyCreatureDeathTriggers` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkBatchedAllyCreatureDeathTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreatureDeathTriggers. Self: the dying permanent is skipped iff its printed card has type CREATURE; a printed noncreature that was animated is explicitly added as a watcher and sees its own death. SURPRISE: the batched path TriggerCollectionService#collectBatchedAllyCreatureDeathTriggers always excludes the watcher's own death, so an animated noncreature behaves differently in single vs batched deaths. Grouping: targeted effects -> own DeathTriggerTarget each; MayEffect/MayPayManaEffect/MayPayLifeEffect -> dispatch (handleAllyCreatureMay/MayPay) each; TriggeringCard/TriggeringPermanent-conditional registrations -> own stack entry each; all remaining plain registrations share ONE stack entry (no CardEffect default handler for this slot). Also temporary global watchers (collectTemporaryGlobalAllyCreatureDeathTriggers) and Relic Vial look-back grants. Choke: PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers; batch from performSimultaneousRemovals/DepartureService -> checkBatchedAllyCreatureDeathTriggers.
- **`ON_ALLY_ARTIFACT_OR_CREATURE_DIES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` (each), `collectBatchedAllyArtifactOrCreatureDeathTrigger` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `OneOrMoreArtifactOrCreatureDeathTriggerEffect`. Trigger effects: `OneOrMoreArtifactOrCreatureDeathTriggerEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#enforceAttachmentLegality`, `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`, `PermanentRemovalService#removeOrphanedAuras`; `TriggerCollectionService#collectBatchedAllyArtifactOrCreatureDeathTrigger` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkBatchedAllyArtifactOrCreatureDeathTriggers`. Notes: Single death: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers (skipped when the permanent is in simultaneousDyingPermanents) dispatches for every permanent of the dying controller plus the dying permanent itself; batch: TriggerCollectionService#checkBatchedAllyArtifactOrCreatureDeathTriggers -> collectBatchedAllyArtifactOrCreatureDeathTrigger (once per watcher, first matching death, eventContainsCreature flag; dying watchers included). SURPRISE: only OneOrMoreArtifactOrCreatureDeathTriggerEffect has a handler (no CardEffect default), so any other effect class registered here never triggers. Type check uses printed+granted types, not layer-effective types. Choke: PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers / removeOrphanedAuras; performSimultaneousRemovals, DepartureService.
- **`ON_CREATURE_PUT_INTO_CONTROLLER_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCreaturePutIntoOwnersGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` (each), `dispatchSlotEffect` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 7 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `?`, `CreateTokenEffect`, `MayEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `RegisterDelayedReturnDyingCreatureUnderControlEffect`, `ReturnDyingCreatureToOwnerHandUnlessTargetPaysLifeEffect`, `ReturnTriggeringCardToOwnerHandEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 4, `MayEffect` 3, `MayPayManaEffect` 1, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 1. Aware: `DyingCreatureCardAwareEffect` 7. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkCreaturePutIntoOwnersGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`; `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlotEffect` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscardTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#collectBatchedOncePerTurnAllyNontokenDeathTrigger`, `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchDepartedManaSourceSpellCastTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlot`. Notes: Borderline 11/12: it is a creature death but owner-keyed and coded with the permanent-to-graveyard family (AnyPermanentGraveyard context). TriggerCollectionService#checkCreaturePutIntoOwnersGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers from PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers (wasCreature branch); dispatchSlot over the owner's battlefield + simultaneousDyingPermanents of the owner + the dying creature itself (look-back). TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlotEffect special-cases this slot: TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect with PermanentPowerAtMostPredicate/PermanentToughnessAtLeastPredicate is judged from LKI power/toughness, everything else via unwrapCreatureDeathConditional, plus intervening-if. No default handler: only 7 effect classes (MayEffect is put on the stack directly, MayPayManaEffect -> queueMayAbility, ReturnDyingCreatureToOwnerHandUnlessTargetPaysLifeEffect -> queueInteraction, others stack/enqueue). The same method also fires GRAVEYARD_ON_CREATURE_PUT_INTO_*_GRAVEYARD slots.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_OR_PLANESWALKER_DIES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyCreatureOrPlaneswalkerDeathTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `MayEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreatureOrPlaneswalkerDeathTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreatureOrPlaneswalkerDeathTriggers: dying permanent is not on the battlefield and is excluded from the simultaneous watcher list by id. MayEffect/MayPayManaEffect/MayPayLifeEffect -> dispatch (handleAllyCreatureOrPlaneswalkerMay) each; all other registrations share ONE stack entry with no target routing (a targeted non-may effect would get no target). Creature-death doublers only when wasCreature. Choke: PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers.
- **`ON_DAMAGED_CREATURE_DIES`**. Readers: `GraveyardService`: `enqueueDamagedCreatureDiesTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 3, `MayEffect` 2, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 2. Aware: `DyingCreatureCardAwareEffect` 6. Choke points: `GraveyardService#enqueueDamagedCreatureDiesTriggers` ← `GraveyardService#triggerDamagedCreatureDiesAbilities`. Notes: GraveyardService#triggerDamagedCreatureDiesAbilities -> enqueueDamagedCreatureDiesTriggers, run from GraveyardService#addCardToGraveyard (updateThisTurnBattlefieldToGraveyardTracking) when a creature card arrives from the battlefield - SURPRISE: collected at graveyard arrival in GraveyardService, not in PermanentRemovalService with the other death slots. Self: not excluded, but a source that dies is only found if it is in simultaneousDyingCreatures (batched death) - a lone self-death never sees itself. Printed + temporary + granted effects; TriggeringPermanentConditional checked; targeted -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget, else direct stack.add (MayEffect stays inside the entry). Equipment variant: Equipment currently attached to the damaging creature (its own controller is 'you').

Uses unwrapCreatureDeathConditional (TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect supported) then #queueGraveyardCreatureDeathTrigger: MayPayManaEffect/MayEffect -> queueMayAbility, else stack.add with triggeringCardId and targetId=dying controller for LoseLifeEffect(DYING_CREATURE_CONTROLLER). SURPRISE: batched registrations are skipped whenever any creature of the graveyard owner died in the same batch (collector switches to the ALLY slot). SourceCardInGraveyard conditions are not version-bound here.

Notes: mixed: triggered + replacement + marker. Main check EtbTriggerService#processCreatureETBEffects (processLandETBEffects first runs land as-enters choices; processFaceDownCreatureETBTriggers skips own ETB), queueing via EtbTriggerService#queueMandatoryETBEffects, gating via EtbEffectResolver#resolve (ChooseOneEffect unwrapped to cast-time etbMode, Kicked/CastFromZone/Prowl/Madness/Gift/Tribute/evoke etc. intervening-ifs unwrap or drop, gate conditions such as Metalcraft/Morbid stay wrapped, LoseGameIfNotCastFromHandEffect/ReturnSelfToHandIfDashCostPaidEffect/blitz riders dropped). Replacement-style classes read in place and filtered out of the trigger list: every ReplacementEffect implementer (isEntryReplacementEffect, also inside ConditionalEffect/ConditionalReplacementEffect) - in this slot mainly EnterWithCountersEffect, CopyPermanentOnEnterEffect, DevourEffect, AmplifyEffect, ChooseOpponentOnEnterEffect, ChoosePlayerOnEnterEffect, ChooseTwoPlayersOnEnterEffect, ChooseCardNameOnEnterEffect, ChooseNonlandPermanentOnEnterEffect, ChooseManaValueParityOnEnterEffect, ChoosePowerToughnessFormEffect, BecomeDayAsEntersEffect, PayAnyAmountOfLifeOnEnterEffect, MillAsEntersEffect, DiscardHandAsEntersEffect, DiscardCreatureCardsAsEntersForKeywordCountersEffect, SacrificeAllPermanentsAsEntersEffect, LoseLifeEqualToLifeTotalAsEntersEffect, FlipCoinOnEnterEffect, NoteControllerLifeTotalEffect, CreateEmblemAsEntersEffect, WerewhatOnEnterEffect, UpgradeEffect, CopyCreatureCardInExileOnEnterEffect, CopyCreatureCardFromGraveyardOnEnterEffect, MimeoplasmCopyOnEnterEffect, EnterWithKeywordEffect, EnterWithTokenAttachedEffect, EntersTappedEffect, ExileIfLeavesBattlefieldEffect, PutPhylacteryCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect, RollDiceAndEnterWithCountersEffect, Sacrifice*OnEnter*/RemoveAllCounters*ThenEnterWithCountersEffect; plus non-ReplacementEffect as-enters choices filtered by class: ChooseColorEffect, ChooseCardTypeOnEnterEffect, ChooseBasicLandTypeOnEnterEffect, ChooseCreatureOnEnterEffect, ChooseEquipmentAttachmentOnEnterEffect, ChoosePrimalClayFormOnEnterEffect, SubtypeChoiceOnEnterEffect, ChooseRandomNumberOnEnterEffect, ChooseModeOnEnterEffect, ChooseIndependentModesOnEnterEffect, DraftProteanWarEngineSpellbookEffect, DraftCardFromSpellbookToExileEffect (lands only - on a nonland it stays a trigger), BecomePreparedEffect(asTriggeredAbility=false). They are applied by BattlefieldPlacementService#applyEntryReplacementEffects (EntryReplacementHandlerRegistry, reads ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD + STATIC) / applyEnterWithCounters / applyEnterWithKeywords / applyEnterWithAttachedTokens, CloneService, AuraCopyService, AsEntersInteractionService, StackResolutionService#beginChoose*OnEnter, BattlefieldEntryService#begin*CardNameChoice. Grouping: all plain mandatory registrations share ONE stack object (and one target choice through the card's target filter/groups); each MayEffect is queued separately (graveyard-target, Soulbond, Puca's Mischief and no-legal-target special cases); each ChooseOneAtTriggerTimeEffect (modal ETB of a non-cast permanent, or of a cast one with no declared mode) is its own TriggeredModalTrigger; each TriggerMode.INDEPENDENT registration and every granted/temporary/persistent/perpetual ETB is its own object; SacrificeSelfIfEvokedEffect is its own object; OncePerTurnTriggerEffect is unwrapped. Inside the shared group, graveyard-targeting effect kinds (ExileGraveyardCardsEffect, CastTargetInstantOrSorceryFromGraveyardEffect, ReturnTargetCards*, etc.) and spell-targeting effects are split off into their own target-choice triggers. SURPRISE: if the mandatory group contains an ExileCardsFromGraveyardEffect, otherEffects becomes empty and the whole mandatory list rides along with the graveyard-exile targeting flow. SURPRISE (suspected rules issue): a cast permanent's modal ChooseOneEffect ETB uses the mode declared at cast time (etbMode) rather than choosing when the trigger is put on the stack. SURPRISE: no single choke point - ~140 call sites call processCreatureETBEffects directly plus ~156 handleCreatureEnteredBattlefield calls. Also read as a presence marker elsewhere (StepTriggerService/TurnCleanupService look for RememberTargetPlayerEffect/ChooseOpponentOnEnterEffect; PermanentRemovalService#handleExileReturnOnLeave; AI/target services).
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyCreatureEntersTriggers` (each); `EnterTriggerCollectorService`: 31 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `AttachSourceEquipmentToEnteringCreatureEffect`, `BecomeCopyOfEnteringCreatureUntilEndOfTurnEffect`, `BecomeSaddledUntilEndOfTurnEffect`, `BoostEnteringCreatureEffect`, `CardEffect`, `ChampionOfTheHareishTriggerEffect`, `ChooseModeNotYetChosenEffect`, `ChooseModeNotYetChosenThisTurnEffect`, `ChooseOneAtTriggerTimeEffect`, `CreateTokenCopyOfTargetPermanentEffect`, `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `DealDamageToTargetCreatureThenBoostSourceIfDamagedEffect`, `EnteringCreaturePowerBranchEffect`, `EnteringCreatureSourcePowerBranchEffect`, `EvolveTriggerEffect`, `ExileGraveyardCardsEffect`, `ExploreEffect`, `GainLifeEqualToToughnessEffect`, `IntensifySourceByEnteringPowerEffect`, `LookAtTopCardsEqualToEnteringPowerPutOneOnTopRestOnBottomEffect`, `MayEffect`, `MayPayLifeAndPutCountersOnEnteringCreatureEqualToPowerEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `MoveChosenCounterFromSourceToEnteringCreatureEffect`, `PerpetuallyGainKeywordsOfTriggeringCreatureEffect`, `PutCountersOnEnteringCreatureEffect`, `ReturnTargetCardsFromGraveyardToHandEffect`, `SoulbondPairWithEnteringEffect`, `TargetOpponentLosesLifeEqualToPowerToughnessDifferenceEffect`, `TransformEnteringCreatureEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 101, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 27, `SequenceEffect` 23, `MayEffect` 21, `ConditionalEffect` 11, `EnteringCreatureMaxPowerConditionalEffect` 8, `EnteringCreatureMinPowerConditionalEffect` 8, `MayPayManaEffect` 6, +1 more. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 9, `EvolveTriggerEffect` 4, `ChampionOfTheHareishTriggerEffect` 1. 4 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreatureEntersTriggers` ← `EtbTriggerService#collectEntersTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreatureEntersTriggers; self skipped by perm.getCard()==enteringCreature; per registration: TriggeringCard/TriggeringPermanent/EnterCreature conditionals, once-per-turn, intervening-if, then dispatch to EnterTriggerCollectorService (default handleEnterDefault, handleEnterMay -> queueMayAbility, handleEnterMayPay); targeted effects go through EntersTriggerTarget/ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger. Also fires turn-scoped TemporaryGlobalTriggeredAbility watchers keyed to this slot and allyCreatureEntersTriggerWatchers. Source skipped only for isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn/face-down (layer ability loss not checked for printed effects). choke point: EtbTriggerService#collectEntersTriggers (run at the end of EtbTriggerService#processCreatureETBEffects / processFaceDownCreatureETBTriggers, which are reached from ~300 call sites via BattlefieldEntryService#processCreatureETBEffects/processLandETBEffects and AsEntersInteractionService#handleCreatureEnteredBattlefield).
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURES_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyCreatureEntersTriggers` (each), `checkAllyTokenEntersTriggers` (each); `EnterTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `CreateTokenCopyOfTargetPermanentEffect`. Wrappers: `EnteringCreatureFromGraveyardConditionalEffect` 2, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 2, `ConditionalEffect` 1, `MayEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 2. SURPRISE: fires whoever caused the exile (e.g. an opponent's reveal-and-exile in CardChoiceHandlerService#handleRevealCardsDiscardChosen) although Hero of Bretagard says 'you exile'; that caller also passes count 1 even when several cards were exiled. Hero/Ranar register the same effect here and in ON_CONTROLLER_SPELL_OR_ABILITY_EXILES_PERMANENT, so one event exiling from both zones gives two triggers.

SURPRISE (suspected rules bug): Ranar's 'exiles one or more permanents' is one trigger per event, but the collector runs once per exiled permanent, so a mass exile makes several Spirits (Hero's counter total is still right). No look-back for watchers exiled in the same event.

Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerCardPutIntoHandFromLibraryTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 2. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerCardPutIntoHandFromLibraryTriggers` ← `DrawService#completeDrawCard`, `LibraryChoiceHandlerService#addCardFromLibraryToHand`, `LibrarySearchSupport#revealDeferredEachPlayerToHandPicks`, `PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryCardChoiceInteractionHandler#swapWithLibraryTop`, `SeekFromTopOfLibraryEffectHandler#resolve`, `SeekLibraryAndPerpetuallyBoostSoughtCardEffectHandler#resolve`, `SeekLibraryAndPerpetuallyReduceSoughtCardEffectHandler#resolve`, `SeekLibraryEffectHandler#resolve`, `SeekLibraryForOwnerOfCardExiledWithSourceBelowManaValueEffectHandler#resolve`, `SeekLibraryToHandAndRegisterDiscardAtNextEndStepEffectHandler#resolve`, `SeekLibraryToHandAndRegisterExileAtNextEndStepEffectHandler#resolve`, `SeekLibraryToHandEffectHandler#resolve`, +1 more. Notes: A library-to-hand zone change; it could also sit in 13.7 because draws fire it. TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerCardPutIntoHandFromLibraryTriggers via dispatchSlot. Choke points: DrawService#completeDrawCard, LibrarySearchSupport#revealDeferredEachPlayerToHandPicks, LibraryChoiceHandlerService#addCardFromLibraryToHand, the Seek*EffectHandler family (SeekLibraryEffectHandler#resolve etc.) and PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryCardChoiceInteractionHandler#swapWithLibraryTop. There is no single choke point, so other library-to-hand moves may miss it. MiscTriggerCollectorService: TriggeringCardConditionalEffect (card predicate) and default; enqueueTrigger with triggeringCardId and a frozen card snapshot.
- **`ON_ALLY_PERMANENT_SACRIFICED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 6 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `ConditionalTriggeringPermanentEffect`, `MayEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 71, `SequenceEffect` 9, `MayEffect` 6, `ConditionalEffect` 5, `MayPayManaEffect` 3, `ConditionalTriggeringPermanentEffect` 1, `MayPayLifeEffect` 1, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 6. Aware: `SacrificedPermanentManaValueAwareEffect` 7, `SacrificedPermanentCardAwareEffect` 6. 2 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers` ← `AbilityActivationService#sacrificeChosenCostPermanents`, `AbilityActivationService#sacrificePermanent`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#doResolveManaAbility`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#sacrificeCostPermanents`, `BattlefieldPlacementService#applySacrificeOtherPermanentsWithSameName`, `DepartureService#stepHooks`, `DestructionSupport#logSacrifice`, `GraveyardService#performSacrificeAuraAndGrantIndestructibleReplacement`, `LifeSupport#replaceLifeLossWithShield`, `MakeAnExampleEffectHandler#completePileSeparationStep2`, `SacrificeEnchantedPermanentAndReattachSourceAuraEffectHandler#resolve`, `SpellCastingService#sacrificeForSpellCost`, +1 more. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers is the sacrifice choke point (about 14 callers: DestructionSupport#logSacrifice, DepartureService#stepHooks, StateBasedActionService#depart, AbilityActivationService#sacrificePermanent, SpellCastingService#sacrificeForSpellCost, GraveyardService, LifeSupport, ...) and also fans out to the OPPONENT/ANY/NONTOKEN sacrifice slots. Watchers: sacrificer's battlefield + simultaneousDyingPermanents + the sacrificed permanent's own snapshot (look-back) unless its ON_DEATH has an onlyTriggersOnSacrifice effect (the heuristic). OncePerTurnTriggerEffect unwrapped in the check; non-TriggeringPermanentConditional effects go through unwrapCreatureDeathConditional. Handlers: MayPayManaEffect/MayEffect -> queueMayAbility; targeted -> queueInteraction; TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect with graveyard-targeting return -> SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger; else enqueueTrigger. Granted triggers are not read.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_PERMANENT_SACRIFICED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentPermanentSacrificedTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 4. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentPermanentSacrificedTriggers` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentPermanentSacrificedTriggers, only from #checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers. Watchers are current battlefields only (no look-back for watchers sacrificed in the same event). Full lost-ability checks. Handlers (MiscTriggerCollectorService): TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect (evaluated on new Permanent(sacrificedCard), so only types survive as LKI, not counters/P-T) and default; both enqueueTrigger with non-targeting targetId = sacrificing player. No may or targeting path.
- **`ON_ANY_PERMANENT_SACRIFICED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyPermanentSacrificedTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentSacrificedTriggers` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentSacrificedTriggers, only from #checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers. Watchers: all battlefields (hasLostAllAbilities check), the sacrificed card's own effects (look-back, Carmen) and simultaneousDyingPermanents. Self exclusion is a text heuristic: watcherExcludesSacrificedSelf checks whether the card text contains 'sacrifices another' (Mazirek). Single default handler (MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleAnyPermanentSacrificedDefault): targeted -> queueInteraction, else enqueueTrigger, controller = watcher's controller.
- **`ON_ANY_NONCREATURE_ARTIFACT_SACRIFICED_OR_DESTROYED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyNoncreatureArtifactSacrificedOrDestroyedTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyNoncreatureArtifactSacrificedOrDestroyedTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#destroyPermanentToGraveyard`, `PermanentRemovalService#sacrificePermanentToGraveyard`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyNoncreatureArtifactSacrificedOrDestroyedTriggers, called only from PermanentRemovalService#sacrificePermanentToGraveyard and #destroyPermanentToGraveyard after removal (fires even if a replacement sent it elsewhere; sacrifice paths not routed through sacrificePermanentToGraveyard do not fire it). dispatchSlot over all battlefields + simultaneousDyingPermanents look-back. SURPRISE: the only handler is for DealDamageToPlayersEffect (DeathTriggerCollectorService#handleNoncreatureArtifactSacrificedOrDestroyedDamage), so any other effect class registered here never triggers.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_NONTOKEN_PERMANENT_SACRIFICED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentNontokenPermanentSacrificedTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `MayEffect`, `StealDyingOpponentPermanentUnlessPaysLifeEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentNontokenPermanentSacrificedTriggers` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentNontokenPermanentSacrificedTriggers, only from #checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers. Raw card.getEffects with NO lost-ability/face-down checks at all (the sibling OPPONENT slot has them), no look-back. Handlers: MayEffect -> queueMayAbility (target = sacrificed card id), StealDyingOpponentPermanentUnlessPaysLifeEffect baked and enqueued, default enqueueTrigger with triggeringCardId + graveyard/exile entry versions.
- **`ON_ANY_CREATURE_SACRIFICED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyCreatureSacrificedTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 4 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `MayEffect`, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect`, `PutCountersOnSourceEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 2. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyCreatureSacrificedTriggers` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyCreatureSacrificedTriggers, only from #checkAllyPermanentSacrificedTriggers (Javadoc still names DestructionSupport.sacrificeAndLog as a second caller; it now funnels through the first). dispatchSlot over all battlefields + simultaneousDyingPermanents + the departed snapshot of the sacrificed creature (look-back); self excluded by the 'sacrifices another' card-text heuristic (Zodiark). Handlers: MayEffect -> queueMayAbility, PutCountersOnSourceEffect/default -> enqueueTrigger. SURPRISE: the OncePerTurnTriggerEffect handler is dead code (dispatchSlotEffect unwraps OncePerTurnTriggerEffect first), so a once-per-turn targeted effect would hit the default handler, which has no targeting path.
- **`ON_ANY_ARTIFACT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyArtifactPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 5 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `MayEffect`, `ReturnTriggeringArtifactToOwnerHandUnlessTargetTakesDamageEffect`, `TriggeringArtifactControllerConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringArtifactControllerConditionalEffect` 10, `MayEffect` 4, `MayPayManaEffect` 3, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Aware: `ArtifactGraveyardCountersAwareEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyArtifactPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyArtifactPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers from PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers (wasArtifact and wentToGraveyard); the same loop fires the two ON_ARTIFACT_PUT_INTO_OPPONENT_* slots and emblem watchers. Watchers: all battlefields + simultaneousDyingPermanents (includes the dying artifact itself, so self yes via look-back). Effect-class-dependent: TriggeringArtifactControllerConditionalEffect = 'you control' (+excludeSelf/onlyIfNontoken/onlyIfNotSacrificed flags); ReturnTriggeringArtifactToOwnerHandUnlessTargetTakesDamageEffect = your nontoken artifact into your graveyard, via queueInteraction; DealDamageToPlayersEffect excludes self (targetId = artifact controller); MayEffect -> queueInteraction if targeted else queueMayAbility; default direct stack.add.
- **`ON_ANY_ARTIFACT_EXILED_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyArtifactExiledFromBattlefieldTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyArtifactExiledFromBattlefieldTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToExile`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyArtifactExiledFromBattlefieldTriggers from PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToExile and #processGraveyardAndTriggers (graveyard move replaced by exile); dispatchSlot over current battlefields only, single default handler (direct stack.add). SURPRISE (rules gap): no simultaneousDyingPermanents look-back, unlike the graveyard twin, so an exiled artifact source (Shard of the Void Dragon) or watchers exiled in the same event do not trigger.
- **`ON_ANY_ENCHANTMENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyEnchantmentPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyEnchantmentPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyEnchantmentPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers from PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers (wasEnchantment); dispatchSlot over all battlefields + simultaneousDyingPermanents (look-back incl. itself, e.g. Femeref Enchantress dying). Single default handler, direct stack.add. Context carries no enchantment card, so per-object filters are impossible.
- **`ON_ALLY_ENCHANTMENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyEnchantmentPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyEnchantmentPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`. Notes: Same method as ON_ANY_ENCHANTMENT (TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyEnchantmentPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers, PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers); keyed on the enchantment's controller, not the graveyard owner. Look-back via simultaneousDyingPermanents includes itself. Single default handler (direct stack.add); context has no card, so no predicate gating is possible.
- **`ON_ARTIFACT_PUT_INTO_OPPONENT_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyArtifactPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` (each), `dispatchSlotEffect` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `MayEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyArtifactPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`; `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlotEffect` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscardTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#collectBatchedOncePerTurnAllyNontokenDeathTrigger`, `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchDepartedManaSourceSpellCastTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlot`. Notes: Fired in the ON_ANY_ARTIFACT loop (TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyArtifactPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers) for every watcher whose controller differs from the artifact's controller, incl. simultaneous look-back watchers. SURPRISE: name says 'opponent graveyard' but the test is the artifact's controller (the _OWNER_ twin keys on the graveyard owner). dispatchSlotEffect drops ConditionalEffect(SourceClassLevelAtLeast) when unmet (Class level gating). Handlers: MayEffect -> queueMayAbility, DealDamageToPlayersEffect(TRIGGERING_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER) targetId baked, default direct stack.add.
- **`ON_ARTIFACT_PUT_INTO_OPPONENT_OWNER_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyArtifactPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `MayEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyArtifactPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`. Notes: Fired in the ON_ANY_ARTIFACT loop (TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyArtifactPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldTriggers, PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers) for watchers whose controller differs from the graveyard owner, incl. simultaneous look-back watchers. Handlers shared with the controller-keyed twin: MayEffect -> queueMayAbility, default direct stack.add. Only user Viridian Revel.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`, `StealDyingOpponentPermanentUnlessPaysLifeEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 3, `SequenceEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers from PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers. Watchers: all battlefields + simultaneousDyingCreatures only (non-creature watchers that left in the same event are not looked back). No default handler: only PutCountersOnSelfEffect, TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect (judged on the LKI snapshot) and StealDyingOpponentPermanentUnlessPaysLifeEffect (baked; entry has no source permanent id) fire; all direct stack.add.
- **`ON_OTHER_PLAYER_OWNED_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOtherPlayerOwnedPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkOtherPlayerOwnedPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOtherPlayerOwnedPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers from PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers. Watchers: all permanents of non-owners + the dying permanent itself when its controller is not its owner (a stolen source sees its own death) + simultaneousDyingPermanents of non-owners. Single default handler, direct stack.add (Kothophed).
- **`ON_ANY_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `dispatchAnyPermanentDeathTriggersForWatcher` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 4 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `MayEffect`, `ReturnTriggeringCardToOwnerHandEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentControllerConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentControllerConditionalEffect` 8, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 7, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 4, `MayEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 2. Aware: `DyingCreatureCounterAwareEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchAnyPermanentDeathTriggersForWatcher` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers (PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers) -> #dispatchAnyPermanentDeathTriggersForWatcher over all battlefields, the dying permanent and simultaneous watchers. Self excluded unless the (TriggeringCardConditional-unwrapped) effect is TriggeringPermanentControllerConditionalEffect with includesSource; Javadoc's 'never sees its own death' is achieved by this skip, not by absence. Watcher check is only isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn (no layer ability loss, no granted triggers). SURPRISE: the MayEffect handler returns false unless the watcher controls the dying permanent (Yuna's 'you control' encoded by effect class); TriggeringPermanentControllerConditionalEffect handler also = you control. Default: direct stack.add, targetId = graveyard owner.
- **`ON_ANY_NONTOKEN_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#enforceAttachmentLegality`, `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`, `PermanentRemovalService#removeOrphanedAuras`. Notes: Inside TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers (PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers): dispatchSlot for all battlefields, the dying permanent itself (look-back) and simultaneousDyingPermanents. Single default handler: direct stack.add with non-targeting targetId = graveyard owner (Liability: 'that player loses 1 life').
- **`ON_ALLY_NONTOKEN_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 4 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `RegisterDelayedReturnCurseAttachedToPlayerEffect`, `ReturnTriggeringCardToOwnerHandEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 3, `MayEffect` 1, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#enforceAttachmentLegality`, `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`, `PermanentRemovalService#removeOrphanedAuras`. Notes: Inside TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers (PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers): owner's battlefield, dying permanent (look-back when controller = owner) and simultaneous watchers. SURPRISE: dispatched straight through the registry from card.getEffects with only TriggeringCardConditionalEffect unwrapping, bypassing dispatchSlot, so no lost-ability check and no granted triggers. Handlers: TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect (LKI permanent), ReturnTriggeringCardToOwnerHandEffect (baked), RegisterDelayedReturnCurseAttachedToPlayerEffect, default direct stack.add.
- **`ON_ALLY_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `dispatchAllyPermanentDeathTriggersForWatcher` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 5 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `RegisterDelayedReturnCurseAttachedToPlayerEffect`, `SacrificePermanentsEffect`, `SacrificePermanentsSharingDyingPermanentTypeEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 6, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 3, `MayEffect` 2. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchAllyPermanentDeathTriggersForWatcher` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#dispatchAllyPermanentDeathTriggersForWatcher from #checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers (PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers) for watchers of the dying permanent's controller + simultaneous watchers of that controller (dying permanent itself excluded). SURPRISE: Javadoc says it fires for the graveyard owner, but code keys on the dying permanent's CONTROLLER; and the watcher loop has no lost-ability check at all and reads no granted triggers. unwrapCreatureDeathConditional applied. Handlers: Martyr's Bond, SacrificePermanentsEffect (LKI snapshot as source), curse return, TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect, default (declared target -> queueInteraction DeathTriggerTarget, else stack.add).
- **`ON_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_YOUR_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `dispatchPutIntoYourGraveyardForWatcher` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 3. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchPutIntoYourGraveyardForWatcher` ← `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchPutIntoYourGraveyardTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#dispatchPutIntoYourGraveyardTriggers -> #dispatchPutIntoYourGraveyardForWatcher, first step of #checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers (PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers). Watchers: owner's battlefield, the dying permanent (look-back when its controller is the owner) and simultaneous watchers. Only isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn checked (no layer ability loss, no grants). Shares handlers with ON_ALLY_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD (TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect on LKI, default).
- **`ON_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_OPPONENT_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` (each), `dispatchSlotEffect` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `GainLifeEqualToDyingCreatureToughnessEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 2, `ConditionalEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#enforceAttachmentLegality`, `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`, `PermanentRemovalService#removeOrphanedAuras`; `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlotEffect` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscardTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#collectBatchedOncePerTurnAllyNontokenDeathTrigger`, `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchDepartedManaSourceSpellCastTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlot`. Notes: Inside TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentPutIntoGraveyardTriggers (PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers): dispatchSlot for watchers whose controller is not the graveyard owner + simultaneous watchers (self skipped; a stolen source dying into its owner's graveyard does not see itself). dispatchSlotEffect applies unwrapCreatureDeathConditional and intervening-if for this slot. Default handler stack.add with targetId = graveyard owner (Patron of the Nezumi). SURPRISE: Grim Feast's 'creature' filter lives in the GainLifeEqualToDyingCreatureToughnessEffect handler (returns false for noncreatures), i.e. object type decided by effect class.
- **`ON_ALLY_LAND_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_BY_OPPONENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkLandPutIntoGraveyardByOpponentTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `ReturnTriggeringLandFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect`. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkLandPutIntoGraveyardByOpponentTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkLandPutIntoGraveyardByOpponentTriggers from PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers (wasLand, skipped while sacrificingAsSpellCost); cause = currentlyResolvingControllerId. dispatchSlot over the owner's battlefield. Only handler: ReturnTriggeringLandFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect (Sacred Ground), so any other effect class never triggers.
- **`ON_ALLY_NONCREATURE_PERMANENT_DESTROYED_BY_OPPONENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkNoncreaturePermanentDestroyedByOpponentTriggers` (each); `DeathTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `MayEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkNoncreaturePermanentDestroyedByOpponentTriggers` ← `PermanentRemovalService#recordNoncreaturePermanentDestroyedByOpponent`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkNoncreaturePermanentDestroyedByOpponentTriggers via PermanentRemovalService#recordNoncreaturePermanentDestroyedByOpponent in PermanentRemovalService#processGraveyardAndTriggers (destroy paths). Cause = gameData.currentlyResolvingControllerId. Sources = controller's battlefield + simultaneousDyingPermanents + the destroyed permanent. Only MayEffect handler (DeathTriggerCollectorService) -> SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget targeted interaction; no default.
- **`ON_ALLY_LAND_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_ANYWHERE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkLandPutIntoGraveyardFromAnywhereTriggers` (each), `dispatchSlot` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 2. Modes: `ONCE_PER_BATCH` 3. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerCardsExiledDuringTurnTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerCardsExiledDuringTurnTriggers` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkCardsExiledTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerCardsExiledDuringTurnTriggers via checkCardsExiledTriggers (GameData cardsExiledListener from GameData#notifyCardsExiled); dispatchSlot on the active player's battlefield; MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleControllerCardsExiledDuringTurn -> stack.add. Uses the card-exile batch, a third batching mechanism separate from the graveyard-leave and permanent-leave batches. A source exiling itself does not see it (no look-back).
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_CREATURE_CARDS_LEAVE_GRAVEYARD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerCreatureCardsLeaveGraveyardTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 5. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerCreatureCardsLeaveGraveyardTriggers` ← `GraveyardService#endGraveyardLeaveBatch`, `GraveyardService#notifyCreatureCardsLeftGraveyard`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerCreatureCardsLeaveGraveyardTriggers via dispatchSlot; skips a permanent made from one of the leaving cards (isPermanentMadeFrom); cards entering under a creature-ETB-trigger suppressor (Hushbringer) are dropped (withoutSuppressedEntries). Shared handler MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleControllerCreatureCardsLeaveGraveyard (also used by the singular slot) -> direct stack.add, no targeting support. Choke: GraveyardService#notifyCreatureCardsLeftGraveyard / #endGraveyardLeaveBatch.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_CREATURE_CARD_LEAVES_GRAVEYARD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerCreatureCardLeavesGraveyardTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalReplacementEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerCreatureCardLeavesGraveyardTriggers` ← `GraveyardService#endGraveyardLeaveBatch`, `GraveyardService#notifyCreatureCardsLeftGraveyard`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerCreatureCardLeavesGraveyardTriggers is called N times with the same card list (the context has no per-card identity); same isPermanentMadeFrom skip and the same shared stack.add handler, no targeting. Along the Crooked Way's ConditionalReplacementEffect here is an if/else effect, not a replacement. Choke: GraveyardService#notifyCreatureCardsLeftGraveyard / #endGraveyardLeaveBatch.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_ARTIFACT_OR_CREATURE_CARDS_LEAVE_GRAVEYARD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerArtifactOrCreatureCardsLeaveGraveyardTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerArtifactOrCreatureCardsLeaveGraveyardTriggers` ← `GraveyardService#endGraveyardLeaveBatch`, `GraveyardService#notifyArtifactOrCreatureCardsLeftGraveyard`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerArtifactOrCreatureCardsLeaveGraveyardTriggers via dispatchSlot; handler MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleControllerArtifactOrCreatureCardsLeaveGraveyard (TriggeringCardsAware binding, interveningIf ConditionalEffect, targeted -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget, else enqueueTrigger). No isPermanentMadeFrom skip, unlike the creature-cards slot. Choke: GraveyardService#notifyArtifactOrCreatureCardsLeftGraveyard / #endGraveyardLeaveBatch.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_ARTIFACT_CARDS_LEAVE_GRAVEYARD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerArtifactCardsLeaveGraveyardTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerArtifactCardsLeaveGraveyardTriggers` ← `GraveyardService#endGraveyardLeaveBatch`, `GraveyardService#notifyArtifactCardsLeftGraveyard`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerArtifactCardsLeaveGraveyardTriggers via dispatchSlot; the context carries no card list. Handler MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleControllerArtifactCardsLeaveGraveyard (interveningIf check; targeted -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget, else enqueueTrigger). No isPermanentMadeFrom skip. Choke: GraveyardService#notifyArtifactCardsLeftGraveyard / #endGraveyardLeaveBatch.
- **`ON_TRANSFORM_TO_BACK_FACE`**. Readers: `AnimationSupport`: `transformToBackFace` (read), `fireTransformTriggers` (mixed). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 4, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `EmblemCreatureDeathTriggerEffect` 1. Modes: simultaneous 1. 2 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Choke points: `AnimationSupport#transformToBackFace` ← `AnimationSupport#completeTransformAndAttach`, `AnimationSupport#transformToBackFaceForDayNight`, `LifeSupport#applyEnduringAngelReplacement`, `LookAtTopCardMayRevealMatchingTransformHandler#handle`, `LookAtTopCardMayRevealTypeTransformHandler#handle`, `MillAndTransformIfTypesEffectHandler#resolve`, `MultiPermanentChoiceHandlerService#handleTransformAnyNumber`, `TransformAllEffectHandler#resolve`, `TransformChosenPermanentEffectHandler#transform`, `TransformCreatedPermanentEffectHandler#resolve`, `TransformGrantingPermanentEffectHandler#resolve`, `TransformSelfAndAttachToCreatureYouControlEffectHandler#resolve`, +5 more; `AnimationSupport#fireTransformTriggers` ← `AnimationSupport#toggleLudevicCopy`, `AnimationSupport#transformToBackFace`, `AnimationSupport#transformToFrontFace`. Notes: mixed: ApplyLudevicCopyEffect and CreateEmblemEffect ('as it transforms', Super Nova/Sephiroth) resolve immediately without the stack. AnimationSupport#fireTransformTriggers (from transformToBackFace and toggleLudevicCopy) reads the back face's slot: each non-targeting MayEffect -> its own queueMayAbility; the first other effect puts the WHOLE slot list into one stack entry, or one target interaction (DealDamageToTargetAndUpTo split-damage, graveyard-target ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect, other targeted effects via TriggerTargetCollector), then returns. SURPRISE (latent): a card mixing a MayEffect or emblem/Ludevic effect with another effect would get it twice (queued, and again inside the combined entry); no current card does. No ability-loss check, direct stack.add, no copies. Casal, Pathbreaker Owlbear's two registrations correctly share one entry.
- **`ON_TRANSFORM_TO_FRONT_FACE`**. Readers: `AnimationSupport`: `transformToFrontFace` (read). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Choke points: `AnimationSupport#transformToFrontFace` ← `AnimationSupport#transformToFrontFaceForDayNight`, `LookAtTopCardMayRevealTypeTransformHandler#handle`, `MultiPermanentChoiceHandlerService#handleTransformAnyNumber`, `TransformAllEffectHandler#resolve`, `TransformChosenPermanentEffectHandler#transform`, `TransformCreatedPermanentEffectHandler#resolve`, `TransformGrantingPermanentEffectHandler#resolve`, `TransformSelfEffectHandler#resolve`, `TransformSelfThenEffectHandler#resolve`, `TransformSelfWhenChosenPermanentLeavesEffectHandler#resolve`, `TransformTargetPermanentEffectHandler#resolve`, `TransformToFrontFaceEffectHandler#resolve`. Notes: AnimationSupport#fireTransformTriggers (from transformToFrontFace and toggleLudevicCopy) reads the front (original) face's slot. Same split as ON_TRANSFORM_TO_BACK_FACE: non-targeting MayEffects queued separately, then the whole list goes into one stack entry or target interaction. The immediate CreateEmblemEffect special case applies only to the BACK slot, so a front-face emblem would go on the stack. No ability-loss check, direct stack.add, no copies.

`TurnFaceUpCopyService#completeChoice` ← `AnyOpponentMayDrawOrCreateTokenHandler#handle`, `ApplejackToyChoiceInteractionHandler#handleAnswer`, `ArtifactPermanentOrGraveyardCardChoiceInteractionHandler#handleAnswer`, `AttachAurasChoiceInteractionHandler#handleAnswer`, `AttackingPlayerChoosesCreatureToBoostEffectHandler#resolve`, `BalduvianWarlordEffectHandler#resolve`, `BeholdChoiceInteractionHandler#handleAnswer`, `ChoiceHandlerService#handleEachPlayerChoosesColorThenExileOtherPermanentsChoice`, `ChoiceHandlerService#handleListChoice`, `ChooseOpponentCreatureAndPerpetuallyBoostEffectHandler#resolve`, `ChooseOpponentCreatureThenBoostOthersEffectHandler#resolve`, `CommandZoneCardChoiceInteractionHandler#handleAnswer`, +101 more; `PermanentRemovalService#handleExileReturnOnLeave` ← `PermanentRemovalService#enforceAttachmentLegality`, `PermanentRemovalService#processAlreadyRemovedToGraveyard`, `PermanentRemovalService#removeOrphanedAuras`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToCommandZone`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToExile`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToGraveyard`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToLibrary`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToLibraryBottom`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToLibraryPosition`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToLibraryTop`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentWithoutDestination`, `PermanentRemovalService#returnPermanentToHand`. Notes: mixed: replacement = PutCountersOnTurnFaceUpEffect (TurnFaceUpReplacementEffect; megamorph/'as turned face up' counters, applied in place before triggers, honours appliesWithoutPayingCost), TurnFaceUpCopyEffect (Vesuvan Shapeshifter copy choice), TurnFaceUpAttachAuraEffect (Gift of Doom), ChoosePowerToughnessFormEffect (Aquamorph Entity, PowerToughnessFormChoiceEffect); all implement ReplacementEffect, read by TurnFaceUpCopyService#prepareChoice/findCopyEffect/preparePowerToughnessChoice/applyTurnFaceUpReplacements and filtered out of the trigger; every other class is the 'when turned face up' trigger. Non-replacement effects (intervening-if ConditionalEffect pre-checked) go into ONE stack object or one targeting interaction (graveyard / spell / player-or-permanent / multi-target); a lone ChooseOneEffect becomes a TriggeredModalTrigger. SURPRISE: two divergent readers: GameService#finishTurningFaceUp adds temporary+persistent granted effects (GrantTurnFaceUpAbilityToOwnFaceDownCreaturesEffect, each granted one as its own stack object in the untargeted branch), checks hasLostAllAbilities and multiplies by countAdditionalTriggeredAbilityTriggers; TurnFaceUpCopyService#finishTurnFaceUp does none of that, and the GameService path hands off to it whenever prepareChoice opens a choice. Other readers: PermanentRemovalService#handleExileReturnOnLeave (looks for exile-until-leaves effects), GrantTurnFaceUpAbilityToOwnFaceDownCreaturesEffectHandler (grants). choke points: GameService#finishTurningFaceUp (morph/megamorph/disguise/manifest/cloak special action, turnPermanentFaceUpWithoutPayingManaCost*) and TurnFaceUpCopyService#finishTurnFaceUp (turnFaceUpWithoutCost from StateBasedActionService#performStateBasedActions masked auto-turn, TurnTargetCreatureFaceUp(IfFaceDown)EffectHandler; completeChoice after copy/aura/PT choice); MutationSupport turns a host face up without triggers.

Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSelfOrAllyCreatureTurnsFaceUpTriggers` (each); `FaceUpTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 2, `ConditionalEffect` 1, `MayEffect` 1. Dispatch per registration to FaceUpTriggerCollectorService: DealDamageToPlayersEffect(EACH_OPPONENT, SourcePower) uses the turned creature as damage source; default handler unwraps TriggeringCard/TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect (CardPredicate checked on effective subtypes), OncePerTurn/OnceOnly; declared-target effects -> EntersTriggerTarget interaction, else stack.add with turned permanent as non-targeting targetId. MayEffect not unwrapped (resolves as may). choke points: GameService#finishTurningFaceUp (morph/megamorph/disguise/manifest/cloak special action, turnPermanentFaceUpWithoutPayingManaCost*) and TurnFaceUpCopyService#finishTurnFaceUp (turnFaceUpWithoutCost from StateBasedActionService#performStateBasedActions masked auto-turn, TurnTargetCreatureFaceUp(IfFaceDown)EffectHandler; completeChoice after copy/aura/PT choice).

Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyTokenEntersTriggers` (each); `EnterTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `CreateTokenCopyOfEnteringTokenForTargetPlayerEffect`, `CreateTokenCopyOfTargetPermanentEffect`. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Modes: `PER_TOKEN` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyTokenEntersTriggers` ← `BattlefieldEntryBatchSupport#placeCards`, `PermanentControlSupport#applyCreateToken`, `TokenCopySupport#putPreparedTokenCopiesOntoBattlefield`. Notes: Opponent loop at the end of TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyTokenEntersTriggers (called from token-creation paths: EtbTriggerService, BattlefieldEntryService, BattlefieldEntryBatchSupport, TokenCopySupport, PermanentControlSupport, AsEntersInteractionService). OncePerTurnTriggerEffect unwrapped in the check; handlers in EnterTriggerCollectorService: default (targeted -> queueInteraction EntersTriggerTarget, else enqueueTrigger with eventValue=count, both x perEffectTriggerCount), CreateTokenCopyOfTargetPermanentEffect (Kambal: one entry over all token ids), CreateTokenCopyOfEnteringTokenForTargetPlayerEffect (first token only). The count-only overload passes no token ids, so the copy handlers return false. No lost-ability check on watchers; Elesh Norn-style suppression checked per watcher controller.

Notes: StepTriggerService#handleUpkeepTriggers battlefield loop over the active player's battlefield only: printed effects (skipped if face-down) + temporary + persistent, all suppressed by hasLostPrintedAbilities (so a printed-ability loss also drops granted ones), + GrantedUpkeepEffectSupport#appendGrantedUpkeepEffects (cumulative upkeep / GrantTriggeredAbilityEffect). Opens a countAdditionalTriggeredAbilityTriggers copy scope (the only step slot here that does). Partitioning: intervening-if ConditionalEffects pre-filtered; ChooseModeNotYetChosenEffect and ChooseOneEffect each a separate modal interaction; any-target effect present -> the WHOLE remaining list in one UpkeepAnyTargetTrigger; player-target effects grouped into one Upkeep(Multi)PlayerTargetTrigger; Puca's-Mischief exchange -> whole list; graveyard-target effects (+PutCounterOnReturnedPermanentsEffect) grouped; a SurveilEffect makes ALL remaining effects one stack entry (break); everything else one stack object per registration (MayEffect -> queueMayAbility, targeted MayEffect/permanent-target -> UpkeepPermanentTarget/ETBTokenMultiTarget interaction, ~15 condition-specific branches). Also read from face-up schemes in the command zone (StepTriggerService#handleUpkeepTriggers COMMAND_ZONE section) and planes (PlanechaseService#step). SURPRISE: PermanentCounterSupport#notifyLastCounterRemoved re-reads this slot to fire Divine Intervention's 'when the last counter is removed' DrawGameEffect outside upkeep. Other readers grant into it (CloneService, GraveyardReturnSupport, PutCounterOnCombatOpponentEffectHandler) or inspect it (Permanent#hasCumulativeUpkeep).

StepTriggerService#queueBeginningOfCombatTriggers grouping: each untargeted MayEffect queued separately via queueMayAbility; each ChooseOneEffect / ChooseModeNotYetChosen(ThisTurn)Effect is its own modal trigger (TriggeredModalTrigger/UpkeepModalTrigger); ChooseOneAtRandomEffect is resolved at trigger time into the shared group; intervening-if ConditionalEffects gated at trigger time; ALL remaining registrations (mandatory, targeted mays, MayPayManaEffect) share ONE stack object or ONE targeting interaction (BeginningOfCombatTriggerTarget / ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger / SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger); TriggerMode is never read. Skips face-down / lost-printed-ability sources; an Equipment attached to a non-creature is skipped. Planes via PlanechaseService#step/trigger (mostly one entry per registration). SURPRISE: the active player's command zone (any non-scheme, face-up card) is scanned unconditionally (added for eminence, e.g. Arahbo) - a commander sitting in the command zone with an ordinary 'at the beginning of combat on your turn' ability triggers unless the card wraps it in a SourceCardInCommandZone/OnBattlefield intervening-if. - **`END_OF_COMBAT_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `StepTriggerService`: `handleEndOfCombatTriggers` (all); `PermanentRemovalService`: `handleExileReturnOnLeave` (all). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 17, `MayEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Choke points: `PermanentRemovalService#handleExileReturnOnLeave` ← `PermanentRemovalService#enforceAttachmentLegality`, `PermanentRemovalService#processAlreadyRemovedToGraveyard`, `PermanentRemovalService#removeOrphanedAuras`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToCommandZone`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToExile`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToGraveyard`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToLibrary`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToLibraryBottom`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToLibraryPosition`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentToLibraryTop`, `PermanentRemovalService#removePermanentWithoutDestination`, `PermanentRemovalService#returnPermanentToHand`. Notes: StepTriggerService#handleEndOfCombatTriggers (from TurnProgressionService after CombatService#clearCombatState): all registrations that pass the intervening-if filter go into ONE non-targeting entry via enqueueTrigger; no may/target/modal handling (MayEffect resolves on the stack); skips face-down / lost-printed-ability sources. PermanentRemovalService#handleExileReturnOnLeave only inspects the slot for ExileAllPermanentsUntilSourceLeavesEffect.returnsOnSourceLeavesTrigger (Wall of Nets return) - a presence read.
- **`PRECOMBAT_MAIN_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `StepTriggerService`: `handlePrecombatMainTriggers` (queue?), `handlePrecombatMainBattlefieldTriggers` (mode). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 15, `SequenceEffect` 8, `ConditionalEffect` 6, `MayEffect` 6, `ExileTargetCardFromGraveyardWithConditionalEffectsEffect` 1, `MayPayTapPermanentsEffect` 1. Modes: `INDEPENDENT` 2. 3 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: StepTriggerService#handlePrecombatMainBattlefieldTriggers: intervening-if gate; each ChooseOneEffect/ChooseModeNotYetChosenEffect its own TriggeredModalTrigger; player-only-targeted effects share one MainPhasePlayerTargetTrigger; permanent-targeted share one UpkeepPermanentTargetTrigger; graveyard-targeted share one SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger; untargeted ones (incl. MayEffect/MayPayManaEffect, resolved on the stack) share one stack object EXCEPT TriggerMode.INDEPENDENT registrations, each its own object (INDEPENDENT honoured only for untargeted effects). Reads playerBattlefields only (no face-up schemes); checks hasLostPrintedAbilities but not face-down. Planes via PlanechaseService#step from handlePrecombatMainTriggers.
- **`GRAVEYARD_PRECOMBAT_MAIN_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `StepTriggerService`: `handlePrecombatMainGraveyardTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1, `MayEffect` 1. Notes: StepTriggerService#handlePrecombatMainGraveyardTriggers (getEffectiveGraveyardEffects): each registration separate; intervening-if checked; MayEffect -> queueMayAbility, else its own stack.add; no target selection support.
- **`EACH_PRECOMBAT_MAIN_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `StepTriggerService`: `handleEachPrecombatMainTriggers` (queue?). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: StepTriggerService#handleEachPrecombatMainTriggers: all registrations passing the intervening-if filter in ONE stack object (stack.add); no may/target/modal handling; no face-down or lost-ability check. Doc says 'first main phase'; code fires at every precombat main phase.
- **`POSTCOMBAT_MAIN_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `StepTriggerService`: `handlePostcombatMainTriggers` (read). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 23, `SequenceEffect` 6, `MayEffect` 4. Trigger effects: `SurvivalTriggerEffect` 16, `OnceOnlyTriggerEffect` 2. Notes: StepTriggerService#handleMainPhaseTriggers (shared with SECOND_MAIN_PHASE_TRIGGERED): intervening-if + OnceOnlyTriggerSupport gates; player-only-targeted effects share one MainPhasePlayerTargetTrigger; graveyard-targeted share one SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger; permanent-targeted share one EndStepTriggerTarget; everything else (incl. untargeted mays) shares one stack object; no INDEPENDENT and no modal handling (a ChooseOneEffect would just go on the stack). Uses hasLostAllAbilities (not hasLostPrintedAbilities), no face-down check - differs from the precombat reader. Survival (SurvivalTriggerEffect, 16 regs, 'second main phase') lives here gated to the first postcombat main instead of SECOND_MAIN_PHASE_TRIGGERED.
- **`SECOND_MAIN_PHASE_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `StepTriggerService`: `handlePrecombatMainTriggers` (queue?), `handlePostcombatMainTriggers` (read). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 4, `SequenceEffect` 2. Notes: Fired by StepTriggerService#handleMainPhaseTriggers when mainPhasesBegunThisTurn == 2, from either handlePrecombatMainTriggers (before the precombat slots) or handlePostcombatMainTriggers (after POSTCOMBAT_MAIN_TRIGGERED); same grouping as POSTCOMBAT_MAIN_TRIGGERED (player-/graveyard-/permanent-targeted groups, rest shared; no INDEPENDENT/modal support).

- **`ON_SELF_MANA_ABILITY_RESOLVES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkManaAbilityResolutionTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkManaAbilityResolutionTriggers, called from AbilityActivationService (tap-for-mana), ActivatedAbilityExecutionService (activated mana abilities) and ChoiceHandlerService (after a mana colour choice).
