Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSpellCastTriggers over the caster's graveyard (getEffectiveGraveyardEffects): only SpellCastTriggerEffect registrations honoured (others ignored); gates spellFilter, castSpellTargetCondition, nthSpellNumber, mana-spent; other SpellCastTriggerEffect fields (targetFilter, intervening, turn restrictions...) are not read here; each registration its own stack.add (no target/may queueing). SURPRISE: the manaCost path wraps only the FIRST resolved effect in a MayPayManaEffect (any further resolvedEffects are dropped) and sets triggeringCardId to the graveyard card rather than the spell.
- **`HAND_ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `SpellCastTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSpellCastTriggers over the caster's hand: only SpellCastTriggerEffect (spellFilter + mana-spent gates); one stack object per registration holding its resolvedEffects; no target/may handling. Only user is Covercast (Summitfest Closing Ceremony), which registers the same trigger in GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL too.
- **`EXILE_ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each); `SpellCastTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `SpellCastTriggerEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `SpellCastTriggerEffect` 3. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSpellCastTriggers loops face-up gameData.exiledCards owned by the caster and registry.dispatch()es each effect (null permanent); SpellCastTriggerCollectorService#handleExileResidentSpellCastTrigger -> handleGenericSpellCastTrigger (full SpellCastTriggerEffect gates, targeting/may there). No default handler, so non-SpellCastTriggerEffect registrations are silently ignored.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_ACTIVATES_EXHAUST_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerActivatesExhaustAbilityTriggersFromGraveyard` (each). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerActivatesExhaustAbilityTriggersFromGraveyard, called from ActivatedAbilityExecutionService only for battlefield-permanent exhaust activations: one enqueueTrigger entry per registration (getEffectiveGraveyardEffects); no target/may/intervening-if handling.
- **`COMMAND_ZONE_ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `SpellCastTriggerEffect` 2. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSpellCastTriggers over the caster's command zone: only SpellCastTriggerEffect.spellFilter checked (no mana/nth/target-condition gates, unlike the graveyard reader); stack.add of resolvedEffects per registration; no target/may handling and no triggeringCardId set. The 'if in the command zone' clause relies on a SourceCardInCommandZone intervening-if wrapper.
- **`ON_SELF_CAST`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSpellCastTriggers` (each); `PerpetualPowerToughnessChoiceInteractionHandler`: `handleAnswer` (read); `PerpetualHandOrGraveyardCardChoiceInteractionHandler`: `handleAnswer` (read); `PredicateEvaluationService`: `matchesCardPredicateInternal` (read); `PerpetuallyGrantAnyColorManaAndSelfCastAbilityToCardEffectHandler`: `resolve` (read); `PerpetuallyGiveSpellCastLifeLossToRandomNonlandCardInDamagedHandEffectHandler`: `resolve` (read); `PerpetuallyGiveSpellCastLifeLossToDefendingHandEffectHandler`: `resolve` (read); `PerpetuallyGivePoisonCounterOnCastToRandomNonlandCardInDamagedHandEffectHandler`: `resolve` (read); `EffectResolution`: `hasManaSpentToCastAmount` (read). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 19, `ConditionalEffect` 5, `MayPayManaEffect` 3, `SequenceEffect` 1, `TriggeringSpellOpponentControllerConditionalEffect` 1. Modes: `INDEPENDENT` 4. 15 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSpellCastTriggers: special classes each get their own stack object (CopyThisSpellIfCondition/IfCasualtyPaid/ForXValue, ReplicateEffect, SpellCastCopyTriggerEffect incl. Gravestorm/Coststorm, StormEffect, CascadeEffect, Demonstrate); intervening-if gated against the spell entry; ALL other registrations share ONE stack object or one targeting interaction (SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget / ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger / SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger), except a lone untargeted non-Ripple MayEffect goes to pendingMayAbilities. TriggerMode.INDEPENDENT ignored (only used on CascadeEffect, which is separate anyway). Granted replicate/demonstrate and perpetual grants are folded in (untargeted perpetual grants each separate). Doc says instant/sorcery but permanent spells use it too (cascade/emerge creatures). Other readers only inspect/add (PredicateEvaluationService, EffectResolution#hasManaSpentToCastAmount, perpetual grant handlers).
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_ACTIVATES_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerActivatesAbilityTriggers` (each), `checkControllerActivatesAbilityCopyTriggers` (each), `checkControllerActivatesNinjutsuAbilityTriggers` (each); `ChoiceHandlerService`: `handleSourceSubtypeChoice` (read). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 8, `TriggeringLoyaltyAbilityConditionalEffect` 2, `TriggeringNonTapAbilityConditionalEffect` 2, `SequenceEffect` 1, `TriggeringBoastAbilityConditionalEffect` 1, `TriggeringNinjutsuAbilityConditionalEffect` 1, `TriggeringOutlastAbilityConditionalEffect` 1, `TriggeringPowerUpAbilityConditionalEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `CopyControllerActivatedAbilityTriggerEffect` 3, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 3. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerActivatesAbilityTriggers (ActivatedAbilityExecutionService, battlefield-permanent abilities only - never for hand/graveyard abilities): one entry per registration; permanent-target -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget, any-target -> SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget, else enqueueTrigger; mana-ability triggers deferred. mixed readers: CopyControllerActivatedAbilityTriggerEffect regs are skipped there and collected after the ability is on the stack by checkControllerActivatesAbilityCopyTriggers (also from hand paths; no lost-ability check); TriggeringNinjutsuAbilityConditionalEffect regs by checkControllerActivatesNinjutsuAbilityTriggers.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_ACTIVATES_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentActivatesAbilityTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 4, `TriggeringNonTapAbilityConditionalEffect` 3, `ConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentActivatesAbilityTriggers: one entry per registration, activating player baked as non-targeting targetId; targeted effects -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget / SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget. Called from ActivatedAbilityExecutionService and from only ONE of the two hand-ability completion paths in AbilityActivationService (~2862, non-mana only); the other hand path (~2743) and graveyard abilities never call it - inconsistent.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_ACTIVATES_EXHAUST_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerActivatesExhaustAbilityTriggers` (each). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerActivatesExhaustAbilityTriggers (ActivatedAbilityExecutionService, battlefield-permanent exhaust activations only): one enqueueTrigger entry per registration; no lost-ability/face-down check, no target/may/intervening-if handling.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_ACTIVATES_NONMANA_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectNonTapActivationTriggersBeforeCosts` (mixed), `checkControllerActivatesNonManaAbilityTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 2, `MayPayManaEffect` 1, `TriggeringTapAbilityConditionalEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `CopyControllerActivatedAbilityTriggerEffect` 11. Notes: mixed readers: TriggeringNonTapAbilityConditionalEffect regs are captured BEFORE costs by TriggerCollectionService#collectNonTapActivationTriggersBeforeCosts (battlefield activations only; no lost-ability check; entries added after the ability); the rest by checkControllerActivatesNonManaAbilityTriggers after the push (battlefield, hand and graveyard activation paths): CopyControllerActivatedAbilityTriggerEffect -> copy trigger wrapped in MayPayMana/MayPayLife/May; other effects -> any-target interaction or plain entry (permanent-only targets are not routed to target selection). One entry per registration.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_ACTIVATES_ETERNALIZE_OR_EMBALM`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerActivatesEternalizeOrEmbalmTriggers` (each). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerActivatesEternalizeOrEmbalmTriggers from AbilityActivationService's graveyard-ability path after the ability is on the stack: one enqueueTrigger per registration; no lost-ability/target/may handling.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_ACTIVATES_NONMANA_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectNonTapActivationTriggersBeforeCosts` (mixed), `checkOpponentActivatesNonManaAbilityTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 4, `MayEffect` 1, `MayPayManaEffect` 1, `TriggeringTapAbilityConditionalEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: Pre-cost capture of TriggeringNonTapAbilityConditionalEffect regs in collectNonTapActivationTriggersBeforeCosts + TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentActivatesNonManaAbilityTriggers (battlefield, hand, graveyard activation paths): one non-targeting entry per registration with the activating player (or triggering-permanent controller) as targetId; no target selection support.

- **`ON_BLOCK`**. Readers: `BoostByBushidoEffectHandler`: `bushidoValue` (read); `CombatBlockService`: `processBlockerDeclaration` (all), `applyBlockFromEffect` (all), `collectEffectBlockTrigger` (all). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 6, `SequenceEffect` 4, `ConditionalEffect` 3, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 3, `MayPayManaEffect` 2. Modes: `ONCE_PER_BLOCK` 62, simultaneous 1. 6 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: CombatBlockService#processBlockerDeclaration: all resolved printed + temporary/persistent/granted effects share ONE stack object per assignment (attacker baked as non-targeting target for combat-opponent effects); 2+ BushidoEffects are split one per instance; card with a target filter + targeted effect -> one AttackTriggerTarget for the group. SURPRISE: ONCE_PER_BLOCK is read as a card-level flag - one ONCE_PER_BLOCK registration makes every ON_BLOCK registration on that card (and granted ones) fire once. Effect-created blocks: applyBlockFromEffect/collectEffectBlockTrigger (no bushido split; SURPRISE: printed effects are not suppressed when the blocker lost its abilities, only face-down is checked). Attachments: CombatTriggerService#checkAuraTriggersForCreature (all-but-independent, ONCE_PER_BLOCK per attachment). BoostByBushidoEffectHandler reads the slot for the bushido value.

- **`ON_OPPONENT_CREATURES_ATTACK_YOU_UNBLOCKED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `isDirectAttackTriggerSlot` (read, switch); `CombatBlockService`: `checkOpponentCreatureAttacksYouUnblockedTriggers` (queue?), `checkOpponentCreaturesAttackYouUnblockedTriggers` (queue?). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: CombatBlockService#checkOpponentCreaturesAttackYouUnblockedTriggers (plural; called at ~277 and ~946). Printed + granted effects bundled into one stack.add, targetId = attacking player; no lost-abilities check, no target support. defenderId comes from getOpponentId, so only one opponent's battlefield is checked (2-player assumption). The singular twin #checkOpponentCreatureAttacksYouUnblockedTriggers (adds an isCreature check) has no callers (dead code). The slot is in TriggerCollectionService#isDirectAttackTriggerSlot, but no reader goes through dispatch, so that entry has no effect.
- **`ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkTemporaryGlobalCreatureAttacksYouTriggers` (queue?), `isDirectAttackTriggerSlot` (read, switch); `RegisterGlobalTriggeredAbilityUntilNextTurnEffectHandler`: `resolve` (read); `CombatAttackService`: `finishAttackerDeclaration` (mode). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1, `MayEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Choke points: `RegisterGlobalTriggeredAbilityUntilNextTurnEffectHandler#resolve` ← `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#doResolveManaAbility`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#performSpecialAction`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#pushAbilityOnStack`, `AnimationSupport#fireTransformTriggers`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeCreatureOrTransformSourceEffectHandler#finish`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeNontokenCreatureTapAndSeekLandSourceEffectHandler#tapThenSeek`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#dealDamage`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#sacrificeSource`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceHandler#handle`, `AnyOpponentMayTapCreatureTapAndCreateTokenSourceEffectHandler#tapAndCreateToken`, `AnyPlayerMaySacrificeLandPutSourceOnTopEffectHandler#putSourceOnTop`, `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrBoostSelfEffectHandler#boostSelf`, +193 more. Notes: CombatAttackService#finishAttackerDeclaration (~2752-2855). Defender's permanents, gated by face-down/lost abilities; printed only. DealDamageToTriggeringAttackerEffect.attackerCondition and any ConditionalEffect checked at trigger time. SURPRISE: non-INDEPENDENT effects from ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU, ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU_DIRECTLY and ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_DEFENDING_PLAYER on the same card are bundled into one stack object; INDEPENDENT ones are separate. Always stack.add nonTargeting with targetId = attacker (no target support). Temporary watchers: TriggerCollectionService#checkTemporaryGlobalCreatureAttacksYouTriggers, registered by RegisterGlobalTriggeredAbilityUntilNextTurnEffectHandler.
- **`ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU_DIRECTLY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `isDirectAttackTriggerSlot` (read, switch); `CombatAttackService`: `finishAttackerDeclaration` (mode). Modes: `INDEPENDENT` 2. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: Same loop as ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU in CombatAttackService#finishAttackerDeclaration; added only when the attack target is a player. Bundled with the card's ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU / _DEFENDING_PLAYER effects (cross-slot); INDEPENDENT separate (Barbed Foliage).
- **`ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_DEFENDING_PLAYER`**. Readers: `CombatAttackService`: `finishAttackerDeclaration` (mode). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: Same loop as ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU in CombatAttackService#finishAttackerDeclaration; the only one of the three that also fires for battle attacks (protector). Bundled cross-slot with the card's ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU(_DIRECTLY) effects. Not in isDirectAttackTriggerSlot, but the loop uses beginAttackTriggerCopies, so attack-trigger doublers still count. Kazuul's MayPayManaEffect goes straight on the stack.
- **`ON_CREATURES_ATTACK_YOU`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `isDirectAttackTriggerSlot` (read, switch); `CombatAttackService`: `finishAttackerDeclaration` (mode). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1, `MayEffect` 1. Notes: CombatAttackService#finishAttackerDeclaration (~2857-2925). Intervening-if ConditionalEffect checked; all printed effects of a card in one object: targeted -> AttackTriggerTarget (target chooser = active player if a MayEffect has ACTIVE_PLAYER choice), else stack.add nonTargeting with attacker snapshots. No lost-abilities/face-down gate, no granted effects.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_ATTACKS_PLANESWALKER_YOU_CONTROL`**. Readers: `CombatAttackService`: `finishAttackerDeclaration` (mode). 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: CombatAttackService#finishAttackerDeclaration (~2927-2975). Javadoc says 'once per combat' but code fires once per attacked planeswalker; that matches the Oath of Kaya ruling (2019-05-03), so the doc is wrong, not the code. All printed effects in one stack.add, targetId = attacking player, no target support, no lost-abilities gate. Copy scope via countAdditionalTriggeredAbilityTriggers(..., true).
- **`ON_ANY_CREATURE_ATTACKS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkTemporaryGlobalCreatureAttackTriggers` (each), `checkPlanarAllyCreatureAttackTriggers` (queue?), `isDirectAttackTriggerSlot` (read, switch); `CombatAttackService`: `finishAttackerDeclaration` (mode). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 22, `SequenceEffect` 4, `MayEffect` 3, `TriggeringPermanentControllerConditionalEffect` 3, `ConditionalEffect` 2, `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: CombatAttackService#finishAttackerDeclaration (~3017-3105): per attacker x every permanent on every battlefield; printed effects only (no lost-abilities/face-down gate, no granted). Nested TriggeringPermanentControllerConditionalEffect / TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect / PutCounterOnAttackingCreatureOnAttacksYouEffect (attached-player check) / AttackedTargetIsMonarch filters; all matching effects in one stack.add nonTargeting with targetId = attacker (targeted effects such as DealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect get the attacker as an implicit non-chosen target). Also TriggerCollectionService#checkTemporaryGlobalCreatureAttackTriggers (temporary watchers, can queue AttackTriggerTarget) and #checkPlanarAllyCreatureAttackTriggers.
- **`ON_ANY_PLAYER_ATTACKS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `isDirectAttackTriggerSlot` (read, switch); `CombatAttackService`: `finishAttackerDeclaration` (mode). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 32, `SequenceEffect` 9, `MayEffect` 6, `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Modes: `INDEPENDENT` 2. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: CombatAttackService#finishAttackerDeclaration (~3134-3315) + #addPlayerAttackTrigger. Every permanent on every battlefield, printed only, no lost-abilities gate. A long condition switch: some unwrapped at trigger time, AllOf and AttackedOpponentHasMoreLife kept for resolution recheck. Per-opponent conditioned effects grouped per attacked opponent into separate objects; the rest: INDEPENDENT separate, others bundled; targeted -> AttackTriggerTarget, else stack.add with targetId = attacking player.
- **`ON_BLOCKS_MULTIPLE_CREATURES`**. Readers: `CombatBlockService`: `processBlockerDeclaration` (all), `applyBlockFromEffect` (all). Notes: CombatBlockService#processBlockerDeclaration (~832-857: blockers with 2+ assignments in the declaration) and #applyBlockFromEffect (~1033: whenever blockingTargetIds.size() >= 2 after an effect-created block, so it can fire again when a third attacker is added). All printed effects in one stack.add, nonTargeting, targetId/sourcePermanentId = blocker. No lost-abilities gate, no granted effects.
- **`ON_ANY_CREATURE_BLOCKS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectTemporaryGlobalTriggers` (read); `CombatBlockService`: `checkAnyCreatureBlocksTriggers` (queue?). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 3, `MayEffect` 1. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#collectTemporaryGlobalTriggers` ← `CombatBlockService#checkAnyCreatureBlocksTriggers`, `CombatDamageService#checkAllyCreatureCombatDamageToPlayerTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyCreatureDealtDamageTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyCreatureDeathTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyNontokenCreatureDeathTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#checkEnchantedPermanentTapTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#checkLifeGainTriggers`. Notes: CombatBlockService#checkAnyCreatureBlocksTriggers, from #processBlockerDeclaration (~862) and #applyBlockFromEffect (~1052, so effect-created blocks also count). Every battlefield; skips hasLostPrintedAbilities; TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect filtered against the blocker; all matching effects in one stack.add nonTargeting, targetId = blocker. Also TriggerCollectionService#collectTemporaryGlobalTriggers(ON_ANY_CREATURE_BLOCKS) for temporary watchers.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_BLOCKED`**. Readers: `CombatBlockService`: `checkAllyBecomesBlockedTriggers` (all). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 4, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 4, `ConditionalEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 1. Modes: `PER_BLOCKER` 1. Notes: CombatBlockService#checkAllyBecomesBlockedTriggers(mode), called per blocked attacker (band-mates included) from #processBlockerDeclaration (NORMAL once, PER_BLOCKER once per blocker, but the blocker itself is not passed in), effect-created blocks (~1251), token entering blocking (~2065) and #fireBecomesBlockedTriggersWithoutBlockers (NORMAL only). Registrations are split by TriggerMode, and all of one mode on a card share one object. Skips hasLostPrintedAbilities (PER_BLOCKER also when the blocked attacker lost abilities). TriggeringCard/TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect unwrapped; targeted -> AttackTriggerTarget, else stack.add with sourcePermanentId = blocked attacker (watcher if BoostReferencedPermanentEffect).
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURES_BECOME_BLOCKED`**. Readers: `CombatBlockService`: `checkAllyCreaturesBecomeBlockedTriggers` (all). Notes: CombatBlockService#checkAllyCreaturesBecomeBlockedTriggers from #processBlockerDeclaration only (effect-created blocks, blocking tokens and blocked-without-blockers never fire it). Only NORMAL registrations; all in one stack.add nonTargeting; no lost-abilities gate, no target support.
- **`ON_ANY_CREATURE_BECOMES_BLOCKED`**. Readers: `CombatBlockService`: `checkAnyCreatureBecomesBlockedTriggers` (queue?), `checkBlockedCreatureTriggers` (queue?). Wrappers: `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 2. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: Two readers in CombatBlockService. #checkAnyCreatureBecomesBlockedTriggers keeps only BlockPairConditionalEffect (DestroyWeakerBlockParticipantEffect, BoostBlockerWhenAttackerMatchesEffect): one stack.add per registration per pair, targetId = named participant. #checkBlockedCreatureTriggers keeps only BlockedCreatureTriggerEffect (implemented by TriggeringCardConditionalEffect): all matching on a card bundled per blocked attacker. SURPRISE: any other effect class in this slot is silently ignored. No lost-abilities gate. Called from #processBlockerDeclaration, #applyBlockFromEffect (attacker not previously blocked), and #fireBecomesBlockedTriggersWithoutBlockers (BlockedCreatureTriggerEffect reader only).
- **`ON_ANY_CREATURES_BLOCK`**. Readers: `CombatBlockService`: `checkAnyCreaturesBlockTriggers` (all). Notes: CombatBlockService#checkAnyCreaturesBlockTriggers from #processBlockerDeclaration (~902) and #applyBlockFromEffect (~1074, so it re-fires for each effect-created block in the same combat). Skips hasLostAllAbilities; all printed effects in one stack.add, no targetId.
- **`ON_SELF_DEALS_COMBAT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `queueSourceDealsCombatDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`; `CombatDamageService`: `finishCombatDamageStep` (read). Wrappers: `MayCastAnySpellFromHandWithoutPayingManaCostEffect` 1, `MayEffect` 1. Aware: `CombatDamageAmountAwareEffect` 1. Notes: CombatDamageService#processSelfDealsCombatDamageTriggers -> TriggerCollectionService#queueSourceDealsCombatDamageTriggers -> #dispatchSourceDealsCombatDamageTriggers: printed sourceCard.getEffects(slot) plus temporary/persistent/granted snapshotted in the combat damage step (~723) before SBA, plus temporary-global watchers bound to this permanent. Handler #handleSelfDealsCombatDamage: graveyard target -> SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger, permanent/player target -> SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget, else enqueueTrigger with eventValue. SURPRISE: printed effects have no face-down/hasLostPrintedAbilities gate (ON_SELF_DEALS_DAMAGE has one), so Humility or a face-down source would still trigger.
- **`ON_SELF_DEALS_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER_OR_PLANESWALKER`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `queueSourceDealsCombatDamageToPlayerOrPlaneswalkerTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`; `CombatDamageService`: `finishCombatDamageStep` (read). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Aware: `CombatDamageAmountAwareEffect` 4, `DamageSourceControllerAwareEffect` 1. Notes: CombatDamageService#processSelfDealsCombatDamageToPlayerOrPlaneswalkerTriggers -> TriggerCollectionService#queueSourceDealsCombatDamageToPlayerOrPlaneswalkerTriggers -> #dispatchSourceDealsCombatDamageTriggers; totalDamage = player + planeswalker damage. Same handler and same missing lost-abilities/face-down gate on printed effects as ON_SELF_DEALS_COMBAT_DAMAGE.
- **`ON_SELF_DEALS_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER_OR_BATTLE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `queueSourceDealsCombatDamageToPlayerOrBattleTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `ChooseOneAtTriggerTimeEffect`; `CombatDamageService`: `finishCombatDamageStep` (read). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Aware: `CombatDamageAmountAwareEffect` 5. Notes: CombatDamageService#processSelfDealsCombatDamageToPlayerOrBattleTriggers -> TriggerCollectionService#queueSourceDealsCombatDamageToPlayerOrBattleTriggers -> #dispatchSourceDealsCombatDamageTriggers (same call also fires ON_EQUIPPED_CREATURE_DEALS_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER_OR_BATTLE). Extra ChooseOneAtTriggerTimeEffect handler -> TriggeredModalTrigger. Same missing lost-abilities/face-down gate on printed effects.
- **`ON_EQUIPPED_CREATURE_DEALS_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER_OR_BATTLE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `queueEquippedCreatureDealsCombatDamageToPlayerOrBattleTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#queueEquippedCreatureDealsCombatDamageToPlayerOrBattleTriggers (from #queueSourceDealsCombatDamageToPlayerOrBattleTriggers): all permanents whose printed subtypes contain EQUIPMENT and are attached to the source (last-known attachment if the host died). Handler #queueEquippedCreatureCombatDamageTrigger: targeted -> SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget, graveyard -> SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger, else stack object from the equipment. Printed only, no lost-abilities gate; the Equipment check is the printed subtype, not effective.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_COMBAT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `snapshotAllyCreatureDealsCombatDamageWatchers` (read), `queueSourceDealsCombatDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#snapshotAllyCreatureDealsCombatDamageWatchers (before SBA, so watchers killed by the same damage still trigger) + #queueSourceDealsCombatDamageTriggers -> dispatch per registration -> DamageTriggerCollectorService#handleAllyCreatureDealsCombatDamage (intervening-if, enqueueTrigger nonTargeting with the watcher as source, triggeringPermanentId = dealer, eventValue = damage). Printed only; no lost-abilities gate on watchers.
- **`ON_CREATURE_DEALS_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_YOU`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDamageDealtToControllerTriggers` (each), `queueCreatureCombatDamageToYouTriggers` (all), `hasPlanarCreatureCombatDamageToYouTrigger` (read), `queuePlanarCreatureCombatDamageToYouTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 3, `SequenceEffect` 2, `ConditionalEffect` 1, `MayEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `BatchedCombatDamageToYouTriggerEffect` 5. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkDamageDealtToControllerTriggers (combat branch), called per source per recipient from CombatDamageService after state-based actions. queueCreatureCombatDamageToYouTriggers resolves TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect and intervening-if ConditionalEffect, unwraps Batched* (deduped by perm:index key) and rewrites DamageSourceControllerGainsControlOfThisPermanentEffect. It then puts ONE stack entry per watcher holding all surviving effects (grouping all): targetId=damaging creature, nonTargeting, direct stack.add. queuePlanarCreatureCombatDamageToYouTriggers instead makes one entry per registration (grouping each); hasPlanarCreatureCombatDamageToYouTrigger is a presence check. Cabal Slaver and Brood Sliver use this slot as the 'to you' half of 'to a player', paired with ON_ANY_CREATURE_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT. SURPRISE: watchers are read after combat state-based actions, so a watcher killed by the same damage misses its trigger, and two independent abilities on one card would be merged into one stack object.

| `ON_ALLY_CREATURES_DEAL_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` | triggered | one or more creatures deal damage to a player (combat or noncombat) | creature (TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect predicate) | dealers you control; damaged player any | battlefield | per event: combat once per attacking controller per damaged player per damage step; noncombat once per damage application | yes | each | 2 |
| `ON_ALLY_CREATURES_DEAL_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT` | triggered | one or more creatures (or predicate-matching objects) deal damage to an opponent (combat or noncombat) | creature; with a predicate, any matching object including spells (Kindred Pirates) | dealers you control; damaged player is an opponent | battlefield | per event: combat once per attacking controller per defender per damage step; noncombat once per damage application | yes | each | 2 |
| `ON_ALLY_SOURCE_DEALS_NONCOMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT` | triggered | a source deals noncombat damage to an opponent | source (any) | source you control; damaged player is another player | battlefield | per damage application (per source per damaged player) | yes | each | 5 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_DEALT_DAMAGE_BY_OPPONENT` | triggered | you are dealt damage (combat or noncombat) by a source an opponent controls | source controlled by an opponent | you (damaged player); source controlled by an opponent | battlefield | per damage source (combat: per opposing source; noncombat: per damage application) | n/a | each | 4 |
| `ON_OPPONENT_SOURCE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_YOU_OR_YOUR_PERMANENT` | triggered | a source an opponent controls deals damage to you or a permanent you control (combat or noncombat) | source controlled by an opponent; recipient is you or a permanent you control | source controlled by an opponent; recipient you / you control | battlefield | per damage source per recipient (player or each damaged permanent) per damage event | n/a | each | 2 |
| `ON_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_YOU_OR_YOUR_PERMANENT` | triggered | a creature deals damage (combat or noncombat) to you or a permanent you control | creature of the watcher's chosen color; damaged permanent filtered by the effect | recipient is you or a permanent you control; creature of any controller | battlefield | per damaging creature per recipient per damage event | n/a | each | 1 |
| `ON_ALLY_SOURCE_DEALS_NONCOMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` | triggered | a source deals noncombat damage to a creature | creature (damaged; any controller) | source you control | battlefield | per damaged creature per damage application | yes | each | 2 |

- **`ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSourceDealsDamageToPlayerTriggers` (read); `CombatDamageService`: `processCombatDamageToPlayerTriggers` (each), `checkAttachedCombatDamageToPlayerTriggers` (all). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 4, `SequenceEffect` 1. Aware: `CombatDamageDealerAwareEffect` 5, `CombatDamageAmountAwareEffect` 4. Notes: combat: merged into CombatDamageService#processCombatDamageToPlayerTriggers (each) and checkAttachedCombatDamageToPlayerTriggers (all); noncombat: TriggerCollectionService#checkSourceDealsDamageToPlayerTriggers (from DamageSupport) -> queueNoncombatDamageToPlayerEffect per effect (each; attachments each too). SURPRISE: the noncombat path evaluates ANY ConditionalEffect at trigger time and unwraps it (combat gates only intervening-if), uses only hasLostAllAbilities (no face-down check) and ignores temporary/persistent ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER grants that the combat path includes.
- **`ON_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSourceDealsDamageToPlayerTriggers` (read); `CombatDamageService`: `processCombatDamageToPlayerTriggers` (each), `checkAttachedCombatDamageToPlayerTriggers` (all). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 8, `SequenceEffect` 5, `ConditionalEffect` 1. Aware: `CombatDamageDealerAwareEffect` 13, `CombatDamageAmountAwareEffect` 2. Notes: Same readers as ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER: combat creature path each, combat attached path all, noncombat checkSourceDealsDamageToPlayerTriggers each (with the same trigger-time unwrap of any ConditionalEffect). Opponent test = dealer controller != damaged player.
- **`ON_ANY_PERMANENT_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_YOU`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDamageDealtToControllerTriggers` (each), `hasPlanarPermanentDamageToYouTrigger` (read), `checkPlanarPermanentDealsDamageToYouTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 5 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `DamageSourceControllerGainsControlOfThisPermanentEffect`, `DestroyDamageSourcePermanentEffect`, `ExileDamageSourcePermanentUntilSourceLeavesEffect`, `PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect`, `ReturnDamageSourcePermanentToHandEffect`. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnPerCreatureTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkDamageDealtToControllerTriggers (from DamageSupport and CombatDamageService) dispatches each registration through the registry: only effect classes with a DamageTriggerCollectorService handler fire (ReturnDamageSourcePermanentToHand, DamageSourceControllerGainsControlOfThisPermanent, DestroyDamageSourcePermanent, PutCounterOnReferencedPermanent(TRIGGERING), ExileDamageSourcePermanentUntilSourceLeaves) - no default handler, other classes silently dropped. Planes via checkPlanarPermanentDealsDamageToYouTriggers (OncePerTurnPerCreatureTriggerEffect unwrap). Skips lost-printed-ability watchers.
- **`ON_ENCHANTED_CREATURE_DEALT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkEnchantedCreatureDealtDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 9 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `CreateTokenEffect`, `DestroyReferencedPermanentEffect`, `EnchantedCreatureControllerLosesLifeEffect`, `EnchantedCreatureDealsDamageEqualToDealtDamageToControllerEffect`, `EnchantedCreatureDealsDamageToEachOpponentEffect`, `EnchantedCreatureDealsPowerDamageToAnyTargetEffect`, `GainLifeEffect`, `SacrificeSelfEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkEnchantedCreatureDealtDamageTriggers (noncombat via checkDealtDamageToCreatureTriggers from DamageSupport#finishPermanentDamage; combat from CombatDamageService per damage portion): each registration dispatched to DamageTriggerCollectorService effect-class handlers or the CardEffect default, one enqueueTrigger entry each (damage as eventValue). No lost-ability check on the attachment.
- **`ON_ENCHANTED_PLAYER_DEALT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkEnchantedPlayerDealtDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkEnchantedPlayerDealtDamageTriggers -> DamageTriggerCollectorService CardEffect default: one enqueueTrigger per registration (damaged player targetId, amount eventValue). Doc says once per damage event; the combat reader aggregates all combat damage to the defender in the step.
- **`ON_ENCHANTED_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_YOU`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkEnchantedCreatureDealtDamageToControllerReflectTriggers` (queue?). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkEnchantedCreatureDealtDamageToControllerReflectTriggers (DamageSupport noncombat, CombatDamageService combat) scans the damaged player's battlefield for attachments on the source: all registrations in ONE non-targeting entry (amount as xValue, creature's controller as targetId); no lost-ability/face-down checks.
- **`ON_ENCHANTED_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectEnchantedCreatureDealsDamageTriggers` (all). Aware: `DamageSourceControllerAwareEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectEnchantedCreatureDealsDamageTriggers (combat, from CombatDamageService) / queueEnchantedCreatureDealsDamageTriggers (DamageSupport): an attachment's registrations share ONE entry (DamageSourceController* effects bound, damage as eventValue); no lost-ability check.
- **`ON_ENCHANTED_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectEnchantedCreatureDealsDamageToCreatureTriggers` (all). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectEnchantedCreatureDealsDamageToCreatureTriggers (combat) / queueEnchantedCreatureDealsDamageToCreatureTriggers (DamageSupport noncombat): all registrations in ONE non-targeting entry with the damaged creature as targetId; source must currently be a creature; self-damage excluded.
- **`ON_DEALT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDealtDamageToCreatureTriggers` (read); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 12 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `DamageSourceControllerExilesRandomHandCardEffect`, `DamageSourceControllerGainsControlOfDamagedPermanentEffect`, `DamageSourceControllerGetsPoisonCounterEffect`, `DamageSourceControllerLosesLifeUnlessSacrificesPermanentsEffect`, `DamageSourceControllerMillsEffect`, `DamageSourceControllerSacrificesPermanentsEffect`, `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect`, `DealDamageToDamageSourceCreatureOrSpellControllerEffect`, `DealDamageToTargetPlayerOrPlaneswalkerEffect`, `MayEffect`; `SpecializeKlementEffectHandler`: `resolve` (read); `CombatDamageService`: `collectDealtDamageTriggerData` (read). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 6, `SequenceEffect` 5, `ConditionalEffect` 3. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1, `PerDamageSourceTriggerEffect` 1. Aware: `DamageSourceControllerAwareEffect` 11, `DamageSourceAwareEffect` 1. Choke points: `SpecializeKlementEffectHandler#resolve` ← `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#doResolveManaAbility`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#performSpecialAction`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#pushAbilityOnStack`, `AnimationSupport#fireTransformTriggers`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeCreatureOrTransformSourceEffectHandler#finish`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeNontokenCreatureTapAndSeekLandSourceEffectHandler#tapThenSeek`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#dealDamage`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#sacrificeSource`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceHandler#handle`, `AnyOpponentMayTapCreatureTapAndCreateTokenSourceEffectHandler#tapAndCreateToken`, `AnyPlayerMaySacrificeLandPutSourceOnTopEffectHandler#putSourceOnTop`, `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrBoostSelfEffectHandler#boostSelf`, +193 more. Notes: readers disagree in mechanics: noncombat TriggerCollectionService#checkDealtDamageToCreatureTriggers (DamageSupport#finishPermanentDamage, any permanent) dispatches through DamageTriggerCollectorService handlers; combat CombatDamageService#collectDealtDamageTriggerData/processDealtDamageTriggers is bespoke and covers creature recipients only. Both: one stack object/interaction per registration; face-down / lost-printed-abilities skip printed effects.
- **`ON_NONCOMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_SELF`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkNoncombatDamageToSelfTriggers` (read); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkNoncombatDamageToSelfTriggers (DamageSupport#finishPermanentDamage) -> handleNoncombatDamageToSelfDefault, which is the ON_DEALT_DAMAGE default handler (one entry per registration, amount as eventValue). Draft family 13.3 was wrong.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_DEALT_NONCOMBAT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkNoncombatDamageToOpponentTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 4 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `BoostAllOwnCreaturesEffect`, `BoostSelfEffect`, `DealDamageToTargetCreatureOrPlaneswalkerEffect`, `DrawCardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkNoncombatDamageToOpponentTriggers (DamageSupport) dispatches each registration to MiscTriggerCollectorService handlers (BoostSelf, BoostAllOwnCreatures, DrawCard, DealDamageToTargetCreatureOrPlaneswalker); no default handler; relation depends on the effect class; no lost-ability/face-down check. Draft family 13.3 was wrong.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_DEALT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentCreatureDealtDamageTriggers` (each), `checkTemporaryGlobalOpponentCreatureDealtDamageTriggers` (read). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentCreatureDealtDamageTriggers(gameData, damagedControllerId) is passed only the controller, not the creature: printed effects only, direct stack.add with no targetId, no lost-abilities check, no targeting support. Called from DamageSupport (noncombat, gated by isCreature) and CombatDamageService (~907-916, once per entry of defDamageTaken/atkDamageTaken). Temporary-global variant TriggerCollectionService#checkTemporaryGlobalOpponentCreatureDealtDamageTriggers sets targetId = damaged creature (Green Dragon registers via RegisterGlobalTriggeredAbilityUntilEndOfTurnEffect, so the slot doubles as a temporary-watcher key).
- **`ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_OR_PLANESWALKER_DEALT_EXCESS_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentPermanentDealtExcessDamageTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentPermanentDealtExcessDamageTriggers (shared with the _NONCOMBAT_ slot); callers DamageSupport (noncombat, per damage application) and CombatDamageService#checkCombatExcessDamageTriggers (before SBA). Printed + temporary + persistent effects; intervening-if ConditionalEffect checked with targetId = damaged permanent; DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect -> queueInteraction SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget (target other than the damaged permanent), else stack.add nonTargeting with eventValue = excess. Planeswalker test is printed type. No lost-abilities check.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_OR_PLANESWALKER_DEALT_EXCESS_NONCOMBAT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentPermanentDealtExcessDamageTriggers` (each). Notes: Same reader as ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_OR_PLANESWALKER_DEALT_EXCESS_DAMAGE; the slot is only included when noncombatDamage is true (DamageSupport passes !event.combat(); CombatDamageService passes false). Draft family 13.3 is wrong: this is noncombat damage (13.4). DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect (Toralf) goes through queueInteraction, others stack.add.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_DEALT_EXCESS_NONCOMBAT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentCreatureDealtExcessNoncombatDamageTriggers` (each). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentCreatureDealtExcessNoncombatDamageTriggers, called only from DamageSupport permanent-damage path. Code fires once per source stack entry (matches Magmatic Galleon's 'one or more'); Javadoc reads as per creature. Printed + temporary + persistent; direct stack.add nonTargeting with targetId = first damaged creature; no lost-abilities check. Draft family 13.3 is wrong (noncombat, 13.4).
- **`ON_ANY_CREATURE_DEALT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyCreatureDealtDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 4 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `EnchantedCreatureDealsDamageEqualToDealtDamageToControllerEffect`, `MayEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 3, `MayEffect` 2. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyCreatureDealtDamageTriggers -> dispatch per registration to DamageTriggerCollectorService handlers (EnchantedCreatureDealsDamageEqualToDealtDamageToController -> stack.add; MayEffect / TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect / default -> enqueueAnyCreatureDealtDamage: targeted MayEffect -> queueInteraction SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget, else stack.add with targetId = damaged creature, eventValue = damage). Skips only hasLostAllAbilities. Also collectTemporaryGlobalTriggers and DelayedWatchedCreatureDealtDamage. Callers: DamageSupport (noncombat, isCreature-gated) and CombatDamageService#processAnyCreatureDealtDamageTriggers. Minor: the permanent-conditional handler computes the once-per-turn unwrap but enqueues the wrapped effect.
- **`ON_ANY_PERMANENT_DEALT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyPermanentDealtDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 3. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPermanentDealtDamageTriggers -> dispatchSlot (lost-abilities gate, printed + granted, one dispatch per registration). Only two handlers: DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect and TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect (no CardEffect default, so other effect classes silently never trigger). Both queueInteraction SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget. SURPRISE: Javadoc says 'regardless of its controller' but queueAnyPermanentDealtDamageTrigger requires the damaged permanent's controller == watcher's controller (the DealDamageToPlayersEffect paired branch skips that check). Matches the registered cards (Howlpack Avenger, Wrathful Raptors), so it is the name/doc that is off. Callers: DamageSupport (noncombat) and several CombatDamageService paths (~4358, 4617, 4624, 4854, 5422).
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `fireAnySourceDealtDamageToCreatureTriggers` (read), `fireAllyDealtDamageToCreatureTrigger` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 7 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CreateTokenEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `ExileDamagedCreatureEffect`, `MayEffect`, `PutCountersOnSourceEffect`, `SacrificePermanentsEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1, `MayPayManaEffect` 1, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyDealtDamageToCreatureTriggers = #checkWatchersDealtDamageToCreatureTriggers (other permanents of the source's controller, equipment on the source even if an opponent controls it, and any-controller watchers via #fireAnySourceDealtDamageToCreatureTriggers) + #checkSelfDealtDamageToCreatureTriggers (the source itself, incl. granted). Core: #fireAllyDealtDamageToCreatureTrigger tries registry.dispatch first (MayEffect, TriggeringPermanentConditional, PutCountersOnSource, CreateToken, SacrificePermanents, ExileDamagedCreature, DealDamageToPlayers handlers), then a long instanceof chain (ReflectAllyDamage..., DamageDamagedCreatureControllerAndSelf, DamagedCreatureTriggerEffect, TapAndSkipUntap, EquipmentTapsAndLocks, DestroyDamagedCreatureAtEndOfCombat, EquipmentDamagesOtherDefendingCreatures). Self/ally/equipped is decided per effect class (watcher==source checks). SURPRISE: the name says ally but most registrations are 'this creature deals damage to a creature'; no isCreature check on the damage source; the MayEffect handler only fires for combat damage (queueMayAbility). Mixed stack.add / enqueueTrigger / queueMayAbility. Callers: DamageSupport (noncombat) and CombatDamageService watcher/self reflection passes before SBA.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_OPPONENT_DAMAGED_BY_RED_SPELL_OR_PLANESWALKER`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkRedSpellOrPlaneswalkerDamageToOpponentTriggers` (each). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkRedSpellOrPlaneswalkerDamageToOpponentTriggers, from DamageSupport noncombat player-damage path (~1679) only. Entry must be INSTANT_SPELL/SORCERY_SPELL or have a planeswalker source permanent; color from effective colors or LKI. Plain stack.add per registration, no graveyard-version binding. Combat damage from a red planeswalker creature never reaches this reader.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_ALLY_INSTANT_OR_SORCERY_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT_OR_BATTLE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkInstantOrSorceryDamageToOpponentOrBattleTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkInstantOrSorceryDamageToOpponentOrBattleTriggers, called getInstantOrSorceryDamageRecipientCount() times from DamageSupport#flushSourceDamageReflections (end of resolution; also DamageSupport ~1755/1915, EffectResolutionService ~609). Recipients recorded in DamageSupport#accumulateSourceDamageForReflection (player != caster, or printed BATTLE). enqueueTrigger per registration; MayPayManaEffect goes on the stack unwrapped (resolved later); no graveyard-version binding.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_ALLY_SOURCE_DEALS_NONCOMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkNoncombatDamageToOpponentTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkNoncombatDamageToOpponentTriggers (graveyard tail), from DamageSupport noncombat player-damage path (~1677). Reads the source controller's graveyard; stack.add per registration, no graveyard-version binding, MayPayManaEffect not unwrapped. Draft family 13.3 is wrong (noncombat, 13.4).
- **`ON_ANY_SOURCE_DEALS_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `queueSourceDealsDamageReflections` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 7 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `GainLifeEffect`, `GhyrsonStarnKelermorphEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `OtherChosenPlayerLosesLifeEffect`, `PutCountersOnSourceCardForColorSourceDamageEffect`, `ReflectSourceDamageToItsControllerEffect`, `SourceDamageGainLifeForSourceControllerEffect`. Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#queueSourceDealsDamageReflections (watcher scan over all battlefields, skipping hasLostPrintedAbilities) -> dispatch per registration. Choke points: CombatDamageService#processSourceDealsDamageReflectionTriggers (per source per step, before SBA, entries held and re-added after SBA) and DamageSupport#flushSourceDamageReflections (end of resolution: EffectResolutionService ~609, DamageSupport ~1755/1915). Only effect-specific handlers (ReflectSourceDamageToItsController -> stack.add; SourceDamageGainLifeForSourceController, OtherChosenPlayerLosesLife, GainLife (noncreature source you control), PutCountersOnSourceCardForColorSourceDamage, GhyrsonStarnKelermorph (other source you control) -> enqueueTrigger; MayPayManaEffect (chosen color, damage to you) -> queueInteraction). No CardEffect default handler, so other effect classes never trigger. Relation, self and multiplicity all depend on the handler.
- **`ON_ALLY_INSTANT_OR_SORCERY_DEALS_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyInstantOrSorcerySpellDealsDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 4 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `DealDamageToEachOpponentWhenSingleTargetCreatureSpellDealsDamageEffect`, `DealDamageToTargetCreatureDamagedPlayerControlsEffect`, `ExileTopCardMayPlayThisTurnWhenInstantOrSorceryDealsDamageToPlayerEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyInstantOrSorcerySpellDealsDamageTriggers, called from DamageSupport#flushSourceDamageReflections only for batches keyed by a spell stack entry (INSTANT_SPELL/SORCERY_SPELL, see DamageSupport#accumulateSourceDamageForReflection); noncombat only. Scans the caster's battlefield, skips hasLostPrintedAbilities, dispatch per registration to DamageTriggerCollectorService handlers (DealDamageToTargetCreatureDamagedPlayerControls, DealDamageToEachOpponentWhenSingleTargetCreatureSpellDealsDamage, ExileTopCardMayPlay..., default). Javadoc says it shares queueSourceDealsDamageReflections; it is actually a sibling call from the same flush.
- **`ON_SELF_DEALS_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `queueSourceDealsDamageReflections` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`; `DamageSupport`: `snapshotSelfDealsDamageEffects` (read); `CombatDamageService`: `snapshotSelfDealsDamageEffects` (read). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 4, `SequenceEffect` 2, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: Self section of TriggerCollectionService#queueSourceDealsDamageReflections, using effects snapshotted at damage time by CombatDamageService#snapshotSelfDealsDamageEffects (before SBA) or DamageSupport#snapshotSelfDealsDamageEffects (first accumulation; returns empty if the source already left), so it still fires when the source died. Printed effects are gated by face-down/hasLostPrintedAbilities, plus temporary/persistent/granted. Handler DamageTriggerCollectorService#handleSelfDealsDamage: optional TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect on damaged-permanent snapshots, intervening-if with eventValue; targeted -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget, else enqueueTrigger with eventValue = total. Same choke points as ON_ANY_SOURCE_DEALS_DAMAGE.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_PLANESWALKER`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyDealtDamageToPlaneswalkerTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 3. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyDealtDamageToPlaneswalkerTriggers from DamageSupport (~285, noncombat) and CombatDamageService#processAllyDealtDamageToPlaneswalkerTriggers (combat; entries deferred to state.allyCreatureDealsDamageToPlaneswalkerTriggers and added after SBA). Printed + temporary + persistent; calls registry.dispatch directly (bypassing TriggerCollectionService#dispatch, so no additional-trigger copy counting). Only handler: TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect (with combatDamageOnly flag), so bare effects never trigger. No lost-abilities gate.
- **`ON_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_ENCHANTED_PLANESWALKER`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCreatureDealsDamageToEnchantedPlaneswalkerTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkCreatureDealsDamageToEnchantedPlaneswalkerTriggers, invoked at the start of #checkAllyDealtDamageToPlaneswalkerTriggers (same choke points). Printed + temporary + persistent + granted; registry.dispatch directly (no copy counting) -> default handler DamageTriggerCollectorService#handleCreatureDealsDamageToEnchantedPlaneswalker: targetId = damaging creature, nonTargeting, deferred list in combat. No lost-abilities gate.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_DEALT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerDealtDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 4 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `MayEffect`, `RemoveCounterFromSourceEffect`, `SacrificePermanentsOrLoseGameEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 3. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerDealtDamageTriggers -> dispatch per registration to default/MayEffect/SacrificePermanentsOrLoseGame/RemoveCounterFromSource handlers (all enqueueTrigger with eventValue = amount). Scans the damaged player's battlefield; printed only, no lost-abilities gate. Callers: DamageSupport (~1638, noncombat) and CombatDamageService (~972, includeAllSources=true with the total across all sources; the per-controller follow-up calls pass includeAllSources=false and only feed the _BY_OPPONENT/_BY_ALLY_SOURCE slots). SURPRISE: the Javadoc says simultaneous sources trigger once each, but combat sums them into one trigger. Rulings for similar 'is dealt damage' triggers favour one trigger, so the doc looks stale; worth confirming.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_DEALT_DAMAGE_BY_ALLY_SOURCE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerDealtDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerDealtDamageTriggers (fromAllySource gate: source controller == damaged player), called from DamageSupport (noncombat player damage) and CombatDamageService. SURPRISE: in combat, when the source controller is the recipient, CombatDamageService passes the summed damage of all that player's sources in one call, so it fires once per step instead of once per source (the BY_OPPONENT twin fires per source). OncePerTurnTriggerEffect unwrapped and marked in the collector. Only handler: the default DamageTriggerCollectorService#handleControllerDealtDamageByAllySource. A player-targeting effect gets queueInteraction(SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget) carrying the amount (Daredevil). Otherwise enqueueTrigger with eventValue=amount. No ability-loss check.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_DAMAGE_PREVENTED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerDamagePreventedTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerDamagePreventedTriggers via DamagePreventionService#notifyControllerDamagePrevented, which is called from the blanket-flag/shield paths of applyPlayerPreventionShield, applyPlayerDamagePreventionShield and PlayerDamagePreventions#playerFlag. Default handler handleControllerDamagePreventedDefault: enqueueTrigger with eventValue=amount prevented. SURPRISE: not every prevention path notifies. DamagePreventionService#applyHalfDamagePrevention (Gisela) and applyControllerAndCreaturesFixedPerSourceDamagePrevention prevent damage silently, so Selfless Squire misses them.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_DEALT_DAMAGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentDealtDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentDealtDamageTriggers scans every other battlefield. Called from DamageSupport (noncombat) and CombatDamageService (per source in the combat-damage-to-player loop, and per redirected portion). SURPRISE: calls registry.dispatch directly, bypassing the private dispatch wrapper, so additional-trigger copy effects are never applied. No ability-loss check. Handlers: PutCountersOnSelfEffect (War Elemental, eventValue). ConditionalEffect: intervening-if evaluated with the damage amount (Pain Magnification 3+). Default: targetId=damaged player, nonTargeting, eventValue.
- **`ON_ALLY_SOURCE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllySourceDealtDamageToOpponentTriggers` (each), `checkPlanarAllySourceDealtDamageToOpponentTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 6 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `DrawCardIfEventValueAtLeastEffect`, `MayEffect`, `PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnPerCreatureTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllySourceDealtDamageToOpponentTriggers (skips permanents that lost printed abilities). Called from CombatDamageService (per source) and DamageSupport (noncombat). Planes go through checkPlanarAllySourceDealtDamageToOpponentTriggers: the planar controller must control the source, which must be a creature, and dispatchPlanarDamageTrigger unwraps OncePerTurnPerCreatureTriggerEffect. SURPRISE: self semantics depend on the effect class. handleSelfDealsDamageToOpponent (DealDamageToPlayersEffect, Flesh Reaver) fires only when the watcher is the source. TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect and PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect need a creature source. PutCountersOnSelfEffect/MayEffect/DrawCardIfEventValueAtLeastEffect fire for any source, except that excludeDamageSource drops the watcher itself (Talon of Pain). There is no default CardEffect handler, so other effect classes silently never trigger. Everything is enqueueTrigger.
- **`ON_ALLY_SOURCE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllySourceDealtDamageToOpponentTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `DrawCardIfEventValueAtLeastEffect`, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: Read in the same loop as ON_ALLY_SOURCE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT (TriggerCollectionService#checkAllySourceDealtDamageToOpponentTriggers), but without the 'damaged player != controller' gate. No planar reader. Handlers: TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect (needs a source permanent, no creature check, sets triggeringPermanentId and eventValue; Vraska) and DrawCardIfEventValueAtLeastEffect. No default handler. SURPRISE: Dragonborn Champion's '5 or more damage' threshold is only checked when DrawCardIfEventValueAtLeastEffectHandler resolves, so the ability triggers and uses the stack for any amount.
- **`ON_SOURCE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_ITS_CONTROLLER_PLANAR`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkPlanarSourceDealtDamageToItsControllerTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect`. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnPerCreatureTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: Read only by TriggerCollectionService#checkPlanarSourceDealtDamageToItsControllerTriggers, called from checkAllySourceDealtDamageToOpponentTriggers when source controller == damaged player. Dispatched under the planar controller. dispatchPlanarDamageTrigger unwraps OncePerTurnPerCreatureTriggerEffect per creature (registry.dispatch, no copy wrapper). Only handler: PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect (TRIGGERING), shared with the planar ON_ALLY_SOURCE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT path. SURPRISE: Stensia is split across three slots, and in multiplayer none of them covers a non-planar-controller's creature damaging a third player.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURES_DEAL_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyCreaturesDealDamageToPlayerTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 2. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreaturesDealDamageToPlayerTriggers, called from CombatDamageService#processCombatDamageToPlayerTriggers (batch of dealers) and from DamageSupport (noncombat, only when the source is a creature). Reads printed + temporary + persistent + granted effects with no ability-loss check. Only handler: TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect; dealers must be creatures even when a predicate is given (unlike the OPPONENT twin), and bare effects never trigger. targetId=damaged player, nonTargeting. SURPRISE: watchers are read live after combat state-based actions, with no look-back snapshot (unlike ON_ALLY_CREATURES_DEAL_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT), so a watcher killed by the same combat damage misses its trigger.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURES_DEAL_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `snapshotAllyCreaturesDealDamageToOpponentTriggers` (read), `checkAllyCreaturesDealDamageToOpponentTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 2. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreaturesDealDamageToOpponentTriggers runs over watchers from snapshotAllyCreaturesDealDamageToOpponentTriggers, which CombatDamageService takes before state-based actions (look-back). Watchers include printed (skipped if printed abilities lost) + temporary + persistent + granted effects. Called from CombatDamageService#processCombatDamageToPlayerTriggers and DamageSupport, where the dealer is the source permanent or a spell snapshot. Only handler: handleAllyCreaturesDealDamageToOpponent (TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect); an unfiltered trigger requires creature dealers. targetId=damaged player, nonTargeting.
- **`ON_ALLY_SOURCE_DEALS_NONCOMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_OPPONENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllySourceDealtNoncombatDamageToOpponentTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `ConditionalEffect`, `DealDamageToTargetCreatureDamagedPlayerControlsEffect`; `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 4 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CreateTokenEffect`, `MayEffect`, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect`, `SequenceEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 2, `MayEffect` 2, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: The draft's 13.3 is wrong: the slot is noncombat only. TriggerCollectionService#checkAllySourceDealtNoncombatDamageToOpponentTriggers, called only from DamageSupport (noncombat player damage). The path depends on the effect class. MayEffect: queueMayAbility immediately with the amount (Virtue of Courage, Thor). OncePerTurnTriggerEffect (inner ConditionalEffect checked at trigger time; Molten Lavamancer), CreateTokenEffect and SequenceEffect: enqueueNoncombatDamageTrigger (MiscTriggerCollectorService). DealDamageToTargetCreatureDamagedPlayerControlsEffect, alone or wrapped in a ConditionalEffect (DamageTriggerCollectorService): intervening-if checked with the amount, target limited to the damaged player's creatures. A ConditionalEffect wrapping any other class returns false. No default handler and no ability-loss check.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_DEALT_DAMAGE_BY_OPPONENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerDealtDamageTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 4 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `MayEffect`, `SacrificePermanentsEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 2, `ConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerDealtDamageTriggers (fromOpponent gate). The first combat call passes a null source controller and skips this slot; the per-controller calls then fire it once per source amount. Handlers: SacrificePermanentsEffect works only with a TARGET_PLAYER recipient (bound to the source's controller, Michiko); any other recipient returns false with no fallback. ConditionalEffect (intervening-if), MayEffect and default: enqueueTrigger with eventValue=amount. No ability-loss check.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_SOURCE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_YOU_OR_YOUR_PERMANENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentSourceDamageToYouOrYourPermanentTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `LoseLifeUnlessPaysEffect`, `MayEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentSourceDamageToYouOrYourPermanentTriggers, called from DamageSupport (noncombat batch recipients) and CombatDamageService#processOpponentSourceDamageToYouOrYourPermanentTriggers. The opponent gate lives in the handlers. LoseLifeUnlessPaysEffect: enqueue with targetId=source controller (Elesh Norn). SURPRISE: the MayEffect handler (handleOpponentSourceDamageToYouOrYourPermanent) ignores the wrapped effect and always queueMayAbility's a hard-coded DealDamageToDamageSourceControllerEffect(1,...) (Flameblade Angel). No default handler.
- **`ON_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_YOU_OR_YOUR_PERMANENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCreatureDamageToYouOrYourPermanentTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `ReflectDamageToChosenColorCreatureEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkCreatureDamageToYouOrYourPermanentTriggers, called from the four damage choke points: DamageSupport player and creature paths, CombatDamageService player and creature paths. Only handler: ReflectDamageToChosenColorCreatureEffect (handleChosenColorCreatureDamageReflection). It gates on the watcher's chosenColor and the damagedPermanentFilter, then rewrites the effect into DealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect(damage) with targetId=damaging creature, nonTargeting, via direct stack.add (not enqueueTrigger). No ability-loss check.
- **`ON_ALLY_SOURCE_DEALS_NONCOMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllySourceDealtNoncombatDamageToCreatureTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `ConditionalEffect`, `DrawCardEffect`, `PerpetuallyBoostCreatureCardInHandEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: The draft's 13.3 is wrong: the slot is noncombat only. TriggerCollectionService#checkAllySourceDealtNoncombatDamageToCreatureTriggers, called from DamageSupport's noncombat permanent-damage path (handlers drop noncreature recipients). Handlers: ConditionalEffect (intervening-if at trigger, Crude Abattoir door) and PerpetuallyBoostCreatureCardInHandEffect enqueue nonTargeting with a source snapshot. SURPRISE: the DrawCardEffect handler hard-codes Taii Wakeen's 'damage equal to that creature's toughness' check, so any DrawCardEffect in this slot inherits it. No default handler.

- **`ON_SELF_TIME_COUNTER_REMOVED_FROM_EXILE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkTimeCounterRemovedFromExiledCardTriggers` (each); `ExiledCardTriggerCollectorService`: `handleTimeCounterRemoved` (all), 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `YouRemoveTimeCounterFromExiledCardTriggerEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 2, `MayEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `YouRemoveTimeCounterFromExiledCardTriggerEffect` 1. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkTimeCounterRemovedFromExiledCardTriggers dispatches each registration with sourceCard. Called from RemoveTimeCounterFromExiledCardEffectHandler#removeTimeCounter and RemoveSuspendCounterFromExiledSpellEffectHandler. The ExiledCardTriggerCollectorService default handler re-reads the slot and queues every registration as one entry, but only on the first registration's dispatch (later ones return true without queuing); a targeted effect goes to SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget. ConditionalEffect(NotCondition(SourceCardSuspended)) is the 'last counter' gate (remaining==0); YouRemoveTimeCounterFromExiledCardTriggerEffect gates on who removed it. SURPRISE: RemoveSuspendCounterFromExiledSpellEffectHandler only calls the check when the last counter comes off, so suspendedSpellExiles cards miss intermediate 'a time counter is removed' triggers. The bundling also needs the first registration to pass its own gate: a failing first conditional suppresses the rest, and later last-counter conditionals are not re-checked.
- **`ON_SELF_BECOMES_RENOWNED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkBecomesRenownedTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkBecomesRenownedTriggers, called from RenownEffectHandler when renown flips. One enqueueTrigger per registration; no ability-loss check and no target handling.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_RENOWNED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkBecomesRenownedTriggers` (each). Notes: Same TriggerCollectionService#checkBecomesRenownedTriggers (forEachPermanent filtered to the renowned creature's controller). TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect filters the renowned creature. One enqueueTrigger per registration; no ability-loss check, no target handling, and the triggering permanent id is not stamped.
- **`ON_SELF_BECOMES_MONSTROUS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkBecomesMonstrousTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: `handleSelfBecomesMonstrous` (all), 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. 3 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkBecomesMonstrousTriggers, called from MonstrosityEffectHandler. It uses registry.dispatch directly, so there is no additional-trigger copy wrapper and no ability-loss check. Only handler: the default MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleSelfBecomesMonstrous. It re-reads the slot and queues everything once, on the first registration's dispatch (later ones return true without queuing). Targeted -> ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger (multi-target) or SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget; else direct stack.add carrying X.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_GETS_ENERGY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkEnergyGainTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `BoostSelfEffect`, `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkEnergyGainTriggers via dispatchSlot. Called from EnergyCountersEffectHandler, DoubleCountersOnControllerEffectHandler, AddAnotherCounterOfEachKindToTargetEffectHandler, ChoiceHandlerService and MultiPermanentChoiceHandlerService. MiscTriggerCollectorService: BoostSelfEffect, and a default handler that checks intervening-if, sends targeted effects to SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget and otherwise enqueues.
- **`ON_YOU_PUT_COUNTERS_ON_CREATURE_YOU_DONT_CONTROL`**. Readers: `PermanentCounterSupport`: `fireCountersPutOnCreatureYouDontControlTriggers` (all). 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: PermanentCounterSupport#fireCountersPutOnCreatureYouDontControlTriggers, from both PermanentCounterSupport#notifyCountersPlaced overloads; watchers on the placer's battlefield; all slot effects in one stack.add entry (Kros's 2 registrations = one ability), nonTargeting, eventValue=count, triggeringPermanentId/controller set; skips sources that lost abilities.
- **`ON_YOU_PUT_COUNTERS_ON_CREATURE`**. Readers: `PermanentCounterSupport`: `fireYouPutCountersOnCreatureTriggers` (all). Notes: PermanentCounterSupport#fireYouPutCountersOnCreatureTriggers (private), from both PermanentCounterSupport#notifyCountersPlaced overloads (counter-type overload only when triggerCollectionService is wired); one stack.add entry per watcher with all effects, nonTargeting, eventValue=count, triggeringPermanentId set; skips sources that lost abilities.
- **`ON_SELF_PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE_COUNTERS_PUT`**. Readers: `PermanentCounterSupport`: `firePlusOnePlusOneCountersPutOnSelfTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 4, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 3, `YouPutCounterOnControlledCreatureTriggerEffect` 2. Modes: `PER_COUNTER` 2. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: PermanentCounterSupport#firePlusOnePlusOneCountersPutOnSelfTriggers (via PermanentCounterSupport#firePlusOnePlusOneCounterTriggers, RenownEffectHandler). PER_COUNTER registrations: one stack object per registration per counter (SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget interaction if targeted); all other registrations share one object (interaction if any declares a target, else stack.add); YouPutCounterOnControlledCreatureTriggerEffect gates placer==controller; OncePerTurn unwrapped; hasLostPrintedAbilities check. SURPRISE: PER_COUNTER uses the caller's count, but most callers (PutCountersOnSourceEffectHandler, PutCounterOnEach*Handler, DoublePlusOneCounters*Handler, the short firePlusOnePlusOneCounterTriggers overloads) pass 1, so a Fathom Mage-style per-counter trigger fires once for N counters there.
- **`ON_SELF_TRAINS`**. Readers: `TrainEffectHandler`: `resolve` (queue?); `PermanentRemovalService`: `handleExileReturnOnLeave` (all). ## Step 13.6: becomes the target

| Constant | Kind | Event | Object | Relation | Zone | Mult. | Self | Grouping | Cards |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `ON_CONTROLLER_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL` | triggered | you become the target of a spell | spell (any controller, including yours) | you | battlefield | per targeting spell (targeted players deduplicated per spell) | n/a | each | 1 |

| `ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` | triggered | becomes the target of a spell or ability | spell or ability (any controller; TriggeringSpellController/OpponentController conditionals filter) | this; or the enchanted/equipped permanent when the source is an attached Aura/Equipment (a bestowed permanent: itself) | battlefield | per targeting object per targeted permanent | heuristic | all | 52 |
| `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` | triggered | becomes the target of a spell or ability | creature | you control; source is an opponent's spell or ability | battlefield | per object (per targeted creature) | yes | split | 5 |
| `ON_ANOTHER_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` | triggered | becomes the target of a spell or ability | creature | another you control; any spell/ability controller | battlefield | per object (per targeted creature) | no | all | 1 |
| `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_ACTIVATED_ABILITY` | triggered | one or more become the target of an activated ability | creature | you control; any ability controller | battlefield | per event (once per ability per watcher, however many creatures it targets) | yes | each | 1 |
| `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL` | triggered | becomes the target of a spell | creature | you control; source is an opponent's spell | battlefield | per object (per targeted creature) | yes | all | 1 |
| `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_OR_CREATURE_SPELL_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` | triggered | becomes the target of a spell or ability | creature or creature spell | you control; source is an opponent's spell or ability | battlefield | per object (per targeted creature or creature spell) | yes | all | 1 |

| `ON_ALLY_PERMANENT_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` | triggered | becomes the target of a spell or ability | permanent | you control; source is an opponent's spell or ability | battlefield | per object (per targeted permanent) | yes | all | 2 |
| `ON_ANOTHER_ALLY_PERMANENT_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` | triggered | becomes the target of a spell or ability | permanent | another you control; source is an opponent's spell or ability | battlefield | per object (per targeted permanent) | no | split | 2 |
| `ON_ANY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` | triggered | becomes the target of a spell or ability | creature | any; any spell/ability controller | battlefield | per object (per targeted creature) | yes | all | 4 |
| `ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_YOUR_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` | triggered | becomes the target of a spell or ability | creature | opponent controls; source is your spell or ability | battlefield | per object (per targeted creature) | no | all | 2 |
