| `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_INSTANT_OR_SORCERY` | triggered | becomes the target of an instant or sorcery spell (any spell controller; spell path only) | creature | you control (incl. this) | battlefield | per targeted creature | yes | all | 2 |

- **`ON_ANY_PLAYER_CHOOSES_TARGETS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkTargetChoiceTriggers` (queue?). Trigger effects: `AbilityTargetChoiceTriggerEffect` 1, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkTargetChoiceTriggers loops over every permanent. It runs after becomes-target collection on the spell and ability paths, and also from StackedDividedDamageTriggerService, GraveyardChoiceHandlerService and MultiPermanentChoiceHandlerService. Effect-class filters: TargetChoiceTriggerEffect (Loki, with OncePerTurn unwrap/mark); ReselectTargetAtRandomEffect (Grip of Chaos, single-target entries only); others unfiltered (Psychic Battle). enqueueTrigger with triggeringCardId=the targeting object. Ability loss is checked only for the first two classes. The trigger is about the targeting object, not about becoming a target (could arguably be 13.2).
- **`ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectBecomesTargetTriggers` (all). Wrappers: `TriggeringSpellControllerConditionalEffect` 2, `SequenceEffect` 1. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: self: the source is the object unless it is attached, in which case the object is the permanent it is attached to. TriggerCollectionService#collectBecomesTargetTriggers, called from collectBecomesTargetOfSpellTriggers (spell path only). It runs for the unattached targeted permanent and for every Aura/Equipment attached to it; an attached Aura that is itself the target does not trigger, and bestow is not special-cased. Skips cloaked permanents and those that lost printed abilities; granted effects are read only when the source is the target. Split into stack objects: non-targeting effects in one entry; TriggeringSpellReferencingEffect ones in another (triggering card/controller set); targeting ones -> SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget interaction. All use direct stack.add (enqueueTargetTriggeredAbility).
- **`ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_AURA_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectBecomesTargetOfAuraSpellTriggers` (all). Notes: self: the source is the object unless it is an attached Aura (then the enchanted creature). TriggerCollectionService#collectBecomesTargetOfAuraSpellTriggers, spell path only, with the same unattached-self + attached-permanents call pattern as ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL. All registrations go in one entry via direct stack.add. SURPRISE: only isCloaked is checked; unlike its siblings there is no hasLostPrintedAbilities check and no granted triggers.
- **`ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectBecomesTargetOfOpponentCounterTriggers` (each), `collectBecomesTargetOfOpponentSpellOrAbilityNonCounterTriggers` (all), `hasWardAbility` (read), `hasGrantedCounterUnlessTrigger` (read); `PutRandomCreatureFromTargetOpponentLibraryOntoBattlefieldEffectHandler`: `rememberPerpetualWard` (read). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 11, `MayEffect` 3. Notes: This is where the Ward keyword's counter-unless lives (CounterUnless* = about 156 of 182 registrations, more inside ConditionalEffect). SURPRISE: despite the name it fires for abilities too. collectBecomesTargetOfOpponentCounterTriggers and collectBecomesTargetOfOpponentSpellOrAbilityNonCounterTriggers run from both the spell and ability paths; only a non-ward CounterUnlessDiscardsEffect is dropped for abilities (Reality Smasher). Split: each CounterUnlessEffect (after intervening-if) is its own nonTargeting entry targeting the triggering object in the STACK zone. All non-counter effects share one entry (targeted -> AttackTriggerTarget interaction), and that path runs only for the unattached targeted permanent. The counter collector also handles ward suppression/removal, the rule that printed ward needs the source to be the target, granted/perpetual ward, the cloaked/face-down ward placeholder and temporary/persistent grants. hasWardAbility and hasGrantedCounterUnlessTrigger are presence checks; rememberPerpetualWard writes perpetual grants. All direct stack.add.
- **`ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_ONLY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectBecomesTargetOfOpponentSpellOnlyTriggers` (each). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectBecomesTargetOfOpponentSpellOnlyTriggers (spell path only, unattached targeted permanent). Printed registrations go in ONE entry; each granted effect gets its own entry (Opaline Sliver's grant). Direct stack.add with no target handling. SURPRISE: no ability-loss check, so printed effects fire even after the permanent lost its abilities.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectControllerBecomesTargetOfSpellTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectControllerBecomesTargetOfSpellTriggers (spell path only). Printed (unless printed abilities lost) + granted. MayEffect -> queueMayAbility; else direct stack.add.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectControllerBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers` (each). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectControllerBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers, from the spell and ability paths. SURPRISE: only CounterUnlessEffect registrations are queued; any other effect class is silently ignored. The entry targets the triggering object in the STACK zone, sets setTriggerOrderChosen(true) and uses direct stack.add; the log text hard-codes '{1}'. Only isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn is checked.
- **`ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectBecomesTargetOfSpellOrAbilityTriggers` (all). Wrappers: `TriggeringSpellControllerConditionalEffect` 11, `MayEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 11. Notes: self: the source is the object unless it is a non-bestow attached Aura/Equipment (then the attached-to permanent). TriggerCollectionService#collectBecomesTargetOfSpellOrAbilityTriggers, from the spell and ability paths. Reads printed (unless cloaked or printed abilities lost) + temporary + persistent + granted effects, plus Makeshift Mannequin's SacrificeSelfEffect. Conditionals and OncePerTurn are unwrapped, then everything goes in ONE entry: CounterSpellingEffect -> entry targeting the triggering object in the STACK zone; TriggeringSpellReferencingEffect -> entry carrying the triggering card/controller; targeted -> SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget; else plain. Direct stack.add. SURPRISE: 'first time each turn' (Glyph Keeper) is inferred from the CounterSpellEffect class via becomeTargetCounterUsedThisTurn, which is set on every permanent the collector runs for, including attached Auras.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 3, `MayEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers from TriggerCollectionService#checkBecomesTargetOfSpellTriggers and #checkBecomesTargetOfAbilityTriggers (activated and triggered abilities), wrapped in collectWithAllyCreatureBecomesTargetCopies; includes granted triggered abilities; TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect filtered; each CounterSpellingEffect gets its own stack object targeting the triggering entry; other non-targeting effects share one stack.add; targeting effects share one SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget (hard-coded 'another creature you control' filter if the card declares no target group); skips hasLostPrintedAbilities sources.
- **`ON_ANOTHER_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAnotherAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfSpellOrAbilityTriggers` (queue?). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAnotherAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfSpellOrAbilityTriggers (spell and ability paths); skips the targeted creature itself, cloaked sources and isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn; includes granted; one stack.add with targetId=targeted creature (non-targeting); MayEffect resolved from the entry, not queueMayAbility.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_ACTIVATED_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfActivatedAbilityTriggers` (each). Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfActivatedAbilityTriggers, ability path only, ACTIVATED_ABILITY entries that are not copies; one enqueueTrigger per registration; OncePerTurnTriggerSupport unwrap/mark; skips cloaked and isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn sources; not wrapped in collectWithAllyCreatureBecomesTargetCopies.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfOpponentSpellTriggers` (queue?). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfOpponentSpellTriggers, spell path only; TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect filtered; includes granted; one stack.add; no ability-loss check. SURPRISE: creature check is the printed getCard().hasType(CREATURE), not the effective type (animated noncreatures don't trigger it, type-stripped creatures do).
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_OR_CREATURE_SPELL_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAllyCreatureOrCreatureSpellBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers` (queue?). Notes: Two overloads of TriggerCollectionService#collectAllyCreatureOrCreatureSpellBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers: permanent target (spell and ability paths) and stack target (targetZone STACK, targeted entry is CREATURE_SPELL); one stack.add each with all effects; includes granted; no TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect handling, no ability-loss check.
- **`ON_ALLY_PERMANENT_OR_PLAYER_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAllyPermanentOrPlayerBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 4, `ConditionalEffect` 1, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 1. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAllyPermanentOrPlayerBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers (spell and ability paths; repeated targets deduped); each CounterUnlessEffect gets its own stack object per target, targeting the triggering entry; remaining effects share one stack.add, deduped per (source, effect) across the spell's targets except DrawCardEffect (also under Conditional/May) via isPerTargetDrawTrigger; TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect never matches player targets; skips hasLostPrintedAbilities; includes granted. Dedup is effect-class heuristic, not a registration flag.
- **`ON_ALLY_PERMANENT_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAllyPermanentBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers` (queue?). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 2. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAllyPermanentBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers (spell and ability paths); one stack.add with all effects incl. granted and a source snapshot; no triggeringPermanentId, no conditional filtering, no ability-loss check; MayEffect resolved from the entry.
- **`ON_ANOTHER_ALLY_PERMANENT_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_OPPONENT_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAnotherAllyPermanentBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAnotherAllyPermanentBecomesTargetOfOpponentTriggers (spell and ability paths); each CounterUnlessEffect gets its own stack object targeting the triggering entry; the rest share one stack.add with triggeringPermanentId/controller (non-targeting); includes granted; no ability-loss check.
- **`ON_ANY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAnyCreatureBecomesTargetTriggers` (all). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1, `TriggeringAbilityFromNamedLandConditionalEffect` 1, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAnyCreatureBecomesTargetTriggers (spell and ability paths), watchers on every battlefield; TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect filtered; each effect passed through CardEffect#resolveForBecomesTargetOfSpellOrAbility (can drop it, e.g. the named-land condition); one stack.add with targetId=creature (non-targeting); trigger-copy multiplier only when the watcher's controller controls the creature; skips hasLostPrintedAbilities; no granted triggers.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_YOUR_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectOpponentCreatureBecomesTargetOfYourSpellTriggers` (all). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectOpponentCreatureBecomesTargetOfYourSpellTriggers (spell and ability paths), watchers on the triggering entry controller's battlefield, creature controlled by any other player; one stack.add with targetId=creature (non-targeting) and the watcher as source; skips hasLostPrintedAbilities; no granted triggers; not wrapped in the copies helper.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfSpellTriggers` (queue?). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfSpellTriggers, spell path only; skips sources that lost abilities; includes granted. SURPRISE: always queues a target-choice interaction (ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger if several target groups, else ETBTokenTargetTrigger with the card's target filter) with all effects, even non-targeting ones, and the targeted creature is not recorded on the trigger.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_BACKUP_ABILITY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfBackupAbilityTriggers` (all). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfBackupAbilityTriggers, called only from collectBecomesTargetOfAbilityTriggers (checkBecomesTargetOfAbilityTriggers). Printed + granted effects bundled into one non-targeting entry (targetId = triggering entry), direct gameData.stack.add. 'Backup ability' = any TRIGGERED_ABILITY whose source card has printed Keyword.BACKUP, not specifically the backup trigger. Once-per-turn via Permanent.backupAbilityCopyUsedThisTurn set when queued. No ability-loss check on printed effects.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_INSTANT_OR_SORCERY`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfInstantOrSorceryTriggers` (queue?). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#collectAllyCreatureBecomesTargetOfInstantOrSorceryTriggers from collectBecomesTargetOfSpellTriggers (checkBecomesTargetOfSpellTriggers). All registrations (TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect filtered against the target) bundled into one non-targeting entry with targetId = targeted creature, direct stack.add via enqueueTargetTriggeredAbility. Skips hasLostPrintedAbilities sources; granted abilities not read; instant/sorcery test uses the spell card's printed types.

## Step 13.7: library, draw and discard

| Constant | Kind | Event | Object | Relation | Zone | Mult. | Self | Grouping | Cards |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `ON_CONTROLLER_DRAWS_SECOND_CARD` | triggered | you draw your second card of the turn | card (drawn) | you | battlefield | once per turn (on the draw that makes cardsDrawnThisTurn == 2) | n/a | each | 40 |
| `GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_DRAWS_SECOND_CARD` | triggered | this card's owner draws their second card of the turn | card (drawn) | you (graveyard owner) | graveyard | once per turn | n/a | each | 1 |
| `GRAVEYARD_ON_OPPONENT_DRAWS_SECOND_CARD` | triggered | an opponent of this card's owner draws their second card of the turn | card (drawn) | opponent (of the graveyard owner) | graveyard | once per turn per drawing opponent | n/a | each | 1 |
| `ON_OPPONENT_DRAWS` | triggered | an opponent draws a card | card (drawn) | opponent | battlefield (+ command zone for planes) | per card drawn | n/a | each | 29 |

| `ON_OPPONENT_DISCARDS` | triggered | an opponent discards a card (cycling included) | card (TriggeringCardConditionalEffect filters type) | opponent | battlefield | per card discarded | n/a | each | 23 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_DISCARDS` | triggered | you discard a card (cycling included) | card (TriggeringCardConditionalEffect filters type) | you | battlefield | per card discarded | n/a | each | 62 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_DISCARD_EVENT` | triggered | you discard one or more cards in one discard event | cards (any discarded card can satisfy TriggeringCardConditionalEffect) | you | battlefield | per discard event | n/a | each | 15 |
| `ON_ANY_PLAYER_CYCLES` | triggered | any player cycles a card | card (cycled) | any player (including you) | battlefield | per cycle | n/a | each | 7 |
| `ON_SELF_CYCLED` | triggered | this card is cycled | card (this) | this | hand | per cycle | only | all | 56 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_SCRIES` | triggered | you scry |  | you | battlefield | per scry | n/a | each | 9 |
| `ON_OPPONENT_SCRIES` | triggered | an opponent scries |  | opponent | battlefield | per scry | n/a | each | 1 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_SEARCHES_LIBRARY` | triggered | you search your own library (also when nothing is found) |  | you | battlefield | per search | n/a | all | 1 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_SURVEILS` | triggered | you surveil |  | you | battlefield | per surveil | n/a | each | 9 |
| `ON_OPPONENT_SURVEILS` | triggered | an opponent surveils |  | opponent | battlefield | per surveil | n/a | each | 1 |
| `ON_SELF_DISCARDED` | triggered | this card is discarded for any reason (cycling included) | card (this) | this | hand | per discard | only | all | 3 |
| `ON_SELF_DISCARDED_BY_OPPONENT` | triggered | this card is discarded by a spell or ability an opponent controls | card (this) | this; discard caused by an opponent | hand | per discard | only | all | 15 |
| `ON_OPPONENT_SHUFFLES_LIBRARY` | triggered | an opponent shuffles their library |  | opponent | battlefield | per shuffle | n/a | each | 2 |
| `ON_ANY_PLAYER_SHUFFLES_LIBRARY` | triggered | any player shuffles their library |  | each player (including you) | battlefield | per shuffle | n/a | each | 1 |
| `ON_SPELL_OR_ABILITY_CAUSES_CONTROLLER_SHUFFLE` | triggered | a spell or ability causes its controller to shuffle their library | spell or ability (the resolving object) | any player (the shuffler must be the resolving object's controller) | battlefield | per shuffle | n/a | each | 1 |
| `ON_OPPONENT_SEARCHES_LIBRARY` | triggered | an opponent searches their own library (also when nothing is found) |  | opponent | battlefield | per search | n/a | all | 4 |
| `GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_SURVEILS` | triggered | you surveil |  | you (owner of the graveyard) | graveyard | per event (per surveil) | n/a | each | 1 |

- **`ON_CONTROLLER_DRAWS`**. Readers: `DrawService`: `checkControllerDrawTriggers` (read), `checkPlanarDrawTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 8, `ConditionalEffect` 6, `MayEffect` 2, `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `NthCardDrawTriggerEffect` 11, `FirstDrawStepDrawOnlyTriggerEffect` 1, `FirstOrSecondCardDrawTriggerEffect` 1, `OnceOnlyTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: DrawService#checkControllerDrawTriggers -> checkControllerDrawTriggerSlot, called from DrawService#completeDrawCard (single draw choke point). Planes are read by checkPlanarDrawTriggers when the planar controller draws. Reads printed (unless hasLostAllAbilities) + granted. Per registration it handles OncePerTurn/OnceOnly, the ExceptFirstDrawStep/FirstDrawStepDrawOnly/FirstDrawReveal/DrawReveal/DrawnCard/DrawTrigger wrappers, triggersOnControllerDrawCount, intervening-if and the Diviner's Wand gate; copies come from countAdditionalDrawTriggeredAbilityTriggers. Paths: non-targeting MayEffect -> queueMayAbility; player/any target -> DrawTriggerAnyTarget; permanent target -> DrawTriggerPermanentTarget/SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget; else direct stack.add (targetId=drawer). SURPRISE: NthCardDrawTriggerEffect(2, ...) here on about 9 cards duplicates ON_CONTROLLER_DRAWS_SECOND_CARD. RevealDrawnCardIfInstantOrSorceryEffect is not a trigger at all: it is pushed straight onto pendingMayAbilities.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_DRAWS_SECOND_CARD`**. Readers: `DrawService`: `checkControllerDrawTriggers` (read). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 9, `MayEffect` 1. Notes: Same DrawService#checkControllerDrawTriggerSlot as ON_CONTROLLER_DRAWS (all wrappers and effect-class paths apply). checkControllerDrawTriggers gates it on cardsDrawnThisTurn == 2. No planar reader. SURPRISE: there is a second encoding of the same trigger, NthCardDrawTriggerEffect(2, ...) on ON_CONTROLLER_DRAWS.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_DRAWS_SECOND_CARD`**. Readers: `DrawService`: `checkControllerDrawTriggers` (read). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: DrawService#checkGraveyardControllerDrawTriggerSlot, called from checkControllerDrawTriggers when cardsDrawnThisTurn == 2. Scans only the drawing player's graveyard and is skipped when graveyardCardsHaveLostAllAbilities. One enqueueTrigger per registration (MayPayManaEffect stays wrapped for resolution), recording triggeringCardId and the graveyard entry version. Only triggersOnControllerDrawCount is honored; no other wrappers.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_OPPONENT_DRAWS_SECOND_CARD`**. Readers: `DrawService`: `checkGraveyardOpponentDrawTriggerSlot` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Notes: DrawService#checkGraveyardOpponentDrawTriggerSlot, called at the end of checkOpponentDrawTriggers; scans every other player's graveyard. DrawTriggerEffect unwrapped. A MayEffect stays on the enqueued stack entry, whereas battlefield ON_OPPONENT_DRAWS uses queueMayAbility. Unlike the controller-side twin it records no triggering card or graveyard entry version.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_DRAWS`**. Readers: `DrawService`: `checkOpponentDrawTriggers` (each), `checkPlanarDrawTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 4, `MayEffect` 2, `MayPayManaEffect` 2, `SequenceEffect` 2. Trigger effects: `NthCardDrawTriggerEffect` 10, `ExceptFirstDrawStepTriggerEffect` 3, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: DrawService#checkOpponentDrawTriggers (from completeDrawCard) scans every other battlefield, skips hasLostAllAbilities and reads no granted triggers. Handles OncePerTurn/OnceOnly, ExceptFirstDrawStep, DrawReveal and DrawTrigger (NthCardDrawTriggerEffect) wrappers, intervening-if with targetId=drawer, and draw-trigger copies. Paths: MayEffect -> queueMayAbility; player/any target -> DrawTriggerAnyTarget; else direct stack.add with targetId=drawing player, nonTargeting. checkPlanarDrawTriggers reads it for planes when a non-planar-controller draws (MayEffect -> queueMayAbility, else enqueueTrigger).
- **`ON_ENCHANTED_PLAYER_DRAWS`**. Readers: `DrawService`: `checkEnchantedPlayerDrawTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: DrawService#checkEnchantedPlayerDrawTriggers (from completeDrawCard) finds Auras attached to the drawing player on any battlefield. DrawTriggerEffect unwrapped; draw-trigger copies applied. MayEffect -> queueMayAbility without a source permanent id; else direct stack.add with targetId=drawer. No ability-loss check and no granted triggers.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_DISCARDS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDiscardTriggers` (each), `collectTemporaryGlobalDiscardTriggers` (queue?); `DiscardTriggerCollectorService`: 15 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `AwardManaEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `ConjureDuplicateOfDiscardedCardIntoChosenPlayerHandEffect`, `CreateTokenEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `DrawCardEffect`, `ExileDiscardedCardFromGraveyardEffect`, `LoseLifeEffect`, `MayEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `PerpetuallyBoostSourceEffect`, `PutCounterOnEachControlledPermanentEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`, `PutCountersOnSourceEffect`, `SequenceEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 6, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 5, `MayPayManaEffect` 2, `ConditionalEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscardTriggers (central discard hook) scans every other battlefield and skips hasLostAllAbilities. It unwraps TriggeringCardConditionalEffect (dropped when the card went to the library unrevealed), then calls dispatchSlotEffect. DiscardTriggerCollectorService has many class-keyed handlers but no default CardEffect handler, so unlisted effect classes silently never trigger. Some are recipient-restricted: DealDamageToPlayersEffect only TRIGGERING_PLAYER, LoseLifeEffect only TRIGGERING_PLAYER/CONTROLLER. MayEffect -> direct stack.add; others enqueueTrigger. SURPRISE: collectTemporaryGlobalDiscardTriggers reuses this slot with the opposite meaning for a TemporaryGlobalTriggeredAbility (Pure Intentions): it fires when the watcher's own controller is made to discard by an opponent (discardCausedByOpponent).
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_DISCARDS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDiscardTriggers` (each); `DiscardTriggerCollectorService`: 28 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `AwardManaEffect`, `BoostSelfEffect`, `BoostTargetCreatureEffect`, `CastDiscardedCardFromGraveyardEffect`, `ChooseModeNotYetChosenThisTurnEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `ConjureDuplicateOfDiscardedCardIntoChosenPlayerHandEffect`, `CreateTokenEffect`, `CyclingTriggerEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `DrawCardEffect`, `ExileDiscardedCardFromGraveyardEffect`, `ExileTopCardMayPlayThisTurnEffect`, `GrantKeywordEffect`, `LoseLifeEffect`, `MayEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `MillEffect`, `OpponentCausedDiscardTriggerEffect`, `PerpetuallyBoostSourceEffect`, `PutCounterOnEachMatchingPermanentEffect`, `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`, `PutCountersOnSourceEffect`, `ScryEffect`, `SequenceEffect`, `SourceFightsTargetCreatureEffect`, `UntapPermanentsEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 17, `MayEffect` 10, `MayPayManaEffect` 10, `SequenceEffect` 6, `ConditionalEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `CyclingTriggerEffect` 12, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 4, `OpponentCausedDiscardTriggerEffect` 1. 3 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscardTriggers, own-battlefield loop. Unwraps TriggeringCardConditionalEffect and OncePerTurnTriggerEffect (firstCycling and FIRST_NONLAND_DISCARD_KEY special-cased), then uses the private dispatch. CyclingTriggerEffect is the 'whenever you cycle' wrapper (12 cards, parallel to ON_ANY_PLAYER_CYCLES); OpponentCausedDiscardTriggerEffect gates on discardCausedByOpponent. Class-keyed handlers only, no default; several are recipient-restricted (DealDamageToPlayersEffect and MillEffect EACH_OPPONENT, UntapPermanentsEffect SELF). Targeted classes -> DiscardControllerTriggerTarget; MayEffect -> direct stack.add; else enqueueTrigger. SURPRISE: unlike ON_OPPONENT_DISCARDS, this loop has no ability-loss check and ignores granted triggers, so a permanent that lost all abilities still triggers.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_DISCARD_EVENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDiscardEventTriggers` (each), `dispatchSlotEffect` (each); `DiscardTriggerCollectorService`: 11 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CreateTokenEffect`, `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `DrawCardEffect`, `ExileDiscardedCardFromGraveyardEffect`, `ExileTopCardsMayPlayUntilNextEndStepEffect`, `LoseLifeEffect`, `MayEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`, `SeekCardSharingCardTypeWithDiscardedCardsEffect`, `SequenceEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 4. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlotEffect` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscardTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#collectBatchedOncePerTurnAllyNontokenDeathTrigger`, `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchDepartedManaSourceSpellCastTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlot`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscardEventTriggers via dispatchSlot. Fired from finishDiscardEvent for batches (beginDiscardEvent/finishDiscardEvent; PlayerInputService also opens a batch for discard choices), or immediately from checkDiscardTriggers for a lone discard outside a batch. dispatchSlotEffect special-cases TriggeringCardConditionalEffect (matches if any discarded card does) and OncePerTurnTriggerEffect FIRST_DISCARD_EVENT_KEY. Handlers set eventValue=discarded count; MayEffect is enqueued; SequenceEffect and DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect -> DiscardControllerTriggerTarget; DealDamageToPlayersEffect and LoseLifeEffect only with EACH_OPPONENT. No default handler.
- **`ON_ANY_PLAYER_CYCLES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCycleTriggers` (each); `DiscardTriggerCollectorService`: 6 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `BoostSelfEffect`, `GrantKeywordEffect`, `MayEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`, `ReturnToHandEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 2, `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkCycleTriggers runs dispatchSlot over every permanent (forEachPermanent); called from AbilityActivationService on cycling activation. DiscardTriggerCollectorService handlers: MayEffect (targeted -> DiscardControllerTriggerTarget, else enqueue), ReturnToHandEffect, PutCountersOnSelfEffect, MayPayManaEffect, BoostSelfEffect, GrantKeywordEffect. No default handler. Many 'whenever you cycle' cards use ON_CONTROLLER_DISCARDS + CyclingTriggerEffect instead.
- **`ON_SELF_CYCLED`**. Readers: `TriggeredAbilityQueueService`: `processNextDiscardControllerTriggerTarget` (queue?); `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCycleTriggers` (each); `ConjureSkywriterDjinnSpellbookEffectHandler`: `chokingTethers` (read). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 21, `ConditionalEffect` 3, `SequenceEffect` 1. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkCycleTriggers, called from AbilityActivationService on cycling activation. Intervening-if conditionals are filtered at trigger time; all remaining registrations go in ONE stack object (Yidaro's two). Target paths: targets announced with the cycling ability (Rampaging War Mammoth); SPELL target -> ETBSpellTargetTrigger; permanent/player -> DiscardControllerTriggerTarget (TriggeredAbilityQueueService#processNextDiscardControllerTriggerTarget compares against the slot for exact multi-target counts); graveyard target -> SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger; else enqueueTrigger with X. Independent of the discard triggers (cycling also calls checkDiscardTriggers with cycled=true). ConjureSkywriterDjinnSpellbookEffectHandler only writes the slot on a conjured card.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_SCRIES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkScryTriggers` (each); `ScryTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `SequenceEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 3, `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 2. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkScryTriggers runs dispatchSlot over the scrier's battlefield; called from ScryEffectHandler, ScryInteractionHandler and MayMiscHandlerService. ScryTriggerCollectorService: SequenceEffect, MayPayManaEffect and the default all go to enqueueOnScry. That sets eventValue=cards bottomed and cardsLookedAt, sends targeted effects to SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget, skips ExileBottomCardsToSourceEffect(EventValue) when nothing was bottomed, and supports OncePerTurn markOnAcceptance.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_SCRIES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkScryTriggers` (each); `ScryTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `SequenceEffect`. Notes: Same collector and handlers as ON_CONTROLLER_SCRIES (TriggerCollectionService#checkScryTriggers over other battlefields). enqueueOnScry takes the opponent branch: targetId=scrying player, nonTargeting, and targeted effects never get a target interaction. River Song registers one SequenceEffect in three slots (opponent scries / surveils / searches library).
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_SEARCHES_LIBRARY`**. Readers: `LibrarySearchTriggerHelper`: `recordSearchAndQueueTriggers` (all), `anyPermanentReactsToLibrarySearches` (read). Notes: LibrarySearchTriggerHelper#recordSearchAndQueueTriggers is called from about 40 call sites in the search handlers (LibrarySearchSupport, SearchLibraryEffectHandler, BasicLandSearchQueueSupport, LibraryChoiceHandlerService, ...) and only for own-library searches. It puts ALL of the slot's effects into one enqueued, nonTargeting entry per permanent. Only isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn is checked: static ability loss, granted triggers, OncePerTurn, May and target handling are all ignored. anyPermanentReactsToLibrarySearches is a presence check.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_SURVEILS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSurveilTriggers` (each), `dispatchSlotEffect` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`. Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 2. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlotEffect` ← `TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscardTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#collectBatchedOncePerTurnAllyNontokenDeathTrigger`, `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchDepartedManaSourceSpellCastTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#dispatchSlot`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSurveilTriggers (from SurveilEffectHandler) uses dispatchSlot. dispatchSlotEffect drops OncePerTurnTriggerEffect FIRST_SURVEIL_KEY unless this is the first surveil this turn. MiscTriggerCollectorService: PutCountersOnSelfEffect, OncePerTurnTriggerEffect and the default all enqueueTrigger, with no target interaction for targeted effects. The same method also reads GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_SURVEILS.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_SURVEILS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSurveilTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`. Notes: Same TriggerCollectionService#checkSurveilTriggers over other battlefields, with the same handlers as ON_CONTROLLER_SURVEILS; they set targetId=surveiling player, nonTargeting. Only used by River Song.
- **`ON_SELF_DISCARDED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDiscardTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscardTriggers (tail) puts all registrations in one entry. Any permanent/player target -> DiscardTriggerAnyTarget interaction; else enqueueTrigger. No intervening-if filtering (the BY_OPPONENT twin filters) and no ability-loss check.
- **`ON_SELF_DISCARDED_BY_OPPONENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDiscardTriggers` (each); `CardChoiceHandlerService`: `hasEnterBattlefieldOnDiscardEffect` (read); `VoidEffectHandler`: `hasEnterBattlefieldOnDiscardEffect` (read); `TargetPlayerDiscardsAndDestroysCreaturesOfChosenTypeEffectHandler`: `hasEnterBattlefieldOnDiscardEffect` (read); `RevealHandAndDiscardDuplicateNonlandCardsEffectHandler`: `discardCard` (read); `PlayerInteractionSupport`: `resolveRandomDiscardCards` (read), `resolveRevealHandAndRandomDiscardOfType` (read); `ZyymRevealHandEffectMayHandler`: `discardMostRecentlyRevealed` (read); `BattlefieldEntryService`: `putPermanentOntoBattlefieldFromOpponentDiscard` (read). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: mixed: replacement EnterBattlefieldOnDiscardEffect (5 cards) is read in place by the discard paths: CardChoiceHandlerService, VoidEffectHandler, TargetPlayerDiscardsAndDestroysCreaturesOfChosenTypeEffectHandler, RevealHandAndDiscardDuplicateNonlandCardsEffectHandler, PlayerInteractionSupport (x2) and ZyymRevealHandEffectMayHandler, all leading to BattlefieldEntryService#putPermanentOntoBattlefieldFromOpponentDiscard. The trigger path filters it out. Trigger: TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscardTriggers when gameData.discardCausedByOpponent; intervening-if conditionals are filtered and the rest go in one entry (targeted -> DiscardTriggerAnyTarget), recording the graveyard entry version. SURPRISE: every discard path has to check for the replacement itself (7 reader sites in 6 classes).
- **`ON_OPPONENT_SHUFFLES_LIBRARY`**. Readers: `LibraryShuffleHelper`: `checkShuffleTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 2. Notes: LibraryShuffleHelper#checkShuffleTriggers, called from LibraryShuffleHelper#shuffleLibrary (every engine shuffle; mulligan shuffles bypass it). One enqueueTrigger per registration with targetId=shuffling player, nonTargeting, plus a source snapshot. A MayEffect stays on the entry. No ability-loss check and no granted triggers.
- **`ON_ANY_PLAYER_SHUFFLES_LIBRARY`**. Readers: `LibraryShuffleHelper`: `checkShuffleTriggers` (each). Notes: Same LibraryShuffleHelper#checkShuffleTriggers (one entry per registration, targetId=shuffling player, nonTargeting). Psychogenic Probe's 'a spell or ability causes' wording is not checked: it fires on every shuffleLibrary call (in practice all of these come from spells/abilities). No ability-loss check.
- **`ON_SPELL_OR_ABILITY_CAUSES_CONTROLLER_SHUFFLE`**. Readers: `LibraryShuffleHelper`: `checkShuffleTriggers` (each). Notes: Same LibraryShuffleHelper#checkShuffleTriggers. The causing controller is currentlyResolvingControllerId, else pendingEffectResolutionEntry's controller. Fires on every permanent regardless of who controls it; targetId=shuffling player, nonTargeting. No ability-loss check.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_SEARCHES_LIBRARY`**. Readers: `LibrarySearchTriggerHelper`: `recordSearchAndQueueTriggers` (all), `anyPermanentReactsToLibrarySearches` (read). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 2. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: Same LibrarySearchTriggerHelper#recordSearchAndQueueTriggers as ON_CONTROLLER_SEARCHES_LIBRARY. All of the slot's effects go in one enqueued entry per permanent (Ob Nixilis' two registrations bundled correctly), with targetId=searching player, nonTargeting. Only isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn is checked. A search stopped by Leonin Arbiter never calls it.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_SURVEILS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSurveilTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1, `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSurveilTriggers reads getEffectiveGraveyardEffects of the surveiling player's graveyard; one enqueueTrigger per effect; no registry dispatch, no MayEffect/queueMayAbility path, no trigger-time condition check (Blood Operative's ConditionalEffect(SourceCardInGraveyard) only checked on resolution); unlike the proliferate graveyard reader the entry gets no triggeringCardId/graveyard entry version.

## Step 13.8: tap and untap

| Constant | Kind | Event | Object | Relation | Zone | Mult. | Self | Grouping | Cards |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ON_SELF_TAPPED_FOR_MANA` | triggered | this tapped for mana (mana-ability tap path only) |  | this | battlefield | per tap | only | all | 1 |
| `ON_ANY_PLAYER_TAPS_LAND` | triggered | a land is tapped for mana | land | varies by effect class: any player, you, opponent, enchanted land, this land | battlefield; command zone (face-up planes); floating effects | per land tap | yes | each | 56 |
| `ON_ANY_PLAYER_TAPS_PERMANENT_FOR_MANA` | triggered | a permanent is tapped for mana | permanent | any player (Treasure Nabber handler: opponent taps an artifact) | battlefield; command zone (face-up planes) | per tap | yes | each | 2 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_TAPS_CREATURE_FOR_MANA` | triggered | you tap a creature for mana | creature | you control (incl. this) | battlefield | per tap | yes | each | 2 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_TAPS_NONLAND_PERMANENT_FOR_MANA` | triggered | you tap a nonland permanent for mana | nonland permanent (printed type check) | you control (incl. this) | battlefield | per tap | yes | each | 2 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_TAPS_ARTIFACT_TOKEN_FOR_MANA` | triggered | you tap an artifact token for mana | artifact token (printed type, nonland) | you control | battlefield | per tap | yes | each | 1 |
| `ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_TAPPED` | triggered | becomes tapped (any cause) | permanent | enchanted/equipped (any permanent attached to it) | battlefield | per tap | no | each | 23 |
| `ON_ALLY_PERMANENT_BECOMES_TAPPED` | triggered | becomes tapped (any cause) | permanent | you control (incl. this) | battlefield | per tapped permanent; ONCE_PER_BATCH registrations once per tap-batch scope | yes | each | 75 |
| `ON_SELF_TAPPED_TO_PAY_TEAMWORK_COST` | triggered | this is tapped to pay a teamwork cost |  | this | battlefield | per tap | only | each | 1 |
| `ON_SELF_BECOMES_UNTAPPED` | triggered | this becomes untapped (tapped to untapped, any cause) |  | this | battlefield | per untap | only | split | 28 |
| `ON_ALLY_PERMANENT_BECOMES_UNTAPPED` | triggered | becomes untapped | permanent | you control (incl. this) | battlefield | per untapped permanent | yes | each | 1 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_UNTAPS_DURING_UNTAP_STEP` | triggered | you untap one or more permanents during your untap step | permanent | you | battlefield | once per untap step (eventValue = count) | n/a | each | 1 |
| `ON_ANY_PERMANENT_BECOMES_UNTAPPED` | triggered | becomes untapped | permanent | any (incl. this) | battlefield | per untapped permanent | yes | each | 2 |
| `ON_OPPONENT_PERMANENT_BECOMES_TAPPED` | triggered | becomes tapped (any cause) | permanent | opponent controls | battlefield | per tapped permanent; ONCE_PER_BATCH supported | no | each | 15 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_TAPS_OPPONENT_PERMANENT` | triggered | you tap a permanent an opponent controls | permanent | opponent controls; tapper = you | battlefield | per tapped permanent; ONCE_PER_BATCH supported | no | each | 4 |
| `ON_ANY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TAPPED` | triggered | a creature becomes tapped | creature | any | any | per object | n/a | each | 1 |

- **`ON_SELF_TAPPED_FOR_MANA`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSelfTappedForManaTriggers` (all). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSelfTappedForManaTriggers; callers AbilityActivationService (ON_TAP mana path, always) and ActivatedAbilityExecutionService (mana ActivatedAbility with a tap cost). Printed + granted effects bundled into one enqueued entry (triggeringPermanentId = this); both callers move it into pendingManaAbilityTriggers. A regular triggered ability (Zhur-Taa Druid damage), not a mana ability. No ability-loss check on printed effects.
- **`ON_ANY_PLAYER_TAPS_LAND`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkLandTapTriggers` (each); `LandTapTriggerCollectorService`: 25 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `AddExtraManaOfChosenColorOnLandTapEffect`, `AddManaForEachOtherLandWithSameNameEffect`, `AddManaOfTypeProducedByTappedPermanentEffect`, `AddManaOnEnchantedLandTapEffect`, `AddManaWhenLandOfColorTappedForManaEffect`, `AddManaWhenLandOfSubtypeTappedForManaEffect`, `AddManaWhenLandTappedForManaEffect`, `AddOneOfEachManaTypeProducedByLandEffect`, `AddProducedManaWhenLandOfSubtypeTappedEffect`, `AddProducedManaWhenSnowLandTappedEffect`, `AddRestrictedManaWhenLandOfSubtypeTappedForManaEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `CreateTokenForTargetPlayerEffect`, `DealDamageOnLandTapEffect`, `DestroyReferencedPermanentEffect`, `GainLifeWhenOpponentTapsLandOfSubtypeEffect`, `OpponentTappedLandDoesntUntapEffect`, `PutCounterOnTargetCreatureAndCreateTokenIfSharesManaColorEffect`, `RegisterDelayedChooseOpponentGainsControlOfSourceEffect`, `RemoveCounterFromSourceEffect`, `ReturnTappedLandToHandEffect`, `SequenceEffect`, `TapLandsThatCouldProduceSameManaAsTappedLandEffect`, `TapPermanentsEffect`, `TappedSnowLandDoesntUntapEffect`; `PredicateEvaluationService`: `hasTriggeredManaAbility` (read); `PerpetuallyGiveLandExtraManaEffectHandler`: `resolve` (read); `PotentialManaService`: `hasAttachedManaChoiceTrigger` (read); `AiTargetSelector`: `isControllerBeneficialLandAura` (read); `GameSimulator`: `isControllerBeneficialLandAura` (read). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 3, `ConditionalEffect` 1. 2 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Choke points: `PerpetuallyGiveLandExtraManaEffectHandler#resolve` ← `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#doResolveManaAbility`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#performSpecialAction`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#pushAbilityOnStack`, `AnimationSupport#fireTransformTriggers`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeCreatureOrTransformSourceEffectHandler#finish`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeNontokenCreatureTapAndSeekLandSourceEffectHandler#tapThenSeek`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#dealDamage`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#sacrificeSource`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceHandler#handle`, `AnyOpponentMayTapCreatureTapAndCreateTokenSourceEffectHandler#tapAndCreateToken`, `AnyPlayerMaySacrificeLandPutSourceOnTopEffectHandler#putSourceOnTop`, `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrBoostSelfEffectHandler#boostSelf`, +193 more. Notes: Mostly triggered mana abilities (CR 605.1b: no stack). TriggerCollectionService#checkLandTapTriggers (AbilityActivationService ON_TAP and Piracy paths, ActivatedAbilityExecutionService land ActivatedAbility path, ChoiceHandlerService, AwardManaOfChosenColorEffectHandler). Printed effects only (no granted), only hasLostAllAbilities checked; ~25 class-keyed handlers in LandTapTriggerCollectorService and no default (unhandled classes silently ignored). mixed: triggered mana abilities add mana immediately or defer to pendingManaAbilityTriggers (AddManaOnEnchantedLandTap, AddOneOfEachManaTypeProducedByLand, AddManaWhenLandOfSubtypeTappedForMana, AddExtraManaOfChosenColorOnLandTap, AddManaOfTypeProducedByTappedPermanent...) vs stack triggers via enqueueTrigger (DealDamageOnLandTap, GainLifeWhenOpponentTapsLandOfSubtype as a MayEffect, ReturnTappedLandToHand, DestroyReferencedPermanent, OpponentTappedLandDoesntUntap, TapPermanents, Mana Web, RemoveCounterFromSource), CreateTokenForTargetPlayer via direct stack.add with no target step, RegisterDelayedChooseOpponentGainsControlOfSource as a delayed action, Camp effect via a target interaction. 'Opponent' checks compare against the tapping player, not the land's controller. Presence also read by PredicateEvaluationService#hasTriggeredManaAbility, PotentialManaService and AI.
- **`ON_ANY_PLAYER_TAPS_PERMANENT_FOR_MANA`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAnyPlayerTapsPermanentForManaTriggers` (each); `ManaTapTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `AddManaOfTypeProducedByTappedPermanentEffect`, `GainControlOfTargetEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyPlayerTapsPermanentForManaTriggers (dispatchSlot over every permanent + face-up planar cards) from AbilityActivationService (ON_TAP) and ActivatedAbilityExecutionService (tap-cost mana abilities). mixed: ManaTapTriggerCollectorService GainControlOfTargetEffect = stack trigger; AddManaOfTypeProducedByTappedPermanentEffect = triggered mana ability (adds mana to the tapping player, colour choice, or pendingManaAbilityTriggers while the type is pending); no default. SURPRISE: it re-finds the tapped permanent by id and returns if gone, so sources sacrificed in the mana cost (Treasure) never trigger it (Eloren Wilds' extra mana lost). Suspected: in the ActivatedAbilityExecutionService path the call is not wrapped in the stack-diff deferral, so Treasure Nabber's trigger lands on the stack mid-payment instead of in pendingManaAbilityTriggers.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_TAPS_CREATURE_FOR_MANA`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCreatureTapForManaTriggers` (each); `ManaTapTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `AddManaWhenCreatureTappedForManaEffect`. Notes: Triggered mana ability (CR 605.1b: no stack). TriggerCollectionService#checkCreatureTapForManaTriggers (tapping player's battlefield, printed only, hasLostPrintedAbilities skip, registry.dispatch directly so no trigger-copy scope) from AbilityActivationService (ON_TAP mana from a creature) and ActivatedAbilityExecutionService. Only AddManaWhenCreatureTappedForManaEffect handler: adds the mana straight to the pool (triggered mana ability, no stack); no default.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_TAPS_NONLAND_PERMANENT_FOR_MANA`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkNonlandPermanentTapForManaTriggers` (each); `ManaTapTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `AddManaOfTypeProducedByTappedPermanentEffect`. Notes: Triggered mana ability (CR 605.1b: no stack). TriggerCollectionService#checkNonlandPermanentTapForManaTriggers (same callers); returns when the tapped card's printed types include LAND or no new mana type was produced. Only AddManaOfTypeProducedByTappedPermanentEffect handler: adds mana directly, colour-choice interaction, or pendingManaAbilityTriggers entry while the type is pending; no default. Forsaken Monument's land half lives in ON_ANY_PLAYER_TAPS_LAND.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_TAPS_ARTIFACT_TOKEN_FOR_MANA`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkNonlandPermanentTapForManaTriggers` (each); `ManaTapTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `AddManaOfTypeProducedByTappedPermanentEffect`. Notes: Triggered mana ability (CR 605.1b: no stack). Same TriggerCollectionService#checkNonlandPermanentTapForManaTriggers loop; the slot is added only when the tapped card isToken and has printed ARTIFACT, and the early LAND return means artifact-land tokens never trigger. Handler delegates to the nonland AddManaOfTypeProducedByTappedPermanentEffect handler.
- **`ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_TAPPED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkEnchantedPermanentTapTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 12 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `BoostTargetCreatureEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `DestroyReferencedPermanentEffect`, `DrawCardEffect`, `ForcedCostOrElseEffect`, `GivePoisonCountersEffect`, `LoseLifeEffect`, `MillEffect`, `PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect`, `RelicBindTapEffect`, `RemoveCounterFromSourceEffect`, `SequenceEffect`; `AiManaManager`: `attachedTapTriggerCost` (read). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 2, `MayEffect` 1. CrewCostHandler) - no single choke point. CrewCostHandler) - no single choke point. Reads layered (LayerSystemService.currentCard) printed registrations + temporary + persistent + granted, plus temporary global watchers (collectTemporaryGlobalTriggers). Per registration: TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect filter, global OncePerTurnPerCreature, ChooseOneAtTriggerTime -> TriggeredModalTrigger, graveyard target -> SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger, permanent/player target -> EntersTriggerTarget, else enqueue. SURPRISE: begin/endPermanentTapTriggerBatch scopes exist only in AbilityActivationService, CombatAttackService (attack), SpellCastingService and MayCastHandlerService; effect-driven mass taps through TapUntapSupport#tapPermanent are unbatched, so 'one or more' ONCE_PER_BATCH triggers (Deeproot Pilgrimage, Rewrite History) fire once per tapped permanent there.
- **`ON_SELF_TAPPED_TO_PAY_TEAMWORK_COST`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSelfTappedToPayTeamworkCostTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Printed + granted effects; only isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn checked (static ability loss ignored); trigger copies added manually via countAdditionalTriggeredAbilityTriggers.

TriggerCollectionService#checkBecomesUntappedTriggers from TapUntapSupport#untapPermanent (untap step + untap effects) and MayMiscHandlerService; ~9 direct Permanent.untap() calls bypass it (e.g. untap-symbol costs in ActivatedAbilityExecutionService, MultiplePermanentUntapCostHandler). Handler: intervening-if filtered, targeted -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget or ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger, else one enqueued entry. Printed only; TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect filter then plain enqueue. No ability-loss check and no target, once-per-turn or intervening-if handling; no triggeringPermanentId set (unlike ON_ANY_PERMANENT_BECOMES_UNTAPPED).
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_UNTAPS_DURING_UNTAP_STEP`**. Only PutCountersOnSelfEffect handler (MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleUntapStepPutCountersOnSelf); no default.
- **`ON_ANY_PERMANENT_BECOMES_UNTAPPED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkBecomesUntappedTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1, `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: Same TriggerCollectionService#checkBecomesUntappedTriggers; all battlefields, skips face-down and lost-printed-abilities sources; TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect filter, once-per-turn unwrap, entry with triggeringPermanentId and eventPlayerIds = untapped permanent's controller; no target step.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_PERMANENT_BECOMES_TAPPED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectTemporaryGlobalTriggers` (read), `enqueueTemporaryGlobalTrigger` (each), `checkEnchantedPermanentTapTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 14, `MayEffect` 2. Choke points: `TriggerCollectionService#collectTemporaryGlobalTriggers` ← `CombatBlockService#checkAnyCreatureBlocksTriggers`, `CombatDamageService#checkAllyCreatureCombatDamageToPlayerTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyCreatureDealtDamageTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyCreatureDeathTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#checkAnyNontokenCreatureDeathTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#checkEnchantedPermanentTapTriggers`, `TriggerCollectionService#checkLifeGainTriggers`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkEnchantedPermanentTapTriggers, run from ~34 tap call sites (TapUntapSupport#tapPermanent, CombatAttackService, AbilityActivationService, ActivatedAbilityExecutionService, SpellCastingService, MultiPermanentChoiceHandlerService, cost handlers incl. CrewCostHandler) - no single choke point. Printed only (no temporary/persistent/granted, unlike the ally variant) plus temporary global watchers. TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect filter, once-per-turn, intervening-if, graveyard target -> SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger, else enqueue with triggeringPermanentId. SURPRISE: no permanent/player target step (the ally variant has EntersTriggerTarget), so a targeted effect goes on the stack without a chosen target (only TRIGGERING_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER damage gets a baked targetId).
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_TAPS_OPPONENT_PERMANENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkEnchantedPermanentTapTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect` 4, `MayPayManaEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Modes: `ONCE_PER_BATCH` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkEnchantedPermanentTapTriggers, run from ~34 tap call sites (TapUntapSupport#tapPermanent, CombatAttackService, AbilityActivationService, ActivatedAbilityExecutionService, SpellCastingService, MultiPermanentChoiceHandlerService, cost handlers incl. CrewCostHandler) - no single choke point. Tapping player = explicit argument (TapUntapSupport) else currentlyResolvingControllerId / pendingEffectResolutionEntry controller, so taps outside a resolving effect (costs, attacks) never trigger it. Printed + temporary + persistent + granted; hasLostAllAbilities skip; permanent target (non-MayPayMana) -> EntersTriggerTarget, player targets get no target step. Sharae of Numbing Depths combines ONCE_PER_BATCH with OncePerTurnTriggerEffect.
- **`ON_ANY_CREATURE_BECOMES_TAPPED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `enqueueTemporaryGlobalTrigger` (each), `checkEnchantedPermanentTapTriggers` (each). Notes: SURPRISE: never registered on a card - only the key of a TemporaryGlobalTriggeredAbility from RegisterGlobalTriggeredAbilityUntilNextTurnEffect (Don't Move, a scheme spell). TCS#checkEnchantedPermanentTapTriggers calls collectTemporaryGlobalTriggers when the tapped permanent is a creature; TCS#enqueueTemporaryGlobalTrigger sets triggeringPermanentId = tapped creature; one non-targeting entry per watcher.

| Constant | Kind | Event | Object | Relation | Zone | Mult. | Self | Grouping | Cards |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ON_OPPONENT_LOSES_LIFE` | triggered | player loses life (damage or direct loss), amount > 0 |  | opponent (any player other than the source's controller) | battlefield | per event (per losing player per loss event) | n/a | each | 8 |
| `ON_ANY_PLAYER_GAINS_LIFE` | triggered | any player gains life (amount > 0) |  | each player | any | per event | n/a | each | 1 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_GAINS_LIFE` | triggered | you gain life (amount > 0) |  | you | battlefield | per event | n/a | each | 84 |
| `ON_OPPONENT_GAINS_LIFE` | triggered | an opponent gains life (amount > 0) |  | opponent (player) | battlefield | per event | n/a | each | 2 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_LOSES_LIFE` | triggered | you lose life (damage or direct loss), amount > 0 |  | you | battlefield | per event | n/a | each | 13 |
| `ON_CONTROLLER_PAYS_LIFE` | triggered | you pay life (cost payment, distinct from loss) |  | you | battlefield | per event | n/a | each | 1 |
| `GRAVEYARD_ON_OPPONENT_GAINS_LIFE` | triggered | an opponent gains life |  | opponent (player) | graveyard | per event | n/a | each | 1 |
| `GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_GAINS_LIFE` | triggered | you gain life |  | you (owner's graveyard) | graveyard | per event | n/a | each | 1 |

- **`ON_OPPONENT_LOSES_LIFE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkLifeLossTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 6 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `ConditionalEffect`, `FirstOpponentLifeLossEachTurnTriggerEffect`, `GainLifeEffect`, `MillOpponentOnLifeLossEffect`, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect`, `SequenceEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `FirstOpponentLifeLossEachTurnTriggerEffect` 1, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#checkLifeLossTriggers -> loop over effects -> TCS#dispatch (with additional-trigger copies). Skips only hasLostPrintedAbilities (no computeStaticBonus loses-all check, no granted triggered abilities). No CardEffect default handler: only OncePerTurnTriggerEffect (accepts only a wrapped CastTargetInstantOrSorceryFromGraveyardEffect, else silently returns false), FirstOpponentLifeLossEachTurnTriggerEffect, MillOpponentOnLifeLossEffect, GainLifeEffect, SequenceEffect (fires only when exactly 1 life lost: Ob Nixilis), ConditionalEffect have handlers in MiscTriggerCollectorService; any other effect class silently never triggers. Event value = life lost.
- **`ON_ANY_PLAYER_GAINS_LIFE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkLifeGainTriggers` (each). Notes: SURPRISE: never registered on a card with addEffect - used only as the key of a TemporaryGlobalTriggeredAbility created by RegisterGlobalTriggeredAbilityUntilEndOfTurnEffect (False Cure: delayed trigger from a spell). Read by TCS#collectTemporaryGlobalTriggers (called at the end of TCS#checkLifeGainTriggers); one non-targeting stack entry per watcher with targetId = gaining player and eventValue = life gained.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_GAINS_LIFE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkLifeGainTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 22 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `BecomePreparedEffect`, `BoostSelfEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `CradleOfVitalityLifeGainEffect`, `CreateTokenEffect`, `DealDamageOnSpellLifeGainEffect`, `DrawCardEffect`, `DrawCardsEqualToLifeGainedEffect`, `EnergyCountersEffect`, `GrantKeywordEffect`, `LoseLifeEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `NykthosParagonLifeGainEffect`, `PayXManaDrawXCardsEffect`, `PutCounterOnEachControlledPermanentEffect`, `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect` ×2, `PutCountersOnSourceEffect`, `SequenceEffect`, `SurveilEffect`, `UntapPermanentsEffect`; `LifeGainTriggerCollectorService`: 4 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `MayEffect`, `MayPayManaEffect`, `PerpetuallyBoostRandomCreatureCardInHandEffect`, `SequenceEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 9, `SequenceEffect` 8, `MayPayManaEffect` 7, `MayEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 10. 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TCS#checkLifeGainTriggers: printed (skipped if hasLostPrintedAbilities) + granted effects; unwraps OncePerTurnTriggerEffect (firstLifeGain / markOnAcceptance) and ConditionalEffect (unwrapLifeChangeConditional, so the ConditionalEffect handler is dead on this path) before TCS#dispatch. ~26 class-specific handlers in MiscTriggerCollectorService/LifeGainTriggerCollectorService, no CardEffect default -> unlisted classes silently never trigger. Effect-class paths: MayEffect -> queueMayAbility; MayPayManaEffect -> stack/target interaction. SURPRISE: duplicate (slot,class) handlers for MayPayManaEffect (Misc#handleLifeGainMayPayMana vs LifeGain#handleLifeGainMayPay) and SequenceEffect (Misc#handleLifeGainSequence sets eventValue vs LifeGain#handleLifeGainSequence which does not) - TriggerCollectorRegistry.register silently overwrites, last bean scanned wins. Iterates the live battlefield list (not a copy).
- **`ON_OPPONENT_GAINS_LIFE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkLifeGainTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `ConditionalEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#checkLifeGainTriggers second loop -> dispatchSlot over every non-gaining player's battlefield. Only two handlers (PutCountersOnSelfEffect, ConditionalEffect checked at trigger time with targetId = gainer); no CardEffect default, other classes silently never trigger. Event value = life gained.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_LOSES_LIFE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkLifeLossTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 16 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `BoostSelfEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `CreateTokenEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `DrawCardEffect`, `EnergyCountersEffect`, `ExileForEachLifeLostEffect`, `GainLifeEffect`, `MillOpponentOnLifeLossEffect`, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect`, `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect`, `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`, `PutCountersOnSourceEffect`, `SacrificeOtherPermanentUnlessDiscardForEachLifeLostEffect`, `SacrificePermanentsEffect`, `SequenceEffect`; `LifeGainTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `SequenceEffect`; `SpecializeShadowheartEffectHandler`: `resolve` (read). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 4, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 4. Choke points: `SpecializeShadowheartEffectHandler#resolve` ← `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#doResolveManaAbility`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#performSpecialAction`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#pushAbilityOnStack`, `AnimationSupport#fireTransformTriggers`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeCreatureOrTransformSourceEffectHandler#finish`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeNontokenCreatureTapAndSeekLandSourceEffectHandler#tapThenSeek`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#dealDamage`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#sacrificeSource`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceHandler#handle`, `AnyOpponentMayTapCreatureTapAndCreateTokenSourceEffectHandler#tapAndCreateToken`, `AnyPlayerMaySacrificeLandPutSourceOnTopEffectHandler#putSourceOnTop`, `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrBoostSelfEffectHandler#boostSelf`, +193 more. Notes: TCS#checkLifeLossTriggers second loop: hasLostPrintedAbilities only, no granted abilities; unwraps OncePerTurnTriggerEffect (firstLifeLoss vs unkeyed once flag; key/markOnAcceptance ignored) and ConditionalEffect, then registry.dispatch directly (bypasses TCS#dispatch: no additional-trigger copies). So the ConditionalEffect and OncePerTurnTriggerEffect handlers for this slot are dead. No CardEffect default handler. SURPRISE: duplicate SequenceEffect handlers (Misc#handleSequenceOnControllerLifeLoss sets eventValue; LifeGain#handleLifeGainSequence does not) - last registered wins; Marina Vendrell's Grimoire (discard EventValue) depends on the Misc one. Also written at runtime by SpecializeShadowheartEffectHandler#resolve.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_PAYS_LIFE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkLifePaymentTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `PutCountersOnSelfEffect`. Notes: TCS#checkLifePaymentTriggers: printed + granted effects, unwraps OncePerTurnTriggerEffect, TCS#dispatch. No ability-loss check on printed effects. Only handler: PutCountersOnSelfEffect (eventValue = life paid); other classes silently never trigger.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_OPPONENT_GAINS_LIFE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectGraveyardOpponentLifeGainTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#collectGraveyardOpponentLifeGainTriggers (from checkLifeGainTriggers): getEffectiveGraveyardEffects in every non-gaining player's graveyard; plain enqueueTrigger, no entry version; MayPayManaEffect stays wrapped.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_GAINS_LIFE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `collectGraveyardControllerLifeGainTriggers` (each). Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#collectGraveyardControllerLifeGainTriggers: getEffectiveGraveyardEffects; unwraps OncePerTurnTriggerEffect (firstLifeGain, once flag keyed by card id); plain enqueueTrigger.

Readers: `StackResolutionService`: `triggerSagaChapter` (all); `TriggeredAbilityQueueService`: `sagaChapterTargetRequired` (read); `StepTriggerService`: `handleSagaLoreCounters` (all); `TargetLegalityService`: `sagaChapterIgnoresCardTargetConstraint` (read, name startsWith "SAGA_CHAPTER"); `PermanentCounterSupport`: `triggerSagaChapter` (all); `SagaChapterService`: `maximumChapter` (read), `triggerSagaChapter` (all); `ExileAndReturnTransformedService`: `initializeReturnedSaga` (queue?); `Card`: `getSagaFinalChapter` (read). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 13, `MayEffect` 8, `MayCastAnySpellFromHandWithoutPayingManaCostEffect` 1. Modes: simultaneous 1. 30 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: Chapter I also triggers on ETB (initializeSaga) and via initializeReturnedSaga. addSimultaneousEffects used by The Great Work (flag consumed at resolution by StackEntry, not by the collector). All registrations of one chapter slot form ONE ability: a lone ChooseOneAtTriggerTimeEffect -> TriggeredModalTrigger, a lone ChooseOneAtRandomEffect picks its option at trigger time; targeting comes from Card#getSagaChapterTargetGroups (multi-group -> SagaChapterTarget), Card#getSagaChapterTargetFilters (PlayerPredicateTargetFilter -> player target), effect targetSpec (player+permanent -> SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget, graveyard -> SagaChapterGraveyardTarget); Card#isSagaChapterTargetRequired decides whether a no-legal-target chapter is removed (SURPRISE: otherwise it is pushed with no target, so 'target X' chapters are treated as optional unless the card opts in; 17 cards do). readers that queue: StepTriggerService#handleSagaLoreCounters (precombat main, active player's Sagas, every chapter crossed by a Vorinclex-modified placement), SagaChapterService#triggerSagaChapter (initializeSaga on ETB from BattlefieldEntryService/BatchSupport, StackResolutionService, TokenCopySupport, ExileAndReturnTransformedService, read-ahead CardChoiceHandlerService; PermanentCounterSupport#placeCounterOnPermanent and PutCounterOnEachControlledPermanentEffectHandler for effect-placed lore counters; addLoreCounterAndTriggerChapter for Bedtime Stories; triggerCopiedSagaChapter), ExileAndReturnTransformedService#initializeReturnedSaga (chapter I only). SURPRISE: StepTriggerService and SagaChapterService carry duplicated copies of this logic, StackResolutionService#triggerSagaChapter is dead code (no callers, chapters I-IV only), initializeReturnedSaga pushes chapter I with no targeting and ignores doubled counters, addLoreCounterAndTriggerChapter triggers only the final count (skips a chapter when a lore counter is doubled), PermanentCounterSupport passes entry.getControllerId() as chapter controller (an opponent's effect putting a lore counter on your Saga would control the chapter), proliferate (MultiPermanentChoiceHandlerService#proliferatePermanent) adds lore counters WITHOUT triggering any chapter, and no path checks ability loss or face-down (handleSagaLoreCounters filters on the real card's isSaga). TargetLegalityService#sagaChapterIgnoresCardTargetConstraint finds the chapter by name prefix 'SAGA_CHAPTER'; Card#getSagaFinalChapter / SagaChapterService#maximumChapter read presence.

Same collectors and grouping as SAGA_CHAPTER_I (one ability per chapter slot, target data from Card saga chapter maps keyed by this slot; same surprises).
- **`SAGA_CHAPTER_V`**. Readers: `TriggeredAbilityQueueService`: `sagaChapterTargetRequired` (read); `StepTriggerService`: `handleSagaLoreCounters` (all); `TargetLegalityService`: `sagaChapterIgnoresCardTargetConstraint` (read, name startsWith "SAGA_CHAPTER"); `PermanentCounterSupport`: `triggerSagaChapter` (all); `SagaChapterService`: `maximumChapter` (read), `triggerSagaChapter` (all); `Card`: `getSagaFinalChapter` (read). 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: SURPRISE: StackResolutionService#triggerSagaChapter (dead) has no case for chapters V/VI. Same collectors and grouping as SAGA_CHAPTER_I (one ability per chapter slot, target data from Card saga chapter maps keyed by this slot; same surprises).
- **`SAGA_CHAPTER_VI`**. Readers: `TriggeredAbilityQueueService`: `sagaChapterTargetRequired` (read); `StepTriggerService`: `handleSagaLoreCounters` (all); `TargetLegalityService`: `sagaChapterIgnoresCardTargetConstraint` (read, name startsWith "SAGA_CHAPTER"); `PermanentCounterSupport`: `triggerSagaChapter` (all); `SagaChapterService`: `maximumChapter` (read), `triggerSagaChapter` (all); `Card`: `getSagaFinalChapter` (read). Notes: SURPRISE: StackResolutionService#triggerSagaChapter (dead) has no case for chapters V/VI. Same collectors and grouping as SAGA_CHAPTER_I (one ability per chapter slot, target data from Card saga chapter maps keyed by this slot; same surprises).
- **`ON_SAGA_FINAL_CHAPTER_ABILITY_RESOLVES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSagaFinalChapterAbilityResolutionTriggers` (each); `SagaTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: StackResolutionService#checkSagaFinalChapterResolution (two resolution sites) -> TriggerCollectionService#checkSagaFinalChapterAbilityResolutionTriggers; dispatchSlot over all battlefields, default handler SagaTriggerCollectorService#handleFinalChapterResolution keeps only watchers whose controller equals the Saga's controller, event value = Saga mana value, enqueueTrigger per registration. SURPRISE: the final chapter is recognised by matching the stack entry DESCRIPTION string "<name>'s chapter <N> ability", so modal chapters (TriggeredModalTrigger entries) or differently-labelled entries are missed while copied chapter entries with the same label count.
- **`ON_SAGA_FINAL_CHAPTER_ABILITY_TRIGGERS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSagaFinalChapterAbilityTriggers` (each); `SagaTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSagaFinalChapterAbilityTriggers called from StepTriggerService#handleSagaLoreCounters and SagaChapterService#triggerSagaChapter when loreCount == getSagaFinalChapter (not for copied chapters); fires as soon as the chapter is queued, even before its target is chosen or if it is later removed for lack of targets. SURPRISE: both callers 'continue/return' before this check for a modal ChooseOneAtTriggerTimeEffect final chapter, and initializeReturnedSaga never calls it. Default handler SagaTriggerCollectorService#handleFinalChapterResolution (controller filter, event value = mana value), one entry per registration (Historian's Boon).
