- **`PLANESWALK_TO_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `PlanechaseService`: `completeSpatialMerging` (read), `completePlaneswalkToPlaneWithoutDeparting` (read), `reveal` (read). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 3, `SequenceEffect` 2. Notes: Fired from PlanechaseService#reveal(triggerAbilities=true) (via finishPlaneswalk), #completeSpatialMerging and #completePlaneswalkToPlaneWithoutDeparting; a revealed phenomenon uses ENCOUNTER_TRIGGERED instead; PlanechaseService#start uses reveal(false) so the starting plane does not trigger. PlanechaseService#trigger: intervening-if ConditionalEffects filtered at trigger time; each untargeted effect becomes its own enqueued StackEntry, each single-target effect its own SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget/SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger interaction; if the card has multi/optional target groups and an effect of this slot is bound to one, the whole slot goes into one ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger. Controller = planar controller.
- **`PLANESWALK_FROM_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `PlanechaseService`: `finishPlaneswalk` (read), `completeSpatialMerging` (read). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: PlanechaseService#finishPlaneswalk and #completeSpatialMerging call trigger() after adding the departing card to the bottom of the planar deck. Not fired by completePlaneswalkToPlaneWithoutDeparting (nothing departs). Same grouping as PLANESWALK_TO: PlanechaseService#trigger: intervening-if ConditionalEffects filtered at trigger time; each untargeted effect becomes its own enqueued StackEntry, each single-target effect its own SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget/SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger interaction; if the card has multi/optional target groups and an effect of this slot is bound to one, the whole slot goes into one ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger. Controller = planar controller.
- **`CHAOS_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `PlanechaseService`: `chaos` (read); `RevealTopPlanarCardsAndTriggerChaosEffectHandler`: `resolve` (read); `RevealPlanarCardsUntilPlaneAndTriggerChaosEffectHandler`: `resolve` (read). Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 25, `MayEffect` 13, `ConditionalEffect` 4. Trigger effects: `SpellCastTriggerEffect` 1. 5 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Choke points: `RevealTopPlanarCardsAndTriggerChaosEffectHandler#resolve` ← `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#doResolveManaAbility`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#performSpecialAction`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#pushAbilityOnStack`, `AnimationSupport#fireTransformTriggers`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeCreatureOrTransformSourceEffectHandler#finish`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeNontokenCreatureTapAndSeekLandSourceEffectHandler#tapThenSeek`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#dealDamage`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#sacrificeSource`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceHandler#handle`, `AnyOpponentMayTapCreatureTapAndCreateTokenSourceEffectHandler#tapAndCreateToken`, `AnyPlayerMaySacrificeLandPutSourceOnTopEffectHandler#putSourceOnTop`, `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrBoostSelfEffectHandler#boostSelf`, +193 more; `RevealPlanarCardsUntilPlaneAndTriggerChaosEffectHandler#resolve` ← `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#doResolveManaAbility`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#performSpecialAction`, `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService#pushAbilityOnStack`, `AnimationSupport#fireTransformTriggers`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeCreatureOrTransformSourceEffectHandler#finish`, `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeNontokenCreatureTapAndSeekLandSourceEffectHandler#tapThenSeek`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#dealDamage`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffectHandler#sacrificeSource`, `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceHandler#handle`, `AnyOpponentMayTapCreatureTapAndCreateTokenSourceEffectHandler#tapAndCreateToken`, `AnyPlayerMaySacrificeLandPutSourceOnTopEffectHandler#putSourceOnTop`, `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrBoostSelfEffectHandler#boostSelf`, +193 more. Notes: PlanechaseService#chaos (callers PlanechaseService#completeRoll, ChaosEnsuesEffectHandler, EachOpponentFacesMissyVillainousChoiceEffectHandler) loops every face-up planar object; RevealTopPlanarCardsAndTriggerChaosEffectHandler / RevealPlanarCardsUntilPlaneAndTriggerChaosEffectHandler call trigger() on revealed deck cards. SURPRISE: trigger() makes one stack object per untargeted effect, so a multi-effect chaos ability is split into several triggered abilities (Kessig boost/trample/Werewolf = 3 objects, Windriddle Palaces mill split into you / each opponent, Bad Wolf Bay planeswalk + shuffle) - players can order and respond between the parts. PlanechaseService#trigger: intervening-if ConditionalEffects filtered at trigger time; each untargeted effect becomes its own enqueued StackEntry, each single-target effect its own SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget/SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger interaction; if the card has multi/optional target groups and an effect of this slot is bound to one, the whole slot goes into one ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger. Controller = planar controller.
- **`ENCOUNTER_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `PlanechaseService`: `reveal` (read). Notes: Only PlanechaseService#reveal(true) when the revealed card is a PHENOMENON (reached from finishPlaneswalk). Starting reveal skips phenomena; PlanechaseService#checkPhenomena planeswalks away once the encounter ability has left the stack. PlanechaseService#trigger: intervening-if ConditionalEffects filtered at trigger time; each untargeted effect becomes its own enqueued StackEntry, each single-target effect its own SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget/SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger interaction; if the card has multi/optional target groups and an effect of this slot is bound to one, the whole slot goes into one ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger. Controller = planar controller.
- **`ON_SELF_BECOMES_CONTROLLED`**. Readers: `CreatureControlService`: `queueSelfControlChangeTriggers` (each). Notes: CreatureControlService#queueSelfControlChangeTriggers at the end of moveControl (choke point CreatureControlService#recomputeControls/recomputeControl). Reads printed + temporary + persistent triggered effects; one non-targeting enqueued entry per effect. No ability-loss check; granted triggered abilities not read.
- **`ON_SELF_LOSES_CONTROL`**. Readers: `CreatureControlService`: `queueSelfControlLossTriggers` (each). Notes: CreatureControlService#queueSelfControlLossTriggers at the start of moveControl, before the battlefield move (look-back); choke point CreatureControlService#recomputeControls. Printed + temporary + persistent effects, one entry each. No ability-loss check; granted not read; leaving the battlefield does not fire it.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_SETS_SCHEME_IN_MOTION`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSchemeSetInMotionTriggers` (each); `SchemeTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `SchemeSetInMotionTriggerEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `SchemeSetInMotionTriggerEffect` 2. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSchemeSetInMotionTriggers dispatches the setting player's battlefield + dispatchFaceUpSchemes; only handler SchemeTriggerCollectorService#handleSchemeSetInMotion (SchemeSetInMotionTriggerEffect with optional schemeFilter; its resolvedEffects bundled into one non-targeting entry); other effect classes ignored. SURPRISE: the only main-code caller is AbandonSchemeAndSetTriggeringSchemeInMotionAgainEffectHandler (My Laughter Echoes replay) - no archenemy turn path reaches it; card tests call it directly. Plots That Span Centuries uses this slot unfiltered as its own 'when you set this scheme in motion'. With the 3-schemes replacement the same schemeEntry is dispatched 3 times.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_GIVES_GIFT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerGivesGiftTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerGivesGiftTriggers (dispatchSlot over the giver's permanents) called only from TargetOpponentCreatesTokenEffectHandler, EachOtherPlayerDrawsCardEffectHandler and ConjureCardToOpponentBattlefieldEffectHandler - gift detection lives in effect handlers, no central choke point. Default handler MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleControllerGivesGift: one enqueued entry per registration, no target step.
- **`ON_ALLY_CASE_SOLVES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyCaseSolvesTriggers` (each). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCaseSolvesTriggers from SolveSourceEffectHandler. Bypasses dispatch: printed effects only, TriggeringCardConditionalEffect filtered against the Case card, one enqueued entry per registration with triggeringPermanentId = Case. No ability-loss check and no granted, once-per-turn or target handling.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_FORETELLS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerForetellTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `BoostSelfEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerForetellTriggers called only from the GameService foretell special action. Only handler MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleForetellBoostSelf (BoostSelfEffect); no default handler, so any other effect class registered here never triggers.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_FORAGES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkForageTriggers` (each); `ForageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkForageTriggers; callers SpellCastingService (forage costs), AbilityActivationService (forage costs), ForageTriggerEffectHandler. Default handler ForageTriggerCollectorService#handleForage: one enqueued entry per registration, no target step.
- **`ON_PLAYERS_FINISH_VOTING`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkVotingFinishedTriggers` (each); `VotingFinishedTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `ErestorOfTheCouncilEffect`, `ModelOfUnityEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkVotingFinishedTriggers (forEachPermanent over all battlefields) from VotingFinishedSupport. Only ErestorOfTheCouncilEffect / ModelOfUnityEffect handlers (VotingFinishedTriggerCollectorService), each snapshotting the VotingResult into a new effect; no default handler.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_TEMPTS_RING`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkRingTemptTriggers` (each); `RingTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkRingTemptTriggers from TemptTheRingEffectHandler; returns when no Ring-bearer was chosen. Default RingTriggerCollectorService#handleRingTempted skips the source when it is the chosen Ring-bearer - Gandalf, Friend of the Shire's intervening-if ('a creature other than Gandalf') is hard-coded for every effect in the slot. Distinct from ON_RING_TEMPTS_YOU.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_INVESTIGATES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkInvestigateTriggers` (each); `InvestigateTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CreateTokenEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkInvestigateTriggers, gated by playersWhoInvestigatedThisTurn.add; called once per investigate action from CreateTokenEffectHandler and other token/investigate handlers. Only CreateTokenEffect handler (InvestigateTriggerCollectorService#handleClueCreation); no default handler.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_INVESTIGATES_EACH_TIME`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkInvestigateTriggers` (each); `InvestigateTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: Same TriggerCollectionService#checkInvestigateTriggers; default handler InvestigateTriggerCollectorService#handleEveryInvestigation enqueues one entry per registration. Javadoc says 'one or more times' but the code fires once per investigate action.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_MANIFESTS_DREAD`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkManifestDreadTriggers` (each); `ManifestDreadTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `ReturnTriggeringCardToOwnerHandEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkManifestDreadTriggers from ManifestDreadEffectHandler and LibraryChoiceHandlerService. Only ReturnTriggeringCardToOwnerHandEffect handler (bakes the graveyard card id + graveyard entry version); no default handler.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_COMPLETES_DUNGEON`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDungeonCompletionTriggers` (each); `DungeonTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkDungeonCompletionTriggers from VentureIntoDungeonEffectHandler and TriggerCollectionService#completeDungeonRoomIfReady (also reads GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_COMPLETES_DUNGEON). Default DungeonTriggerCollectorService handler: graveyard-only target -> SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger interaction, otherwise plain enqueue (permanent/player targets get no target step).
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_ROLLS_ONE_OR_MORE_DICE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerRollsPlanarDieTriggers` (each), `checkControllerRollsOneOrMoreDiceTriggers` (each); `DiceRollTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 4, `ConditionalEffect` 2, `MayEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerRollsOneOrMoreDiceTriggers (~15 roll effect handlers + CoinFlipService Pokey path) and #checkControllerRollsPlanarDieTriggers (PlanechaseService#completeRoll, also dispatches face-up planar cards). Default DiceRollTriggerCollectorService#handleControllerRollsDice: intervening-if checked with the roll result, permanent/player-targeted effects -> SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget, else enqueue with eventValue = result.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_ROLLS_DIE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerRollsHighestNaturalResultTriggers` (each); `DiceRollTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: Dispatched once per die inside TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerRollsHighestNaturalResultTriggers (called by every die-roll effect handler); same DiceRollTriggerCollectorService handler, eventValue = result. Planar die rolls and Pokey coin-flip d20 rolls never call it, so per-die triggers miss those rolls while the one-or-more slot fires.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_ROLLS_HIGHEST_NATURAL_RESULT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerRollsHighestNaturalResultTriggers` (each); `DiceRollTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkControllerRollsHighestNaturalResultTriggers (called by every die-roll effect handler, interleaved per die after ON_CONTROLLER_ROLLS_DIE); default handler delegates to DiceRollTriggerCollectorService#handleControllerRollsDice. Pokey coin-flip d20s don't reach it.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_BENDS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkBendingTriggers` (each); `BendingTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkBendingTriggers (also records the bending type) from Airbend*/EarthbendTargetLand/AwardManaUntilEndOfCombat effect handlers, WaterbendPaymentService, SpellCastingService and AbilityActivationService. Default BendingTriggerCollectorService handler: one non-targeting entry per registration, no target step.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_EXERTS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkExertTriggers` (each); `ExertTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkExertTriggers (records permanentsExertedThisTurn) from MayAbilityHandlerService (exert on attack) and ActivatedAbilityExecutionService. Default ExertTriggerCollectorService#handleExert: permanent/player-targeted -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget interaction, else non-targeting entry with targetId = exerted creature.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_COLLECTS_EVIDENCE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCollectEvidenceTriggers` (each); `CollectEvidenceTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CreateTokenEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkCollectEvidenceTriggers from SpellCastingService, GraveyardChoiceHandlerService, CollectEvidenceEffectHandler and AbilityActivationService. Only CreateTokenEffect handler (CollectEvidenceTriggerCollectorService); no default handler.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_DISCOVERS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDiscoverTriggers` (each); `DiscoverTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkDiscoverTriggers from DiscoverEffectHandler, LibraryChoiceHandlerService and PermanentChoiceSpellHandlerService. Default DiscoverTriggerCollectorService handler: non-targeting entry, eventValue = discover N; OncePerTurnTriggerEffect unwrapped by dispatchSlotEffect.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_SEEKS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSeekTriggers` (each); `SeekTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkSeekTriggers from ~20 Seek*/seek-and-conjure effect handlers. Default SeekTriggerCollectorService#handleSeek: non-targeting entry; ConjureDuplicatesOfSoughtCardsAndManifestEffect gets the sought cards baked in and a hard-coded 'only during your turn' (active player) gate plus non-empty sought list.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_CONJURES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkConjureTriggers` (each); `ConjureTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkConjureTriggers from ~70 conjure/spellbook handlers (no central choke point); also reads GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_CONJURES. Default ConjureTriggerCollectorService handler: one non-targeting entry per registration.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_GAINS_CONTROL_OF_YOUR_PERMANENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkOpponentGainsControlTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CreateTokenEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkOpponentGainsControlTriggers from CreatureControlService#recomputeControls before any battlefield move (previous controller's battlefield; simultaneous steals counted per permanent). New controller must equal getOpponentId(previous). Only CreateTokenEffect handler (MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleOpponentGainsControlCreateToken); no default.
- **`ON_AURA_ATTACHED_TO_SELF`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAuraAttachedTriggers` (each). Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAuraAttachedTriggers(Card...) (also via the Permanent overload); ~30 scattered callers (BattlefieldPlacementService, PermanentChoiceBattlefieldHandlerService, Attach*/CreateTokenAttached* effect handlers) - no choke point. Printed effects only, no ability-loss check, Aura test by printed subtype; one enqueued entry per registration controlled by the enchanted permanent's controller.
- **`ON_ALLY_AURA_ATTACHED_TO_ALLY_CREATURE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAuraAttachedTriggers` (each). Notes: Only in the Permanent overload of TriggerCollectionService#checkAuraAttachedTriggers (the Card-only overload, e.g. BecomeAuraManifestTopCardEffectHandler, skips it). Watchers on the Aura controller's battlefield, hasLostAllAbilities skip, printed only; one non-targeting entry per registration.
- **`ON_EQUIPMENT_ATTACHED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkEquipmentAttachedTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkEquipmentAttachedTriggers via EquipSupport#notifyEquipmentAttached (24 callers) and LibraryChoiceHandlerService. mixed: ChooseColorForSourceEffect, ChooseCardNameAtResolution+ChooseSubtypeForSource SequenceEffect and AttachedCreatureBecomesCopyOfExiledCreatureEffect(asAttaches) are 'as this becomes attached' replacement-style choices done inline; targeted effects -> ETBTokenTargetTrigger interaction; others enqueued with triggeringPermanentId = equipped creature. Once-per-turn unwrapped; printed only, no ability-loss check. SURPRISE: the Equipment branch of AttachTargetAuraOrEquipmentToTargetCreatureEffectHandler sets attachedTo without notifying, so the slot does not fire there.
- **`ON_CREWS_VEHICLE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCrewsVehicleTriggers` (each); `CombatAttackService`: `processDelayedVehicleAttackTriggers` (queue?). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `RegisterDelayedEndStepTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkCrewsVehicleTriggers from CrewCostHandler (also run for saddle, filtered out by the printed VEHICLE subtype). Entries go to pendingActivatedAbilityCostTriggers, printed only. mixed: RegisterDelayedVehicleAttackEffect (Balthier and Fran) is not queued - it records a DelayedVehicleAttack marker, and the real 'whenever a Vehicle crewed by this attacks' trigger is fired by CombatAttackService#processDelayedVehicleAttackTriggers (which re-reads this slot for presence and ability loss); the marker is recorded before the lost-abilities check.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_CREWS_VEHICLE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyCreatureCrewsVehicleTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `PerpetuallyBoostVehicleWhenMatchingCreatureCrewsEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreatureCrewsVehicleTriggers (dispatchSlot over the crewing creature's controller's battlefield) from CrewCostHandler. Only PerpetuallyBoostVehicleWhenMatchingCreatureCrewsEffect handler (MiscTriggerCollectorService; filters the crewing creature, queues to pendingActivatedAbilityCostTriggers); no default.
- **`ON_DAY_NIGHT_CHANGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDayNightChangeTriggers` (each); `DayNightTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 2, `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#checkDayNightChangeTriggers -> dispatchSlot per permanent in APNAP order -> DayNightTriggerCollectorService#handleDayNightChange (CardEffect default). Permanent-targeting effects go via queueInteraction(DayNightTriggerTarget), else enqueueTrigger; MayEffect stays wrapped on the stack (no queueMayAbility). Player-targeting effects get no target interaction. Caller DayNightService (line ~102). Graveyard variant collected inline in the same loop.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_DAY_NIGHT_CHANGE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkGraveyardDayNightChangeTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#checkGraveyardDayNightChangeTriggers (private, called from checkDayNightChangeTriggers per player after that player's battlefield). Uses getEffectiveGraveyardEffects; one StackEntry per registration via enqueueTrigger with graveyardEntryVersion; no registry, no target handling, MayPayManaEffect resolved on the stack.
- **`ON_ALLY_ROOM_FULLY_UNLOCKED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyRoomFullyUnlockedTriggers` (each); `EnterTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: SURPRISE: EnterTriggerCollectorService#handleRoomFullyUnlockedDefault puts non-targeting entries straight onto gameData.stack.add (bypasses enqueueTrigger/trigger ordering); targeting ones queue EntersTriggerTarget. Check method TCS#checkAllyRoomFullyUnlockedTriggers -> dispatchSlot. Callers: GameService unlock-door special action (~line 638), UnlockControlledRoomDoorEffectHandler, LockOrUnlockTargetRoomDoorEffectHandler (only on locked->fully-unlocked transition).
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_ALLY_ROOM_FULLY_UNLOCKED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyRoomFullyUnlockedTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Notes: Read inside TCS#checkAllyRoomFullyUnlockedTriggers via TCS#queueGraveyardEerieTriggers: uses printed card.getEffects (not getEffectiveGraveyardEffects); MayEffect -> queueMayAbility, anything else -> direct gameData.stack.add (bypasses enqueueTrigger). Only the Room controller's graveyard.
- **`ON_SELF_ROOM_DOOR_UNLOCKED`**. Readers: `StackResolutionService`: `resolveEnchantmentSpell` (read); `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSelfRoomDoorUnlockedTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: `handleSelfRoomDoorUnlocked` (all), 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `TriggeringRoomDoorConditionalEffect`. Wrappers: `TriggeringRoomDoorConditionalEffect` 30, `SequenceEffect` 10. 4 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Choke points: `StackResolutionService#resolveEnchantmentSpell` ← `StackResolutionService#resolveTopOfStack`. Notes: All 30 registrations are TriggeringRoomDoorConditionalEffect(doorIndex). MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleSelfRoomDoorUnlocked re-reads the slot: the first registration for the unlocked door queues ALL registrations for that door in one stack object (later ones return false); other doors' registrations are ignored. Targeting -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget or ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger, else enqueueTrigger. Identity check matchingEffects.getFirst()!=effect compares printed effects with the TextChangeTransformer output, so a text-change rewrite that yields a new effect object would suppress the trigger. Readers: TCS#checkSelfRoomDoorUnlockedTriggers (from StackResolutionService#resolveEnchantmentSpell, GameService unlock action, Unlock/LockOrUnlock effect handlers); StackResolutionService#resolveEnchantmentSpell also reads the slot to append spell-target metadata (appendSpellTargetingForEffectsFrom) for target indices.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_BECOMES_MONARCH`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkBecomesMonarchTriggers` (each). Notes: TCS#checkBecomesMonarchTriggers (shared with ON_OPPONENT_BECOMES_MONARCH, picks slot per permanent). SURPRISE: no ability-loss check at all (no hasLostPrintedAbilities/computeStaticBonus) and no registry/TCS#dispatch (no additional-trigger copies, no granted abilities). Intervening-if ConditionalEffect checked at trigger time but the wrapper stays on the stack; targeting -> SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget, else enqueueTrigger non-targeting; MayEffect stays wrapped.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_BECOMES_MONARCH`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkBecomesMonarchTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: Same reader as ON_CONTROLLER_BECOMES_MONARCH (TCS#checkBecomesMonarchTriggers). Non-targeting entry gets targetId = new monarch; targeting effects implementing TargetControllerIsTriggeringPlayerEffect restrict targets to the monarch's permanents (Garland). Same SURPRISE: no ability-loss check, bypasses registry.
- **`ON_RING_TEMPTS_YOU`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkRingTemptsYouTriggers` (each); `RingTemptsYouTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#checkRingTemptsYouTriggers -> dispatchSlot over the tempted player's battlefield -> RingTemptsYouTriggerCollectorService#handleRingTemptsYou (CardEffect default): always non-targeting enqueueTrigger; MayEffect stays wrapped on the stack.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_WINS_COIN_FLIP`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerWinsCoinFlipTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TCS#checkControllerWinsCoinFlipTriggers: hasActiveTriggeredAbilities check, then registry.dispatch directly (bypasses TCS#dispatch: no additional-trigger copies, no granted abilities) -> MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleCoinFlipWonDefault -> enqueueCoinFlipTrigger (any-target -> SpellTargetTriggerAnyTarget). Called from ~20 scattered coin-flip sites.
- **`ON_ANY_PLAYER_WINS_COIN_FLIP`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerWinsCoinFlipTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: Read in TCS#checkControllerWinsCoinFlipTriggers via forEachPermanent; hasActiveTriggeredAbilities check; registry.dispatch directly (no additional-trigger copies, no granted abilities) -> handleAnyPlayerCoinFlipWonDefault.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_LOSES_COIN_FLIP`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerLosesCoinFlipTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: SURPRISE: TCS#checkControllerLosesCoinFlipTriggers has no ability-loss check (the win path uses hasActiveTriggeredAbilities). registry.dispatch directly -> handleCoinFlipLostDefault, target chosen by an opponent (Karplusan Minotaur). ~16 scattered call sites.
- **`STATE_TRIGGERED`**. Readers: `StateTriggerService`: `checkPlanarStateTriggers` (queue?), `stateTriggeredEffects` (read); `Card`: `validateEffectSlotType` (read). Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 2. Trigger effects: `StateTriggerEffect` 62. Notes: StateTriggerService#checkStateTriggers: key (permanentId, registration index) in stateTriggerOnStack prevents retrigger; skips face-down and hasLostPrintedAbilities; adds granted StateTriggerEffects (getGrantedEffects). Targeting -> ETBTokenMultiTargetTrigger, else direct gameData.stack.add (bypasses enqueueTrigger/ordering). Card#validateEffectSlotType forces StateTriggerEffect in this slot. Also read by StateTriggerService#checkPlanarStateTriggers for face-up planes (command zone).
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_FIGHTS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyCreatureFightsTriggers` (each); `DamageTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect`. Notes: TriggerCollectionService#checkAllyCreatureFightsTriggers, called for both fighters from FightSupport and SourceFightsTargetCreatureEffectHandler; dispatchSlot over the fighter's controller's other permanents, then the fighter itself (still triggers if it died). Only handler: PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect -> enqueueTrigger RegisterDelayedCountersOnTriggeringPermanentEffect; with no default handler other effect classes never trigger. Fight is a keyword action (draft had 13.4).
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_COMPLETES_DUNGEON`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkDungeonCompletionTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#checkDungeonCompletionTriggers (after the battlefield ON_CONTROLLER_COMPLETES_DUNGEON pass): printed card.getEffects (not getEffectiveGraveyardEffects), enqueueTrigger with graveyardEntryVersion; MayEffect stays wrapped on the stack.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_CONJURES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkConjureTriggers` (each). Notes: TCS#checkConjureTriggers (after battlefield ON_CONTROLLER_CONJURES pass): getEffectiveGraveyardEffects, plain enqueueTrigger, no entry version. checkConjureTriggers has ~73 call sites. Slot does not exclude the source card itself being conjured (it is in the graveyard).
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_ROLLS_NATURAL_20`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkControllerRollsNaturalTwentyTriggers` (each). Notes: TCS#checkControllerRollsNaturalTwentyTriggers: printed card.getEffects, enqueueTrigger, no graveyardEntryVersion (unlike dungeon/day-night graveyard readers).
- **`ON_OPPONENT_PAYS_TAX`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkTaxPaymentTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CreateTokenEffect`. Notes: TCS#checkTaxPaymentTriggers (7 call sites): printed + granted effects, no ability-loss check, unwrapOncePerTurnTrigger, TCS#dispatch. Only handler: CreateTokenEffect (eventValue = mana paid, Tax Taker); other classes silently never trigger.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_EXPLORES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkExploreTriggers` (all). Wrappers: `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 2, `MayEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. 2 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TCS#checkExploreTriggers: all registrations of one permanent (after TriggeringCardConditionalEffect filtering against the revealed card, null when nothing revealed) go into ONE stack object; any permanent-targeting effect -> ExploreTriggerTarget for the whole group, else direct gameData.stack.add (bypasses enqueueTrigger). Only isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn checked (no static loss / hasLostPrintedAbilities), no registry, no granted abilities, iterates live battlefield. Callers: ExploreEffectHandler, MayMiscHandlerService.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_CONNIVES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyCreatureConniveTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#checkAllyCreatureConniveTriggers (uses LKI controller) -> dispatchSlot -> MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleAllyCreatureConnivesDefault: always non-targeting, targetId = conniving creature, enqueueTrigger; MayPayManaEffect stays wrapped.
- **`ON_EXPLOIT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkExploitTriggers` (all). Wrappers: `MayEffect` 1. 4 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TCS#checkExploitTriggers: all registrations in ONE stack object, routed by the first matching targeting kind (graveyard-only / stack / permanent / player interaction, else direct gameData.stack.add, bypassing enqueueTrigger); uses source card LKI so it works after the exploiter sacrificed itself. No ability-loss check, no registry. Caller: PermanentChoiceBattlefieldHandlerService (~line 2786, exploit sacrifice completion).
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_EXPLOITS`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyCreatureExploitTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `ConditionalEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1, `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#checkAllyCreatureExploitTriggers -> dispatchSlot per permanent; if the exploiter left the battlefield its LKI is dispatched via TCS#dispatch. MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleAllyCreatureExploit (CardEffect default): TriggeringCardConditionalEffect tested against the exploited card; targeted -> EntersTriggerTarget, else enqueueTrigger with eventValue = exploited power.
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_EXPLOITS_NONTOKEN_CREATURE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAllyCreatureExploitTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: Same reader and handler as ON_ALLY_CREATURE_EXPLOITS, gated on !exploitedCard.isToken().
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_CLASHES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `fireClashTriggers` (all). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 2, `MayCastAnySpellFromHandWithoutPayingManaCostEffect` 1, `MayEffect` 1, `SequenceEffect` 1. 2 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TCS#fireClashTriggers (called for both clashing players): won/lost ClashOutcomeConditionalEffect (IfWonClashEffect/IfLostClashEffect) resolved at trigger time; all remaining registrations in ONE stack object; any targeting -> ClashTriggerTarget for the group, else direct gameData.stack.add (bypasses enqueueTrigger). Only isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn checked; no registry.
- **`ON_PLAYER_LOSES_GAME`**. Readers: `GameOutcomeService`: `firePlayerLosesGameTriggers` (all). 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: GameOutcomeService#firePlayerLosesGameTriggers: all registrations of a permanent in ONE stack entry (targetId = loser, non-targeting), direct gameData.stack.add; skipped in simulations; no ability-loss check. Only intervening-if: WinGameIfPlayerAttackedByControlledSubtypeThisTurnEffect. SURPRISE: Curse of Vengeance ('enchanted player loses') and Shinryu ('chosen player loses') are not restricted to that player; moot since the 2-player game ends first.
- **`ON_CHAMPIONED`**. Readers: `PermanentChoiceBattlefieldHandlerService`: `handleChampionCreature` (read). Notes: SURPRISE: PermanentChoiceBattlefieldHandlerService#handleChampionCreature -> beginChampionedTrigger always treats the group as player-targeting (ChampionedTriggerTarget via setPermanentChoiceContext, not the queued-interaction system), runs SBAs inline, and silently drops the trigger when no valid targets; no non-targeting path, no ability-loss check. One interaction for all registrations.
- **`ON_SELF_SADDLES_OR_CREWS_DURING_MAIN_PHASE`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkSelfSaddlesOrCrewsDuringMainPhaseTriggers` (each). Notes: TCS#checkSelfSaddlesOrCrewsDuringMainPhaseTriggers from CrewCostHandler (per tapped creature): printed + granted, only isLosesAllAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurn checked; entries go to pendingActivatedAbilityCostTriggers (stacked after the activated ability), additional-trigger copies added manually, non-targeting with triggeringPermanentId = Mount/Vehicle.
- **`ON_SELF_BECOMES_STATIONED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkStationedTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Notes: TCS#checkStationedTriggers from TapCreatureCostHandler (station cost): registry.dispatch directly (no ability-loss check, no copies, no granted) -> MiscTriggerCollectorService#handleBecomesStationed: non-targeting, targetId = stationing creature card, added to pendingActivatedAbilityCostTriggers.
- **`ON_CONTROLLER_COMMITS_CRIME`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCrimeTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 3, `MayEffect` 2, `ConditionalEffect` 1, `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 13. Notes: TCS#checkCrimeTriggers (gated by GameData#isCommittedCrime) -> dispatchSlot. Called from ~17 sites (SpellCastingService, AbilityActivationService x7, ActivatedAbilityExecutionService, TriggeredAbilityQueueService, ETBTokenTargetService, PermanentChoiceTriggerHandlerService, StackedDividedDamageTriggerService). dispatchSlot already unwraps OncePerTurnTriggerEffect, so Misc#handleCrimeOncePerTurn is dead on this path; Misc#handleCrimeConditional checks intervening-if then leaves the wrapper; default: graveyard target -> SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger, other target -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget, else enqueueTrigger. MayEffect/MayPayManaEffect stay wrapped.
- **`ON_OPPONENT_COMMITS_CRIME`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCrimeTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 3 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `ConditionalEffect`, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect`. Notes: Same reader/handlers as ON_CONTROLLER_COMMITS_CRIME, over every non-committing player's battlefield.
- **`GRAVEYARD_ON_CONTROLLER_COMMITS_CRIME`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkCrimeTriggers` (each). Wrappers: `MayPayManaEffect` 1. Notes: Read at the end of TCS#checkCrimeTriggers: getEffectiveGraveyardEffects -> TCS#queueGraveyardCrimeTrigger (enqueueTrigger with graveyardEntryVersion); MayPayManaEffect stays wrapped.
- **`ON_SELF_BECOMES_PLOTTED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkPlotTriggers` (all). 1 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: Zone is exile (the plotted card), not battlefield. TCS#checkPlotTriggers: all registrations in ONE stack object (Aloe Alchemist's two registrations = one ability); any permanent/player target -> PlotTriggerAnyTarget, else enqueueTrigger. Callers: SpellCastingService (plot special action), StackResolutionService, CardChoiceHandlerService, ExileTargetSpellAndPlotEffectHandler.
- **`ON_ALLY_AURA_ATTACHED_TO_OPPONENT_NONLAND_PERMANENT`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkAuraAttachedTriggers` (each). Notes: TCS#checkAuraAttachedTriggers(Permanent,UUID) (~27 call sites): skips hasLostAllAbilities; compares printed mana values (enchanted.getCard().getManaValue()); no registry. SURPRISE: the stack entry's source permanent id is the Aura's id (not the watcher's) and targetId = attached permanent, non-targeting, so the effect (GainControlOfAuraAttachedPermanentEffect) can track the Aura.
- **`ON_EQUIPMENT_ATTACHED_TO_CREATURE`**. Readers: none. Notes: SURPRISE: dead constant - no card registers it and no engine code reads it; TCS#checkEquipmentAttachedTriggers reads ON_EQUIPMENT_ATTACHED instead. agent-docs TRIGGER_SLOT_TARGETING.md and CARD_PATTERNS_PERMANENTS_ARTIFACTS.md (Assimilation Aegis) still document it as live.
- **`ON_SELF_BECOMES_CREWED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkBecomesCrewedTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect`. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: SURPRISE: Javadoc says 'for the first time each turn' but the code fires on every crew; only Mindlink Mech wraps OncePerTurnTriggerEffect (Ghost Ark, Mobilizer Mech, Protean War Engine are every-time per Oracle, so code is right and the Javadoc is wrong). TCS#checkBecomesCrewedTriggers from AnimationSupport (crew animation): registry.dispatch directly (no ability-loss check, no copies, no granted) -> Misc#handleBecomesCrewedOncePerTurn / #handleBecomesCrewedDefault (targeted -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget, else enqueueTrigger).
- **`ON_SELF_BECOMES_SADDLED`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkBecomesSaddledTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 2 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`, `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Trigger effects: `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#checkBecomesSaddledTriggers from BecomeSaddledUntilEndOfTurnEffectHandler: registry.dispatch directly (no ability-loss check, no copies, no granted) -> Misc#handleBecomesSaddledOncePerTurn / #handleBecomesSaddledDefault; neither handler supports targets (always enqueueTrigger).
- **`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_MUTATES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkMutateTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `SequenceEffect` 1. Notes: TCS#checkMutateTriggers (from MutationSupport): first every card in the merged permanent, then every other permanent of the controller; registry.dispatch directly (no ability-loss check, no copies, no granted) -> Misc#handleAllyCreatureMutatesDefault: non-targeting, targetId = mutated permanent, enqueueTrigger.
- **`ON_SELF_MUTATES`**. Readers: `TriggerCollectionService`: `checkMutateTriggers` (each); `MiscTriggerCollectorService`: `handleSelfMutatesDefault` (mixed), 1 `@CollectsTrigger` handler(s) for `CardEffect`. Wrappers: `MayEffect` 2, `SequenceEffect` 1. 2 card(s) register 2+ effects in it. Notes: TCS#checkMutateTriggers dispatches each card of the merged permanent (sourceCard = that card) via registry.dispatch directly (no ability-loss check, no copies). Misc#handleSelfMutatesDefault re-reads the slot: registrations sharing a target-group index (getEffectTargetIndex) go into one stack object queued by the first of the group; untargeted registrations are one stack object each. Targeted -> SelfTriggeredAbilityTarget, else enqueueTrigger; MayEffect stays wrapped.
